function notifyFavoriteMatchEvent(id,kind,match){
  // Push/toast notifications are strictly for goals in starred matches.
  if(kind!=='goal'||!state.favs.has(String(id)))return;
  const title='GOL!';
  const home=match&&match.teams&&match.teams.home&&match.teams.home.name||'Ev sahibi';
  const away=match&&match.teams&&match.teams.away&&match.teams.away.name||'Deplasman';
  const g=match&&match.goals||{},score=g.home!=null&&g.away!=null?` · ${g.home}-${g.away}`:'';
  const body=home+' - '+away+score;
  const eventKey=String(id)+'|'+kind+'|'+String(g.home??'')+'-'+String(g.away??'')+'|'+String(match&&match.fixture&&match.fixture.status&&match.fixture.status.short||'');
  if(favoriteNotificationKeys.has(eventKey))return;
  favoriteNotificationKeys.add(eventKey);if(favoriteNotificationKeys.size>600)favoriteNotificationKeys.delete(favoriteNotificationKeys.values().next().value);
  showFavoriteToast(title,body);
  try{
    if('Notification'in window&&Notification.permission==='granted'){
      const options={body,tag:'skorhane-'+String(id)+'-'+kind,renotify:false,icon:'/favicon.ico'};
      if(navigator.serviceWorker&&navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration){navigator.serviceWorker.getRegistration().then(reg=>{if(reg&&reg.showNotification)return reg.showNotification('SkorHane · '+title,options);try{new Notification('SkorHane · '+title,options)}catch(_){}}).catch(()=>{try{new Notification('SkorHane · '+title,options)}catch(_){}})}
      else new Notification('SkorHane · '+title,options);
    }
  }catch(_){}
}
function getSoundContext(){
  const AC=window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext;if(!AC)return null;
  try{if(!soundContext)soundContext=new AC();if(soundContext.state==='suspended')soundContext.resume().catch(()=>{});return soundContext}catch(_){return null}
}
function scheduleTone(ctx,frequency,start,duration,volume=0.045,type='sine',endFrequency=null){
  try{
    const osc=ctx.createOscillator(),gain=ctx.createGain();osc.type=type;
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency,start);
    if(endFrequency&&endFrequency>0)osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(endFrequency,start+duration);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,start);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(volume,start+Math.min(0.018,duration/3));gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,start+duration);
    osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(ctx.destination);osc.start(start);osc.stop(start+duration+0.025);
  }catch(_){}
}
function scheduleWhistle(ctx,start,duration){
  [1880,2360].forEach((frequency,index)=>{
    try{
      const osc=ctx.createOscillator(),gain=ctx.createGain(),lfo=ctx.createOscillator(),lfoGain=ctx.createGain();
      osc.type='sine';osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(frequency,start);
      lfo.frequency.setValueAtTime(8.5,start);lfoGain.gain.setValueAtTime(32+index*5,start);lfo.connect(lfoGain);lfoGain.connect(osc.frequency);
      gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001,start);gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.028,start+0.025);gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.028,start+Math.max(0.04,duration-0.05));gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,start+duration);
      osc.connect(gain);gain.connect(ctx.destination);lfo.start(start);osc.start(start);osc.stop(start+duration+0.03);lfo.stop(start+duration+0.03);
    }catch(_){}
  });
}
function playButtonClickSound(){
  if(!soundsEnabled())return;const ctx=getSoundContext();if(!ctx)return;
  scheduleTone(ctx,920,ctx.currentTime+0.006,0.045,0.022,'sine',610);
}
let goalCrowdAudio=null;
function playGoalCrowdSound(){
  if(!soundsEnabled())return;
  try{
    if(!goalCrowdAudio){
      goalCrowdAudio=new Audio('https://sfxmint.com/dl/stadium-crowd-02.mp3');
      goalCrowdAudio.preload='auto';
      goalCrowdAudio.volume=0.9;
    }
    goalCrowdAudio.pause();
    goalCrowdAudio.currentTime=0;
    const playback=goalCrowdAudio.play();
    if(playback&&typeof playback.catch==='function')playback.catch(()=>playGoalToneFallback());
  }catch(_){playGoalToneFallback()}
}
function playGoalToneFallback(){
  const ctx=getSoundContext();if(!ctx)return;
  const start=ctx.currentTime+0.025;
  [523.25,659.25,783.99,1046.5].forEach((f,i)=>scheduleTone(ctx,f,start+i*0.135,0.19,0.045,'triangle'));
}
function queueMatchSound(kind,matchId,match){
  const id=String(matchId||'');if(!id||kind!=='goal'||!state.favs.has(id))return;
  notifyFavoriteMatchEvent(id,kind,match);
  if(!soundsEnabled())return;
  if(kind==='goal'){
    playGoalCrowdSound();
    const goalCtx=getSoundContext();if(goalCtx)soundTimeline=Math.max(goalCtx.currentTime,soundTimeline)+5.86;
    return;
  }
  const ctx=getSoundContext();if(!ctx)return;
  const start=Math.max(ctx.currentTime+0.035,soundTimeline);
  const patterns={kickoff:[[0,0.62]],halftime:[[0,0.27],[0.4,0.27]],fulltime:[[0,0.43],[0.58,0.7]]};
  const pattern=patterns[kind]||patterns.kickoff,duration=Math.max(...pattern.map(x=>x[0]+x[1]));
  pattern.forEach(([offset,length])=>scheduleWhistle(ctx,start+offset,length));
  soundTimeline=start+duration+0.16;
}
function scoreNumber(value){return value==null||value===''?null:(Number.isFinite(Number(value))?Number(value):null)}
function makeFavoriteSnapshot(match,previous=null){
  const goals=match.goals||{};
  const home=scoreNumber(goals.home),away=scoreNumber(goals.away);
  return {status:String(match.fixture&&match.fixture.status&&match.fixture.status.short||''),home:home==null&&previous?previous.home:home,away:away==null&&previous?previous.away:away};
}
function observeFavoriteMatchUpdates(matches){
  for(const match of matches||[]){
    const id=String(match.fixture&&match.fixture.id||'');if(!id)continue;
    if(!state.favs.has(id)){favoriteSoundSnapshots.delete(id);continue}
    const previous=favoriteSoundSnapshots.get(id),next=makeFavoriteSnapshot(match,previous);
    if(!previous){favoriteSoundSnapshots.set(id,next);continue}
    const events=[];
    if(['NS','TBD'].includes(previous.status)&&['1H','LIVE'].includes(next.status))events.push('kickoff');
    const goalAdded=(next.home!=null&&previous.home!=null&&next.home>previous.home)||(next.away!=null&&previous.away!=null&&next.away>previous.away);
    if(goalAdded)events.push('goal');
    if(['1H','LIVE'].includes(previous.status)&&next.status==='HT')events.push('halftime');
    if((LIVE.includes(previous.status)||HT.includes(previous.status))&&DONE.includes(next.status))events.push('fulltime');
    favoriteSoundSnapshots.set(id,next);
    events.forEach(kind=>queueMatchSound(kind,id,match));
  }
}
function dismissSplash(){const el=$('#splash');if(!el||el.classList.contains('out'))return;el.classList.add('out');setTimeout(()=>el.remove(),350)}
setTimeout(dismissSplash,15000);

/* ---------- Sunucu isteği ---------- */
async function api(path,timeoutOverride){
  // Sunucu yanıt vermezse arayüz sonsuza kadar "yükleniyor" kalmasın.
  const ms=Number(timeoutOverride)>0?Number(timeoutOverride):(/^\/match\?/.test(path)?25000:30000);
  const ctl=new AbortController(),to=setTimeout(()=>ctl.abort(),ms);
  try{
    const r=await fetch(WORKER_URL+path,{cache:'no-store',signal:ctl.signal});
    let j;try{j=await r.json()}catch(e){throw new Error('Sunucu cevabı okunamadı (HTTP '+r.status+')')}
    if(!r.ok||j.error)throw new Error(j.error||('HTTP '+r.status));
    if(!/^\/(search|player)/.test(path))trTeamsIn(j,'');
    return j;
  }catch(e){
    if(e&&e.name==='AbortError')throw new Error('Sunucu yanıt vermedi (zaman aşımı)');
    throw e;
  }finally{clearTimeout(to)}
}

function trLeague(raw){
  if(/^\s*(?:turkish\s+)?(?:s[uü]per\s+lig|super\s+league)\s*$/i.test(String(raw||'')))return {name:'Süper Lig',country:''};
  if(NAMED[String(raw||'').trim()]){const n=NAMED[String(raw).trim()];return {name:n[0],country:n[1]}}
  let s=String(raw||'').trim(),country='';
  const code=s.match(/^([A-Z]{3})\s+(.+)$/);
  if(code&&CODES[code[1]]){country=CODES[code[1]];s=code[2]}
  else{
    const lo=s.toLowerCase();
    const a=Object.keys(ADJ).find(k=>lo.startsWith(k.toLowerCase()+' '));
    if(a){country=ADJ[a];s=s.slice(a.length).trim()}
    else{const c=CTL.find(k=>lo.startsWith(k.toLowerCase()+' ')||lo===k.toLowerCase());if(c){country=CT[c];s=CT[c]+s.slice(c.length)}}
  }
  WORDS.forEach(([re,t])=>{s=s.replace(re,t)});
  return {name:s.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()||String(raw||''),country};
}

  return {name:t.name,country:L.country||t.country||''};
};

function trTeamsIn(o,key){
  if(!o||typeof o!=='object')return;
  if(Array.isArray(o)){o.forEach(x=>trTeamsIn(x,key));return}
  if((key==='home'||key==='away'||key==='team')&&typeof o.name==='string'){o.raw=o.name;o.name=trTeam(o.name)}
  Object.keys(o).forEach(k=>{const v=o[k];if(v&&typeof v==='object')trTeamsIn(v,k)});
}

const trVal=(k,v)=>{
  if(typeof v!=='string')return v;
  const key=String(k).toLowerCase();
  if(/position/.test(key)){const u=v.toUpperCase();if(POS_TR[u])return POS_TR[u];const l=v.toLowerCase();return /keeper|goalie/.test(l)?'Kaleci':/forward|striker|winger|attack/.test(l)?'Forvet':/midfield/.test(l)?'Orta Saha':/back|defen/.test(l)?'Defans':v}
  if(/foot/.test(key))return ({left:'Sol',right:'Sağ',both:'Her iki ayak'})[v.toLowerCase()]||v;
  if(/country|nationality|citizen/.test(key))return trC(v);
  return v;
};

/* ---------- Maç listesi ---------- */
let loadSeq=0,refreshQueued=false,homeRefreshTimer=null;
const PARTS=4;                                  // sunucudaki ESPN lig grubu sayısı (worker.js ile aynı); 's' = SportScore
/* Aynı maç iki kaynaktan gelirse ESPN'inkini tut (lig adı/ülke/sıralama daha iyi); eşleştirme: takım adları + başlama saati */
const teamAliasKey=value=>{
  let x=String(value||'').toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'');
  const women=/\b(women|womens|ladies|femenino|feminino|frauen)\b/.test(x);
  x=x.replace(/\b(women'?s?|ladies|femenino|feminino|frauen)\b/g,' ')
     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim().replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  x=x.replace(/\b(fc|sk|cf|afc|sc|fk|ac|as|cd|ud|club|the|jk|if|bk|sv|vfb|vfl|fsv|ss|us|rc|cs)\b/g,' ')
     .replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
  const aliases={
    'bayern munchen':'bayern munich','bayern munih':'bayern munich','bayern munich':'bayern munich','bayern':'bayern munich',
    '1 koln':'cologne','koln':'cologne','koeln':'cologne','cologne':'cologne',
    '1 fc koln':'cologne','fc koln':'cologne','fc koeln':'cologne','fc cologne':'cologne'
  };
  return (aliases[x]||x)+(women?' women':'');
};
const simT=(a,b)=>{const x=teamAliasKey(a),y=teamAliasKey(b);return !!x&&!!y&&(x===y||(x.length>=7&&y.length>=7&&(x.includes(y)||y.includes(x))))};
function fixtureQuality(m){
  const f=m&&m.fixture||{},g=m&&m.goals||{},l=m&&m.lineups||[],st=f.status||{};
  return (String(f.id||'').startsWith('e:')?3:0)+(g.home!=null&&g.away!=null?5:0)+(st.short&&st.short!=='TBD'?2:0)+(Array.isArray(l)&&l.length?3:0)+(m&&m.statistics&&m.statistics.length?1:0)+(m&&m.referee?1:0)+(m&&m.venue?1:0);
}
function dedupe(list){
  const unique=new Map();
  for(const m of list||[]){
    if(!m||!m.fixture||!m.teams||!m.teams.home||!m.teams.away)continue;
    const id=String(m.fixture.id||'');if(!id)continue;
    const old=unique.get(id);if(!old||fixtureQuality(m)>fixtureQuality(old))unique.set(id,m);
  }
  const out=[];
  for(const m of unique.values()){
    const date=Date.parse(m.fixture.date),home=teamAliasKey(m.teams.home.name),away=teamAliasKey(m.teams.away.name);
    let duplicateIndex=-1;
    if(Number.isFinite(date)){
      for(let i=0;i<out.length;i++){
        const old=out[i],oldDate=Date.parse(old.fixture.date);
        if(!Number.isFinite(oldDate)||Math.abs(date-oldDate)>90*60000)continue;
        if(home===teamAliasKey(old.teams.home.name)&&away===teamAliasKey(old.teams.away.name)){duplicateIndex=i;break}
      }
    }
    if(duplicateIndex<0)out.push(m);
    else {
      const old=out[duplicateIndex],oldId=String(old.fixture.id),newId=String(m.fixture.id);
      const oldFav=state.favs.has(oldId),newFav=state.favs.has(newId);
      const keepNew=(newFav&&!oldFav)||(newFav===oldFav&&fixtureQuality(m)>fixtureQuality(old));
      const keptId=keepNew?newId:oldId,droppedId=keepNew?oldId:newId;
      if(oldFav||newFav){
        state.favs.delete(droppedId);state.favs.add(keptId);
        const droppedSnap=favoriteSoundSnapshots.get(droppedId);
        if(droppedSnap&&!favoriteSoundSnapshots.has(keptId))favoriteSoundSnapshots.set(keptId,droppedSnap);
        favoriteSoundSnapshots.delete(droppedId);
      }
      if(keepNew)out[duplicateIndex]=m;
    }
  }
  return out.sort((a,b)=>{const d=Date.parse(a.fixture.date)-Date.parse(b.fixture.date);if(d)return d;const l=String(a.league&&a.league.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.league&&b.league.name||''),'tr');if(l)return l;const h=String(a.teams.home.name||'').localeCompare(String(b.teams.home.name||''),'tr');return h||String(a.fixture.id).localeCompare(String(b.fixture.id));});
}
async function load(silent){
  if(silent&&state.requesting){refreshQueued=true;return;}
  const my=++loadSeq, day=ymd(state.date);
  state.requesting=true;
  if(!silent){state.data=[];state.partial=0;state.loading=true;$('#list').innerHTML='<div class="empty">Dünyadaki maçlar yükleniyor…</div>';renderTabs();$('#cnt').textContent=''}
  const acc=[],fails=[];
  try{
    const res=await Promise.all([...Array(PARTS).keys(),'s'].map(async i=>{
      try{
        const j=await api('/fixtures?date='+day+'&part='+i);
        if(my!==loadSeq)return null;
        const out=j.response||[];if(j.capped)out.capped=true;
        // İlk açılışta ve gün değişiminde kaynaklar tamamlanmadan kısmi listeyi çizme.
        // Aksi halde bir sağlayıcının geçici/eksik fikstürü 5-10 saniye görünüp sonra değişiyordu.
        acc.push(...out);
        return out;
      }catch(e){fails.push({i,m:e.message||String(e)});return null}
    }));
    if(my!==loadSeq)return;
    state.loading=false;
    const ok=res.filter(Boolean);
    if(!ok.length){
      if(silent&&state.data.length)return;       // arka plan yenilemesi başarısızsa eski veri kalsın
      state.data=[];renderTabs();
      $('#list').innerHTML='<div class="empty">'+esc(state.date.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'numeric',month:'long'}))+' için şu anda görüntülenemiyor:<br><br>'+nice((fails[0]&&fails[0].m)||'bilinmeyen hata')+'</div>';
      dismissSplash();
      return;
    }
    const ssFailed=fails.some(f=>f.i==='s');
    let all=ok.flat();
    // Başarılı gruplardaki yenileri koru; başarısız grupların son görülen kayıtlarını tamamla.
    if(silent&&fails.length)all=all.concat(state.data);
    else if(ssFailed)all=all.concat(state.data.filter(m=>String(m.fixture.id).startsWith('s:')));
    state.capped=res.some(r=>r&&r.capped);
    state.data=dedupe(all);try{localStorage.favs=JSON.stringify([...state.favs])}catch(_){}syncLiveMinutes(state.data);observeFavoriteMatchUpdates(state.data);state.partial=fails.length;state.src='';state.loadedAt=Date.now();render();dismissSplash();
  }finally{
    if(my===loadSeq){state.requesting=false;if(refreshQueued){refreshQueued=false;setTimeout(()=>{if(!document.hidden&&isToday()&&!sheetOpen)load(true)},250);}}
  }
}
function filtered(){
  if(state.tab==='track')return [];
  const q=state.q.toLowerCase().trim();
  return state.data.filter(m=>{
    const s=stat(m);
    if(state.tab==='live'&&!isLive(m))return false;
    if(state.tab==='done'&&!DONE.includes(s))return false;
    if(state.tab==='soon'&&!NS.includes(s))return false;
    if(state.tab==='fav'&&!state.favs.has(String(m.fixture.id)))return false;
    if(state.imp&&rank(m.league)>=1000)return false;
    if(q&&![m.teams.home.name,m.teams.away.name,m.league.name,m.league.country,lgT(m.league).name,lgT(m.league).country].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(q))return false;
    return true;
  });
}
function renderTabs(){
  const live=state.data.filter(isLive).length;
  const T=[['all','Tümü'],['live','Canlı'+(live?'<span class="n">'+live+'</span>':'')],['done','Biten'],['soon','Başlayacak'],['fav','★'],['track','Takip']];
  $('#tabs').innerHTML=T.map(([k,l])=>`<div class="t ${state.tab===k?'on':''}" data-k="${k}">${l}</div>`).join('');
}
/* Takvim: 7 gün geri, 30 gün ileri */
function renderDates(){
  const out=[],names={'-1':'Dün','0':'Bugün','1':'Yarın'};
  for(let i=-14;i<=30;i++){
    const d=new Date();d.setDate(d.getDate()+i);
    const on=ymd(d)===ymd(state.date);
    out.push(`<div class="d ${on?'on':''}" data-d="${ymd(d)}"><small>${names[i]||d.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{weekday:'short'})}</small>${d.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'})}</div>`);
  }
  $('#dates').innerHTML=out.join('');
  const on=$('#dates .on');if(on&&on.scrollIntoView)on.scrollIntoView({inline:'center',block:'nearest'});
}
const MINS={};
// Maç detayı FT/MS gösterdiğinde, ana listedeki eski canlı verinin dakikayı geri almasını önle.
const FINAL_MATCHES=new Map();
function minuteBase(e){const m=String(e==null?'':e).match(/^(\d+)(?:\+(\d+))?$/);return m?Number(m[1])+(Number(m[2])||0):null}
function mergeMatchGoals(incoming,previous){if(!incoming)return previous?{...previous}:null;return {home:incoming.home==null&&previous?previous.home:incoming.home,away:incoming.away==null&&previous?previous.away:incoming.away}}
function setLiveMinute(id,elapsed,status){
  const n=minuteBase(elapsed);if(n==null)return;
  const old=MINS[id];if(old&&old.frozen)return;
  const secondHalf=status==='2H'&&old&&old.status==='1H'&&n<old.e;
  if(!old||n>old.e||secondHalf)MINS[id]={e:n,at:Date.now(),status,frozen:false};
}
function freezeLiveMinute(id,fallbackElapsed=null){
  const old=MINS[id];
  // Uygulama arka planda kaldıysa geçen tüm süreyi maç dakikasına ekleme.
  const age=old?Math.max(0,Date.now()-old.at):0;
  const lastSeen=old?(old.e+(age<=90000?Math.floor(age/60000):0)):null;
  const apiMinute=minuteBase(fallbackElapsed);
  const finalMinute=Math.max(lastSeen==null?-1:lastSeen,apiMinute==null?-1:apiMinute);
  if(finalMinute>=0)MINS[id]={e:finalMinute,at:Date.now(),status:'FT',frozen:true};
}
function rememberFinalMatch(match){
  if(!match||!match.fixture)return;
  const id=String(match.fixture.id||''),short=stat(match);
  if(!id||!DONE.includes(short))return;
  const old=FINAL_MATCHES.get(id);
  FINAL_MATCHES.set(id,{short,goals:mergeMatchGoals(match.goals,old&&old.goals)});
  freezeLiveMinute(id,match.fixture.status&&match.fixture.status.elapsed);
}
function syncLiveMinutes(list){
  for(const m of list||[]){
    const id=String(m.fixture.id||''),final=FINAL_MATCHES.get(id);
    if(final&&!DONE.includes(stat(m))){
      m.fixture.status={...(m.fixture.status||{}),short:final.short,elapsed:null};
      if(final.goals)m.goals=mergeMatchGoals(final.goals,m.goals);
    }
    const s=stat(m);
    if(DONE.includes(s)){rememberFinalMatch(m);continue}
    if(liveS(s))setLiveMinute(id,m.fixture.status.elapsed,s);
  }
}
function minOf(id,e){const o=MINS[id];if(o&&o.frozen)return String(o.e);if(o&&Date.now()-o.at<10800000)return String(o.e+Math.floor((Date.now()-o.at)/60000));return e}
function statusCell(m){
  const s=stat(m),e=minOf(m.fixture.id,m.fixture.status.elapsed);
  if(HT.includes(s))return '<div class="st ht">DA</div>';
  if(LIVE.includes(s))return e==null||e===''?'<div class="st live nm">CANLI</div>':`<div class="st live">${e}</div>`;
  if(DONE.includes(s))return '<div class="st">'+(s==='FT'?'MS':s)+'</div>';
  if(NS.includes(s))return '<div class="st">'+new Date(m.fixture.date).toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})+'</div>';
  const map={PST:'Ertelendi',CANC:'İptal',SUSP:'Durdu',ABD:'Yarıda'};
  return '<div class="st">'+(map[s]||s)+'</div>';
}
function matchOddsAttrs(m){
  const f=m&&m.fixture||{},status=f.status||{},teams=m&&m.teams||{},home=teams.home||{},away=teams.away||{},goals=m&&m.goals||{},league=m&&m.league||{};
  const attr=(key,value)=>`data-${key}="${esc(value==null?'':value)}"`;
  return [
    attr('m',f.id),attr('home-name',home.name||''),attr('away-name',away.name||''),
    attr('home-raw',home.raw||home.name||''),attr('away-raw',away.raw||away.name||''),
    attr('home-id',home.id||''),attr('away-id',away.id||''),attr('date',f.date||''),
    attr('status',status.short||'NS'),attr('elapsed',status.elapsed),
    attr('home-score',goals.home),attr('away-score',goals.away),
    attr('league-name',league.name||''),attr('league-id',league.id||''),
    attr('league-code',league.code||''),attr('league-country',league.country||''),attr('league-season',league.season||'')
  ].join(' ');
}
function trackedPicksHTML(){
  const logo='<img class="tracked-iddaa-logo" src="https://www.iddaa.com/images/logo-secondary.svg" alt="iddaa" loading="lazy">';
  const entries=trackedPicks.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.kickoff||'').localeCompare(String(b.kickoff||''))).map(p=>{
    const id=esc(p.id),finished=p.resolved===true,score=p.score?esc(p.score):'';
    const status=p.void?'<span class="pick-result pending" title="Maç iptal edildi">—</span>':finished?(p.correct?'<span class="pick-result good" title="Seçimin doğru">✓</span>':'<span class="pick-result bad" title="Seçimin yanlış">✓</span>'):(Date.parse(p.kickoff)>Date.now()?`<span class="pick-countdown" data-pick-countdown="${id}">Başlamasına —</span>`:'<span class="pick-result pending" title="Sonuç bekleniyor">◷</span>');
    return `<div class="tracked-pick" data-pick-row="${id}"><div class="tracked-pick-teams"><b>${esc(p.home)}</b><span> – </span><b>${esc(p.away)}</b><small>${esc(p.league||'')} ${score?'· '+score:''}</small></div><div class="tracked-pick-selection"><span>${esc(p.marketName||'Maç Sonucu')} · ${esc(p.selectionName||p.selectionId||'')}</span><b>${Number(p.odd||0).toFixed(2)}</b></div><div class="tracked-pick-status">${status}<button data-pick-remove="${id}" title="Takipten çıkar" aria-label="Takipten çıkar">×</button></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<section class="tracked-picks"><div class="tracked-picks-heading">${logo}<div><b>Takibe Aldığım Maçlar</b><small>Seçtiğin İddaa marketleri ve maç başlama sayaçları</small></div><span class="tracked-count">${trackedPicks.length}</span></div>${entries||'<div class="tracked-empty">Henüz takipte maç yok. Ana sayfadaki İddaa düğmesine basıp bir market seçtiğinde burada görünecek.</div>'}</section>`;
}
function updateTrackedCountdowns(){
  const now=Date.now();document.querySelectorAll('[data-pick-countdown]').forEach(el=>{const p=trackedPicks.find(x=>String(x.id)===el.dataset.pickCountdown);if(!p)return;const ms=Date.parse(p.kickoff)-now;if(ms<=0){el.textContent='Başlama zamanı geldi';return;}const sec=Math.floor(ms/1000),d=Math.floor(sec/86400),hh=Math.floor(sec%86400/3600),mm=Math.floor(sec%3600/60),ss=sec%60;el.textContent='Başlamasına '+(d?d+'g ':'')+[hh,mm,ss].map(v=>String(v).padStart(2,'0')).join(':');});
}
function render(){
  renderTabs();
  const ms=filtered(),groups={};
  const lgKey=L=>{const t=lgT(L);return (t.country||'')+'|'+t.name};
  const lgOf=arr=>(arr.find(m=>m.league.code)||arr[0]).league;   // ESPN kaydı varsa onu kullan (puan durumu bağlantısı, sıralama)
  ms.forEach(m=>{const k=lgKey(m.league);(groups[k]=groups[k]||[]).push(m)});
  const keys=Object.keys(groups).sort((a,b)=>{
    const A=lgOf(groups[a]),B=lgOf(groups[b]),ra=rank(A),rb=rank(B);
    if(ra!==rb)return ra-rb;
    const ta=lgT(A),tb=lgT(B);return ((ta.country||'~')+ta.name).localeCompare((tb.country||'~')+tb.name,'tr');
  });
  $('#cnt').textContent=ms.length+' maç · '+Object.keys(groups).length+' lig';
  $('#upd').textContent='Son güncelleme: '+new Date(state.loadedAt||Date.now()).toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR');
  const tracked=trackedPicksHTML();
  if(!keys.length){$('#list').innerHTML=tracked+'<div class="empty">'+(state.tab==='track'?'Takip listen yukarıda görüntüleniyor.':state.loading?'Maçlar yükleniyor…':state.tab==='live'?'Şu an canlı maç yok.':'Bu filtrede şu anda maç görünmüyor.')+'<br><small>'+state.data.length+' maç yüklendi</small></div>';updateTrackedCountdowns();return}
  $('#list').innerHTML=tracked+keys.map(k=>{
    const L=lgOf(groups[k]);
    const rows=groups[k].sort((a,b)=>new Date(a.fixture.date)-new Date(b.fixture.date)).map(m=>{
      const g=m.goals,lv=isLive(m),id=String(m.fixture.id);
      const dn=DONE.includes(stat(m));
      const hw=g.home!=null&&dn&&g.home>g.away, aw=g.away!=null&&dn&&g.away>g.home;
      const sig=g.home+'-'+g.away, changed=state.prev[id]!==undefined&&state.prev[id]!==sig;
      state.prev[id]=sig;
      return `<div class="m iddaa-home-match ${changed?'flash':''}" ${matchOddsAttrs(m)}>
        ${statusCell(m)}
        <div><div class="tm ${hw?'w':''}">${img(m.teams.home.logo)}${esc(m.teams.home.name)}</div>
             <div class="tm ${aw?'w':''}">${img(m.teams.away.logo)}${esc(m.teams.away.name)}</div></div>
        <div class="sc ${lv?'live':''}"><div>${g.home==null?'-':g.home}</div><div>${g.away==null?'-':g.away}</div></div>
        <div class="fav ${state.favs.has(id)?'on':''}" data-id="${id}" aria-label="Favori">${state.favs.has(id)?'★':'☆'}</div>
        <button type="button" class="iddaa-open-btn" data-iddaa-open="${esc(id)}" title="İddaa marketlerini aç" aria-label="${esc(m.teams.home.name)} – ${esc(m.teams.away.name)} İddaa marketlerini aç"><img       </div>`;
    }).join('');
    const ll=LEAGUE_PNGS[L.code]||LEAGUE_LOGOS[L.code]||leagueLogo(L);
    return `<section class="lg"><div class="lgh" data-lg="${esc(L.code||'')}">${ll?`<img src="${esc(ll)}" alt="" loading="lazy">`:''}${esc(lgT(L).name)} ${(()=>{const t=lgT(L);return t.country&&!t.name.toLowerCase().includes(t.country.toLowerCase())?`<span>${esc(t.country)}</span>`:''})()}${L.code?'<span class="go">📊 Puan</span>':''}</div>${rows}</section>`;
  }).join('');
  updateTrackedCountdowns();
}

/* ---------- Alt ekran ---------- */
let sheetOpen=false,timer=null,cur=null;
function showSheet(){
  if(!sheetOpen){sheetOpen=true;try{history.pushState({s:1},'')}catch(_){}}
  $('#sheet').hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('lock');$('#sheet').scrollTop=0;
}
function hideSheet(){
  sheetOpen=false;$('#sheet').hidden=true;document.body.classList.remove('lock');clearTimeout(timer);cur=null;
  // Detaydan ana listeye dönünce beklemeden son skor/durum verisini al.
  if(isToday())setTimeout(()=>load(true),120);
}
function renderCur(){if(!cur)return;if(cur.kind==='menu'){renderMenu();return}if(cur.kind==='team')renderTeam();else if(cur.kind==='match')renderMatch();else if(cur.kind==='st')renderSt();else if(cur.kind==='league')renderLeague();else if(cur.kind==='search')renderSearch();else if(cur.kind==='player')renderPlayer()}
function goPrev(){cur=cur.prev;renderCur();$('#sheet').scrollTop=0}
window.addEventListener('popstate',()=>{if(!sheetOpen)return;if(cur&&cur.prev){goPrev();try{history.pushState({s:1},'')}catch(_){}}else hideSheet()});
$('#shBack').onclick=()=>{if(cur&&cur.prev){goPrev();return}try{if(history.state&&history.state.s){history.back();return}}catch(_){}hideSheet()};

const STAT_ALIASES_TR={'total shots':'Toplam Şut','shots on goal':'İsabetli Şut','shots on target':'İsabetli Şut','shots off goal':'İsabetsiz Şut','shots off target':'İsabetsiz Şut','shots inside box':'Ceza Sahası İçi Şut','shots outside box':'Ceza Sahası Dışı Şut','total passes':'Toplam Pas','passes accurate':'İsabetli Pas','accurate passes':'İsabetli Pas','pass accuracy':'Pas Başarısı','passes %':'Pas Başarısı','ball possession':'Topa Sahip Olma','possession %':'Topa Sahip Olma','corner kicks':'Korner','throw ins':'Taç','fouls committed':'Faul','fouls suffered':'Yenilen Faul','yellow cards':'Sarı Kart','red cards':'Kırmızı Kart','goalkeeper saves':'Kaleci Kurtarışı','saves':'Kaleci Kurtarışı','tackles won':'Kazanılan Top Kapma','total tackles':'Top Kapma','interceptions':'Top Çalma','clearances':'Uzaklaştırma','total clearances':'Uzaklaştırma','blocked shots':'Engellenen Şut','hit woodwork':'Direkten Dönen Şut','big chances':'Net Gol Fırsatı','big chances created':'Yaratılan Net Gol Fırsatı','expected goals':'Beklenen Gol (xG)','expected assists':'Beklenen Asist (xA)','goals prevented':'Önlenen Gol','duels won':'Kazanılan İkili Mücadele','total duels won':'Kazanılan İkili Mücadele','aerial duels won':'Kazanılan Hava Topu','successful dribbles':'Başarılı Çalım','dribbles completed':'Başarılı Çalım','offsides':'Ofsayt','penalty goals':'Penaltı Golü','penalty kick goals':'Penaltı Golü','accurate long balls':'İsabetli Uzun Pas','long balls':'Uzun Pas','accurate crosses':'İsabetli Orta','total crosses':'Orta'};
const STAT_MORE_TR={'attempts on goal':'Toplam Şut','shots':'Toplam Şut','possession':'Topa Sahip Olma','corners':'Korner','fouls':'Faul','offsides':'Ofsayt','offside':'Ofsayt','tackles':'Top Kapma','crosses':'Orta','passes':'Toplam Pas','accurate passes':'İsabetli Pas','dribbles completed':'Başarılı Çalım','total clearances':'Uzaklaştırma','long balls':'Uzun Pas','penalties':'Penaltı','blocked shots':'Engellenen Şut'};
const STAT_LABELS_TR=new Set([...Object.values(STAT_TR),...Object.values(STAT_ALIASES_TR),...Object.values(STAT_MORE_TR)]);
function statLabelTR(raw){
  const s=String(raw||'').trim();if(!s)return '';
  if(STAT_LABELS_TR.has(s))return s;
  const aliasKey=s.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g,'$1 $2').toLowerCase().replace(/[_-]+/g,' ').replace(/[()]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
  if(STAT_MORE_TR[aliasKey])return STAT_MORE_TR[aliasKey];
  if(STAT_ALIASES_TR[aliasKey])return STAT_ALIASES_TR[aliasKey];
  const entry=Object.entries(STAT_TR).find(([k])=>k.toLowerCase()===s.toLowerCase());if(entry)return entry[1];
  return '';
}
function initialMatchSnapshot(base,id){
  if(!base||!base.fixture||!base.teams||!base.teams.home||!base.teams.away)return null;
  const fixture=base.fixture||{},status={...(fixture.status&&typeof fixture.status==='object'?fixture.status:{short:String(fixture.status||'NS'),elapsed:null})};
  let goals={home:base.goals&&base.goals.home!=null?base.goals.home:null,away:base.goals&&base.goals.away!=null?base.goals.away:null};
  const key=String(id==null?fixture.id:id),final=FINAL_MATCHES.get(key),minute=MINS[key];
  // Reuse the exact final score or live minute already visible in the match list.
  // This avoids briefly reverting to stale NS/old-score data while detail APIs load.
  if(final&&DONE.includes(final.short)){
    status.short=final.short;status.elapsed=null;goals=mergeMatchGoals(final.goals,goals);
  }else if(minute&&!minute.frozen&&Date.now()-Number(minute.at||0)<3*60*60*1000&&!DONE.includes(status.short)){
    status.short=minute.status||status.short||'1H';
    status.elapsed=Number(minute.e)+Math.floor(Math.max(0,Date.now()-Number(minute.at||Date.now()))/60000);
  }
  return {...base,fixture:{...fixture,id:fixture.id==null?id:fixture.id,status},
    teams:{...base.teams,home:{...base.teams.home},away:{...base.teams.away}},goals,
    events:Array.isArray(base.events)?base.events.slice():[],lineups:Array.isArray(base.lineups)?base.lineups.slice():[],
    statistics:Array.isArray(base.statistics)?base.statistics.slice():[]};
}
async function openMatch(id,b2,initialTab='ozet'){
  const prevT=cur&&(cur.kind==='team'||cur.kind==='league')?cur:null;
  const base=(prevT&&b2)||b2||state.data.find(m=>String(m.fixture.id)===String(id))||matchForLongPress(String(id),null);
  const initial=initialMatchSnapshot(base,id);
  showSheet();
  cur={kind:'match',id,tab:initialTab||'ozet',data:initial,base,err:'',detailLoaded:!!initial,detailLoading:!!initial,detailEnrichmentStarted:false,prev:prevT,kview:'kadro',pitchLookupDone:false,pitchLookupBusy:false,pitchLookupAt:0,pitchLoaded:false,matchPlayerId:'',matchPlayerName:'',matchPlayerData:null,matchPlayerLoading:false,matchPlayerError:'',matchPreview:null,matchPreviewLoading:false,matchPreviewLoaded:false,matchPreviewError:'',iddaa:null,iddaaLoading:false,iddaaLoaded:false,h2h:null,h2hLoading:false,h2hLoaded:false,h2hError:''};
  $('#shTitle').textContent=base?base.teams.home.name+' - '+base.teams.away.name:'Maç';
  renderMatch();
  // Immediately start PitchAPI enrichment for every match source, including native PitchAPI IDs.
  if(base&&String(id).startsWith('p:'))loadPitchMatchEvents(cur,false);
  if(base)loadPitchMatchEnrichment(cur,false);
  loadMatch();
}
function pitchSameSide(a,b){
  const ka=playerNameKey(a&&a.name||''),kb=playerNameKey(b&&b.name||'');
  return !!ka&&!!kb&&(ka===kb||(Math.min(ka.length,kb.length)>=6&&(ka.includes(kb)||kb.includes(ka))));
}
function matchPlayerIdentityEqual(a,b){
  if(!a||!b)return false;
  const idsA=[a.pitchId,a.player_id,a.id].filter(Boolean).map(String),idsB=[b.pitchId,b.player_id,b.id].filter(Boolean).map(String);
  if(idsA.some(id=>/^p_[A-Za-z0-9]+$/.test(id)&&idsB.includes(id)))return true;
  const teamA=String(a.teamId||a.team_id||a.team&&a.team.id||''),teamB=String(b.teamId||b.team_id||b.team&&b.team.id||'');
  // Team IDs belong to different providers (PitchAPI t_*, API-Football numeric IDs,
  // synthetic h/a IDs). Compare them only when both IDs use the same namespace.
  const namespace=id=>/^t_/.test(id)?'pitch':/^\d+$/.test(id)?'numeric':/^[ha]$/i.test(id)?'synthetic':'other';
  if(teamA&&teamB&&namespace(teamA)===namespace(teamB)&&teamA!==teamB)return false;
  const ka=matchPlayerKey(a.name||a.short_name||a.full_name||''),kb=matchPlayerKey(b.name||b.short_name||b.full_name||'');
  if(!ka||!kb)return false;
  if(ka===kb)return true;
  const aa=ka.split(' '),bb=kb.split(' '),lastA=aa[aa.length-1],lastB=bb[bb.length-1];
  return !!lastA&&lastA===lastB&&(aa.length===1||bb.length===1||aa[0][0]===bb[0][0]);
}
function matchPositionRank(value){
  const p=String(value||'').trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g,'');
  if(!p)return 0;
  if(['GK','GOALKEEPER','GOALIE','RB','LB','RWB','LWB','RCB','LCB','CB','SW','CDM','DM','RDM','LDM','CM','RCM','LCM','CAM','AM','RAM','LAM','RM','LM','RW','LW','RWF','LWF','ST','CF','RCF','LCF','SS','WB'].includes(p))return 2;
  if(['G','D','DF','DEF','DEFENDER','M','MF','MID','MIDFIELDER','F','FW','FWD','FORWARD','ATTACKER','STRIKER'].includes(p))return 1;
  return 0;
}
function mergeMatchPlayerData(previous,next){
  const merged={...(previous||{}),...(next||{})};
  for(const key of ['pitchId','place','pitchX','pitchY'])if((merged[key]==null||merged[key]==='')&&previous&&previous[key]!=null)merged[key]=previous[key];
  for(const key of ['logo','photo','image_url']){
    const oldUrl=String(previous&&previous[key]||''),newUrl=String(next&&next[key]||'');
    // Once a previously rendered HTTPS portrait is known, a sparse refresh must not replace it.
    if(/^https:\/\//i.test(oldUrl)&&!/^https:\/\//i.test(newUrl))merged[key]=oldUrl;
    else if(/^https:\/\//i.test(oldUrl)&&/^https:\/\//i.test(newUrl)&&oldUrl!==newUrl)merged[key]=oldUrl;
  }
  const oldPos=String(previous&&previous.pos||'').trim(),newPos=String(next&&next.pos||'').trim();
  const oldPositionRank=matchPositionRank(oldPos),newPositionRank=matchPositionRank(newPos);
  const oldSource=String(previous&&previous.posSource||''),newSource=String(next&&next.posSource||'');
  const isVerifiedSource=source=>/PitchAPI maç kadrosu mevki|TheSportsDB maç kadrosu|ESPN maç kadrosu \(Opta\)|API-Football maç kadrosu/i.test(String(source||''));
  const sourcePriority=source=>{const s=String(source||'');if(/API-Football maç kadrosu \(diziliş ızgarası\)/i.test(s))return 60;if(/PitchAPI maç kadrosu mevki/i.test(s))return 55;if(/ESPN maç kadrosu \(Opta\)/i.test(s))return 50;if(/TheSportsDB maç kadrosu/i.test(s))return 45;if(/API-Football maç kadrosu/i.test(s))return 35;if(/koordinat tahmini|formasyon şablonu|formasyon tahmini/i.test(s))return 10;return 20;};
  const oldInferred=/koordinat tahmini|formasyon şablonu|formasyon tahmini/i.test(oldSource);
  const oldVerified=isVerifiedSource(oldSource),newVerified=isVerifiedSource(newSource);
  const oldPriority=sourcePriority(oldSource),newPriority=sourcePriority(newSource);
  // Source trust outranks recency: a verified match-day role replaces an estimate,
  // while a generic roster refresh cannot overwrite an explicit fixture-specific role.
  if(newPos&&newPositionRank>0&&(oldInferred||!oldPos||newPriority>oldPriority||(newPriority===oldPriority&&newPositionRank>oldPositionRank))){merged.pos=newPos;merged.posSource=newSource;}
  else if(oldPos&&oldPositionRank>0&&(oldPriority>newPriority||oldVerified&&!newVerified||oldPositionRank>newPositionRank)){merged.pos=oldPos;merged.posSource=oldSource;}
  else if(newPos&&(!oldPos||newPositionRank>=oldPositionRank)){merged.pos=newPos;merged.posSource=newSource||merged.posSource;}
  else if(oldPos){merged.pos=oldPos;merged.posSource=oldSource||merged.posSource;}
  if((next&&next.rating==null||next&&next.rating==='')&&previous&&previous.rating!=null){merged.rating=previous.rating;merged.ratingSource=previous.ratingSource||merged.ratingSource;}
  return merged;
}
function mergePitchPlayerRows(previousRows,nextRows){
  const oldRows=Array.isArray(previousRows)?previousRows:[],incoming=Array.isArray(nextRows)?nextRows:[];
  if(!incoming.length)return oldRows;
  const merged=incoming.map(next=>{
    const prior=oldRows.find(old=>matchPlayerIdentityEqual(old,next));
    if(!prior)return next;
    const row=mergeMatchPlayerData(prior,next);
    if((!Array.isArray(next.stats)||!next.stats.length)&&Array.isArray(prior.stats)&&prior.stats.length)row.stats=prior.stats;
    if((!next.advanced||!Object.keys(next.advanced||{}).length)&&prior.advanced)row.advanced=prior.advanced;
    return row;
  });
  for(const old of oldRows)if(!merged.some(row=>matchPlayerIdentityEqual(row,old)))merged.push(old);
  return merged;
}
function mergePitchMatchIntoCurrent(my, payload){
  const current=my.data, pm=payload&&payload.match, pitch=payload&&payload.pitch||pm&&pm.pitch;
  if(!current||!pm||!pitch)return false;
  const sides=[current.teams.home,current.teams.away], pitchSides=[pm.teams.home,pm.teams.away];
  const existingSide=src=>pitchSameSide(src,sides[1])?sides[1]:sides[0];
  const mapTeam=src=>{const target=existingSide(src||{});return {id:target.id,name:target.name,logo:target.logo||src&&src.logo||''}};
  if(Array.isArray(pm.lineups)&&pm.lineups.length){
    const oldSides=Array.isArray(current.lineups)?current.lineups:[];
    const mergedSides=pm.lineups.map((side,index)=>{
      const team=mapTeam(side.team||pitchSides[index]||{});
      const oldSide=oldSides.find(x=>pitchSameSide(x.team,team))||oldSides[index]||{};
      if(!(side.startXI||[]).length&&(oldSide.startXI||[]).length)return oldSide;
      const keep=(entry,isSub)=>{
        const player=entry.player||entry,oldRows=[...(oldSide.startXI||[]),...(oldSide.substitutes||[])];
        const prior=oldRows.map(x=>x.player||x).find(x=>matchPlayerIdentityEqual(x,player));
        if(!prior)return entry;
        return {...entry,player:mergeMatchPlayerData(prior,player)};
      };
      return {...side,team,startXI:(side.startXI||[]).map(x=>keep(x,false)),substitutes:(side.substitutes||[]).map(x=>keep(x,true))};
    });
    current.lineups=mergedSides;
    current.lineupsSource='PitchAPI';
  }
  if(Array.isArray(pm.events)&&pm.events.length){
    current.events=mergeMatchEventHistory(current.events,pm.events.map(ev=>({...ev,team:ev.team?mapTeam(ev.team):null})));
    current.eventsSource='Birleştirilmiş maç olayları';
  }
  if(Array.isArray(pm.statistics)&&pm.statistics.length){
    current.statistics=pm.statistics.map(st=>({...st,team:mapTeam(st.team||{})}));
    current.statisticsSource='PitchAPI';
  }
  const oldPitch=current.pitch||{};
  current.pitch={...oldPitch,...pitch};
  if(Array.isArray(pitch.players)&&pitch.players.length)current.pitch.players=mergePitchPlayerRows(oldPitch.players,pitch.players);
  for(const key of ['lineups','stats','events'])if((!Array.isArray(pitch[key])||!pitch[key].length)&&Array.isArray(oldPitch[key])&&oldPitch[key].length)current.pitch[key]=oldPitch[key];
  current.pitchSource='PitchAPI';
  current.referee=pm.referee||current.referee||'';
  current.stadium=pm.stadium||current.stadium||'';
  current.city=pm.city||current.city||'';
  if(pm.fixture&&pm.fixture.venue&&pm.fixture.venue.name){current.fixture.venue={...(current.fixture.venue||{}),...pm.fixture.venue}}
  if((current.goals.home==null||current.goals.away==null)&&pm.goals)current.goals={...current.goals,...pm.goals};
  my.pitchLoaded=true;my.pitchErr='';
  return true;
}
async function loadPitchLivePhotos(my,forceRefresh=false){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=="match"||!my.data)return null;
  const sides=Array.isArray(my.data.lineups)?my.data.lineups:[];
  const hasMissingPhoto=sides.some(side=>[...(side.startXI||[]),...(side.substitutes||[])].some(entry=>{
    const p=entry.player||entry;return !/^https:\/\//i.test(String(p.logo||p.photo||p.image_url||''));
  }));
  if(!hasMissingPhoto)return null;
  const matchId=String(my.data.pitch&&my.data.pitch.id||String(my.id||"").replace(/^p:/,""));
  if(!/^m_[A-Za-z0-9]{6}$/.test(matchId))return null;
  if(my.pitchPhotosPromise)return my.pitchPhotosPromise;
  if(!forceRefresh&&Date.now()-Number(my.pitchPhotosAt||0)<9000)return null;
  my.pitchPhotosAt=Date.now();
  const task=(async()=>{
    try{
      const result=await api('/pitch-match-photos?id='+encodeURIComponent(matchId));
      if(cur!==my||!my.data)return null;
      const photos=Array.isArray(result&&result.photos)?result.photos:[];
      let changed=false;
      const sides=my.data.lineups||[];
      for(const side of sides){
        const sideTeamId=String(side.team&&side.team.id||'');
        for(const entry of [...(side.startXI||[]),...(side.substitutes||[])]){
          const player=entry.player||entry;
          if(/^https:\/\//i.test(String(player.logo||player.photo||player.image_url||'')))continue;
          const id=String(player.pitchId||player.id||player.player_id||'');
          const candidates=photos.filter(row=>{
            const rid=String(row.id||row.player_id||''),rowTeam=String(row.team_id||'');
            if(sideTeamId&&rowTeam&&sideTeamId!==rowTeam)return false;
            if(id&&rid&&id===rid)return true;
            return matchPlayerIdentityEqual(player,{name:row.name||row.full_name||'',teamId:rowTeam});
          });
          const row=candidates.length===1?candidates[0]:candidates.find(x=>matchPlayerKey(x.name)===matchPlayerKey(player.name));
          const url=String(row&&(row.image_url||row.photo||row.logo)||'');
          if(/^https:\/\//i.test(url)){player.logo=url;player.photo=url;player.image_url=url;player.photoSource='PitchAPI';changed=true;}
        }
      }
      const pitchPlayers=my.data.pitch&&Array.isArray(my.data.pitch.players)?my.data.pitch.players:[];
      for(const player of pitchPlayers){
        if(player.logo||player.photo||player.image_url)continue;
        const id=String(player.id||player.player_id||'');
        const teamId=String(player.teamId||player.team_id||'');
        const candidates=photos.filter(row=>(!teamId||!row.team_id||teamId===String(row.team_id))&&((id&&String(row.id||row.player_id||'')===id)||matchPlayerKey(row.name)===matchPlayerKey(player.name)));
        const row=candidates.length===1?candidates[0]:null;
        const url=String(row&&(row.image_url||row.photo||row.logo)||'');
        if(/^https:\/\//i.test(url)){player.logo=url;player.photo=url;player.image_url=url;changed=true;}
      }
      for(const event of (my.data.events||[])){
        const sideTeam=event&&event.team||{},sideTeamId=String(sideTeam.id||'');
        for(const player of [event&&event.player,event&&event.assist]){
          if(!player||typeof player!=='object'||/^https?:\/\//i.test(String(player.logo||player.photo||player.image_url||'')))continue;
          const pname=player.name||player.full_name||player.short_name||'';if(!pname)continue;
          const rows=photos.filter(row=>{const rowTeam=String(row.team_id||'');if(sideTeamId&&rowTeam&&sideTeamId!==rowTeam)return false;return matchPlayerKey(row.name||'')===matchPlayerKey(pname)});
          const found=rows.length===1?rows[0]:rows.find(row=>row.team_id&&sideTeamId&&String(row.team_id)===sideTeamId);
          const url=String(found&&(found.image_url||found.photo||found.logo)||'');
          if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(url)){player.logo=url;player.photo=url;player.image_url=url;player.photoSource='PitchAPI';changed=true;}
        }
      }
      if(changed)renderMatch();
      return photos;
    }catch(_){return null}
    finally{if(cur===my)my.pitchPhotosPromise=null}
  })();
  my.pitchPhotosPromise=task;return task;
}
async function loadPitchLiveAnalytics(my,forceRefresh=false){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=="match"||!my.data||!liveS(my.data.fixture&&my.data.fixture.status&&my.data.fixture.status.short))return null;
  const matchId=String(my.data.pitch&&my.data.pitch.id||String(my.id||"").replace(/^p:/,""));
  if(!/^m_[A-Za-z0-9]{6}$/.test(matchId))return null;
  if(my.pitchAnalyticsPromise)return my.pitchAnalyticsPromise;
  if(!forceRefresh&&Date.now()-Number(my.pitchAnalyticsAt||0)<9000)return my.data.pitch;
  my.pitchAnalyticsAt=Date.now();
  const task=(async()=>{
    try{
      const result=await api('/pitch-match-analytics?id='+encodeURIComponent(matchId));
      if(cur!==my||!my.data)return null;
      const root=result&&result.data&&typeof result.data==='object'?result.data:result||{};
      const analytics=root.analytics&&typeof root.analytics==='object'?root.analytics:root;
      const old=my.data.pitch||{};
      const take=(key)=>analytics[key]&&typeof analytics[key]==='object'&&Object.keys(analytics[key]).length?analytics[key]:(old[key]||null);
      my.data.pitch={...old,live:take('live'),advanced:take('advanced'),network:take('network'),heatmaps:take('heatmaps'),momentum:take('momentum'),shots:take('shots'),analyticsLive:true,analyticsCheckedAt:Date.now(),analyticsErrors:analytics.errors||{}};
      renderMatch();return my.data.pitch;
    }catch(_){return null}
    finally{if(cur===my){my.pitchAnalyticsPromise=null}}
  })();
  my.pitchAnalyticsPromise=task;return task;
}
async function loadPitchMatchEnrichment(my,forceRefresh=false){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='match')return null;
  if(my.pitchLookupPromise)return my.pitchLookupPromise;
  if(my.pitchLookupBusy)return null;
  const currentStatus=String(my.data&&my.data.fixture&&my.data.fixture.status&&my.data.fixture.status.short||'');const liveRefreshWindow=liveS(currentStatus)?15e3:3*60e3;if(!forceRefresh&&my.pitchLookupAt&&Date.now()-my.pitchLookupAt<liveRefreshWindow)return my.data;
  const task=(async()=>{
    my.pitchLookupBusy=true;my.pitchLookupDone=true;my.pitchLookupAt=Date.now();
    try{
      const base=my.data||my.base;if(!base)throw new Error('Maç bilgisi henüz yüklenmedi.');
      const code=matchLeagueCode(base);if(!code)throw new Error('Bu maçın ligi PitchAPI ile eşleştirilemedi.');
      const refresh=forceRefresh?'&refresh=1':'';let j;
      if(String(my.id).startsWith('p:'))j=await api('/pitch-match-detail?id='+encodeURIComponent(String(my.id).slice(2))+'&code='+encodeURIComponent(code)+refresh);
      else{
        const d=new Date(base.fixture.date||'');if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))throw new Error('Maç tarihi eşleştirilemedi.');
        const params=new URLSearchParams({code,date:d.toISOString().slice(0,10),home:base.teams.home.raw||base.teams.home.name,away:base.teams.away.raw||base.teams.away.name,season:String(matchSeasonYear(base))});
        j=await api('/pitch-match-data?'+params.toString()+refresh);
      }
      if(cur!==my)return null;
      if(!my.data){
        my.data=j.match;
        my.data.fixture.id=my.id;
        my.data.pitch=j.pitch;
        my.pitchLoaded=true;
        my.pitchErr='';
        renderMatch();
        loadPitchLivePhotos(my,false);
        if(liveS(my.data.fixture&&my.data.fixture.status&&my.data.fixture.status.short))loadPitchLiveAnalytics(my,false);
        return my.data;
      }
      if(!mergePitchMatchIntoCurrent(my,j))throw new Error('PitchAPI maç verisi eksik döndü.');
      my.pitchErr='';renderMatch();
      loadPitchLivePhotos(my,false);
      if(liveS(my.data.fixture&&my.data.fixture.status&&my.data.fixture.status.short))loadPitchLiveAnalytics(my,false);
      return my.data;
    }catch(e){if(cur===my){my.pitchLookupAt=0;my.pitchLoaded=false;my.pitchErr=e&&e.message||String(e);renderMatch()}return null}
    finally{if(cur===my){my.pitchLookupBusy=false;my.pitchLookupDone=false;my.pitchLookupPromise=null}}
  })();
  my.pitchLookupPromise=task;return task;
}
function matchLineupsConfirmed(m){
  const sides=m&&Array.isArray(m.lineups)?m.lineups:[];
  return sides.length>=2&&sides.every(side=>side&&side.confirmed===true);
}
function matchInPredictedLineupWindow(m){
  const status=m&&m.fixture&&m.fixture.status&&m.fixture.status.short;
  const until=Date.parse(m&&m.fixture&&m.fixture.date||'')-Date.now();
  return NS.includes(status)&&Number.isFinite(until)&&until<=48*3600e3&&until>=-2*3600e3;
}

function matchStatsQualityClient(rows){return Array.isArray(rows)?rows.reduce((n,row)=>n+((row.statistics||[]).filter(x=>x&&x.value!=null&&x.value!=='').length),0):0}
function mergeMatchEventHistory(previousEvents,nextEvents){
  const oldRows=Array.isArray(previousEvents)?previousEvents:[];
  const newRows=Array.isArray(nextEvents)?nextEvents:[];
  const norm=value=>matchPlayerKey(String(value==null?'':value));
  const cleanTeam=value=>norm(value).split(' ').filter(Boolean).filter(token=>!['fc','cf','sc','afc','club','football','calcio'].includes(token)).join(' ');
  const playerInfo=(player,event)=>{
    const p=player&&typeof player==='object'?player:{};
    const rawName=String(p.name||event&&event.player_name||event&&event.player&&event.player.name||'').trim();
    const full=norm(rawName);
    const parts=full.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
    const id=String(p.id||event&&event.player_id||event&&event.playerId||'').trim();
    return {id,name:full,short:parts.length>1?parts[0].slice(0,1)+' '+parts[parts.length-1]:full,surname:parts.at(-1)||'',initial:parts.length>1?parts[0].slice(0,1):''};
  };
  const eventMinute=e=>{
    const tm=e&&e.time&&typeof e.time==='object'?e.time:{};
    const raw=tm.elapsed ?? (e&&e.elapsed) ?? (e&&e.minute) ?? (e&&e.min) ?? 0;
    if(typeof raw==='string'&&/^\s*\d+\s*\+\s*\d+\s*$/.test(raw)){
      const bits=raw.split('+').map(Number);return bits[0]+bits[1];
    }
    const elapsed=Number(raw);
    const extra=Number(tm.extra ?? (e&&e.minute_added) ?? (e&&e.added_time) ?? (e&&e.extra) ?? 0)||0;
    return Number.isFinite(elapsed)?elapsed:0;
  };
  const identity=e=>{
    const tm=e&&e.team&&typeof e.team==='object'?e.team:{};
    const rawType=String(e&&(e.type||e.event_type||e.kind)||'').toLowerCase();
    const type=/goal|penalty/.test(rawType)?'goal':/card|yellow|red/.test(rawType)?'card':/sub/.test(rawType)?'substitution':/var/.test(rawType)?'var':rawType;
    const detail=String(e&&(e.detail||e.description||e.subtype)||'').toLowerCase();
    let kind='';
    if(type==='goal')kind=(e&&(e.is_own_goal||e.own_goal)||/own goal|own-goal/.test(detail))?'own':(e&&(e.is_missed_penalty||e.missed_penalty)||/missed|disallow|cancel|no goal/.test(detail))?'missed':'';
    else if(type==='card')kind=/second.?yellow/.test(detail)?'second-yellow':/red/.test(detail)?'red':'yellow';
    const player=playerInfo(e&&e.player,e);
    const incoming=e&&(e.sub_in_player||e.player_in||e.assist)||{};
    const secondary=type==='substitution'?playerInfo(incoming,{}):{id:'',name:''};
    const teamName=tm.name||tm.short_name||e&&e.team_name||'';
    const teamId=String(tm.id||e&&e.team_id||'');
    const score=e&&e.score||{};
    const scoreHome=score.home ?? (e&&e.score_home) ?? null,scoreAway=score.away ?? (e&&e.score_away) ?? null;
    return {
      eventId:String(e&&(e.eventId||e.event_id||e.id||e.uuid)||''),
      type,kind,team:cleanTeam(teamName),teamId,
      minute:eventMinute(e),player,secondary,
      scoreHome,scoreAway
    };
  };
  const sameTeam=(a,b)=>{
    if(!a.team||!b.team){if(!a.team&&!b.team&&a.teamId&&b.teamId&&a.teamId!==b.teamId)return false;return true;}
    if(a.team===b.team)return true;
    const strip=s=>s.replace(/\b(man utd|man united)\b/g,'manchester united').replace(/\binter milan\b/g,'internazionale').replace(/\batletico\b/g,'atletico madrid');
    const aa=strip(a.team),bb=strip(b.team);
    return aa===bb||(Math.min(aa.length,bb.length)>=9&&(aa.includes(bb)||bb.includes(aa)));
  };
  const samePlayer=(a,b)=>{
    if(a.id&&b.id&&a.id===b.id)return true;
    if(a.name&&b.name){
      if(a.name===b.name||a.short===b.short)return true;
      return !!(a.surname&&a.surname===b.surname&&((a.initial&&b.initial&&a.initial===b.initial)||a.name===a.surname||b.name===b.surname));
    }
    return !a.name||!b.name;
  };
  const mergeOne=(old,e)=>{
    if(!old)return e;
    const merged={...old,...e};
    for(const key of ['team','player','assist','sub_in_player','player_in']){
      if(old[key]||e[key])merged[key]={...(old[key]||{}),...(e[key]||{})};
    }
    const oldTime=old.time&&typeof old.time==='object'?old.time:{};
    const nextTime=e.time&&typeof e.time==='object'?e.time:{};
    merged.time={...oldTime,...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(nextTime).filter(([,v])=>v!=null&&v!==''))};
    const oldScore=old.score&&typeof old.score==='object'?old.score:{};
    const nextScore=e.score&&typeof e.score==='object'?e.score:{};
    merged.score={...oldScore,...Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(nextScore).filter(([,v])=>v!=null&&v!==''))};
    if(!merged.player_name)merged.player_name=old.player_name||e.player_name;
    if(old.detail&&(!e.detail||String(old.detail).length>String(e.detail).length))merged.detail=old.detail;
    if(!merged.eventId)merged.eventId=old.eventId||old.event_id||old.id||'';
    return merged;
  };
  const rows=[],meta=[];
  const add=e=>{
    if(!e||typeof e!=='object')return;
    const id=identity(e);
    let candidates=[];
    for(let i=0;i<meta.length;i++){
      const prior=meta[i];
      if(id.eventId&&prior.eventId&&id.eventId===prior.eventId&&id.type===prior.type){candidates.push(i);continue;}
      if(prior.type!==id.type||prior.kind!==id.kind||!sameTeam(prior,id)||Math.abs(prior.minute-id.minute)>1)continue;
      if(id.type==='substitution'){
        if(prior.secondary.name&&id.secondary.name&&!samePlayer(prior.secondary,id.secondary))continue;
        const priorActor=prior.player.id||prior.player.name||prior.secondary.id||prior.secondary.name;
        const nextActor=id.player.id||id.player.name||id.secondary.id||id.secondary.name;
        // Without a player identity, same-minute substitutions cannot safely be
        // collapsed: more than one change can happen at the same minute.
        if(!priorActor||!nextActor)continue;
        if(prior.player.id&&id.player.id&&prior.player.id!==id.player.id)continue;
        if(prior.player.name&&id.player.name&&!samePlayer(prior.player,id.player))continue;
      }else if(['goal','card'].includes(id.type)){
        // Do not collapse two different (or unidentified) scorers/cards merely
        // because they share a minute and team. Provider event IDs still merge above.
        if(!prior.player.id&&!prior.player.name||!id.player.id&&!id.player.name)continue;
        if(!samePlayer(prior.player,id.player))continue;
      }else{
        if(prior.player.id&&id.player.id&&prior.player.id!==id.player.id)continue;
        if(prior.player.name&&id.player.name&&!samePlayer(prior.player,id.player))continue;
      }
      // The running score is metadata, not event identity: providers often report a
      // different snapshot score for the same historical goal.
      candidates.push(i);
    }
    if(candidates.length===1){
      const index=candidates[0];
      rows[index]=mergeOne(rows[index],e);
      meta[index]=identity(rows[index]);
    }else{
      rows.push(e);meta.push(id);
    }
  };
  for(const e of oldRows)add(e);
  for(const e of newRows)add(e);
  return rows.sort((a,b)=>eventMinute(a)-eventMinute(b));
}
function mergeMatchSnapshot(previous,incoming){
  if(!previous||!incoming)return incoming||previous;
  const oldTeams=previous.teams||{},newTeams=incoming.teams||{};
  const sameTeamName=(a,b)=>!a||!b||pitchSameSide(a,b);
  const sameMatch=sameTeamName(oldTeams.home,newTeams.home)&&sameTeamName(oldTeams.away,newTeams.away);
  if(!sameMatch&&previous.pitchSource!=='PitchAPI'&&previous.lineupsSource!=='PitchAPI')return incoming;
  const oldSides=Array.isArray(previous.lineups)?previous.lineups:[],newSides=Array.isArray(incoming.lineups)?incoming.lineups:[];
  const mergeRows=(rows,oldRows)=> (rows||[]).map(entry=>{
    const player=entry.player||entry,priorEntry=(oldRows||[]).find(x=>matchPlayerIdentityEqual(x.player||x,player));
    if(!priorEntry)return entry;
    return {...entry,player:mergeMatchPlayerData(priorEntry.player||priorEntry,player)};
  });
  const mergeSide=(nextSide,oldSide)=>{
    if(!nextSide)return oldSide;
    const nextCount=(nextSide.startXI||[]).length,oldCount=(oldSide&&oldSide.startXI||[]).length;
    if(!nextCount&&oldCount)return oldSide;
    const oldRows=[...(oldSide&&oldSide.startXI||[]),...(oldSide&&oldSide.substitutes||[])];
    return {...(oldSide||{}),...nextSide,
      startXI:mergeRows(nextSide.startXI,oldRows),
      substitutes:mergeRows(nextSide.substitutes,oldRows)};
  };
  if(newSides.length){incoming.lineups=newSides.map((side,index)=>{
    const oldSide=oldSides.find(x=>matchPlayerKey(x.team&&x.team.name)===matchPlayerKey(side.team&&side.team.name))||oldSides[index];
    return mergeSide(side,oldSide);
  });}
  if(!incoming.lineups||!incoming.lineups.some(x=>(x.startXI||[]).length)&&oldSides.some(x=>(x.startXI||[]).length))incoming.lineups=oldSides;
  if(matchStatsQualityClient(incoming.statistics)<matchStatsQualityClient(previous.statistics)){incoming.statistics=previous.statistics;incoming.statisticsSource=previous.statisticsSource||'Önceki maç verisi'}
  if((previous.events||[]).length||(incoming.events||[]).length){incoming.events=mergeMatchEventHistory(previous.events,incoming.events);if(previous.eventsSource&&!incoming.eventsSource)incoming.eventsSource=previous.eventsSource}
  const oldPitch=previous.pitch||{},newPitch=incoming.pitch||{};
  const mergedPitch={...oldPitch,...newPitch};
  if(Array.isArray(newPitch.players)&&newPitch.players.length)mergedPitch.players=mergePitchPlayerRows(oldPitch.players,newPitch.players);
  for(const key of ['lineups','stats','events'])if((!Array.isArray(newPitch[key])||!newPitch[key].length)&&Array.isArray(oldPitch[key])&&oldPitch[key].length)mergedPitch[key]=oldPitch[key];
  for(const key of ['heatmaps','advanced','network','momentum','shots','h2h'])if((newPitch[key]==null||newPitch[key]==='')&&oldPitch[key]!=null)mergedPitch[key]=oldPitch[key];
  if(Object.keys(mergedPitch).length)incoming.pitch=mergedPitch;
  if((incoming.lineups||[]).some(x=>(x.startXI||[]).length)&&previous.lineupsSource==='PitchAPI')incoming.lineupsSource='PitchAPI';
  return incoming;
}

function matchDetailUrl(my,fast){
  const base=my.data||my.base;
  const suffix=base?'&home='+encodeURIComponent(base.teams.home.raw||base.teams.home.name)+'&away='+encodeURIComponent(base.teams.away.raw||base.teams.away.name)+'&date='+encodeURIComponent(base.fixture.date):'';
  return '/match?id='+encodeURIComponent(my.id)+suffix+(fast?'&fast=1':'');
}
function loadPitchMatchEvents(my,force=false){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='match'||!String(my.id).startsWith('p:')||my.pitchEventsLoading||my.pitchEventsLoaded&&!force)return;
  my.pitchEventsLoading=true;
  const pitchId=String(my.id).slice(2),params=new URLSearchParams({id:pitchId});
  api('/pitch-match-events?'+params.toString(),10000).then(j=>{
    if(cur!==my||!my.data||!j)return;
    const events=Array.isArray(j.events)?j.events:[];
    if(events.length){my.data.events=mergeMatchEventHistory(my.data.events||[],events);my.data.pitch={...(my.data.pitch||{}),events};}
    my.pitchEventsLoaded=true;my.pitchEventsError='';renderMatch();
  }).catch(error=>{if(cur===my)my.pitchEventsError=String(error&&error.message||error).slice(0,120);}).finally(()=>{my.pitchEventsLoading=false;});
}
function loadFullMatchEnrichment(my){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='match'||my.detailEnrichmentStarted||!/^(?:e:|a:)/.test(String(my.id)))return;
  my.detailEnrichmentStarted=true;
  api(matchDetailUrl(my,false)).then(j=>{
    if(cur!==my||!j||!j.match)return;
    my.data=mergeMatchSnapshot(my.data,j.match);my.detailEnriched=true;
    // The fast response has already painted score/clock/events; optional stats/lineup data
    // upgrades the same view without blocking the first render.
    renderMatch();
  }).catch(()=>{});
}
async function loadMatch(){
  const my=cur;if(!my||my.kind!=='match')return;
  try{
    if(String(my.id).startsWith('p:')){
      // Paint the fixture-card snapshot immediately. Fetch only summary+events in the
      // foreground; the full XI/photos/analytics lookup already runs in the background.
      const pitchId=String(my.id).slice(2);
      const code=matchLeagueCode(my.data||my.base||{});
      const params=new URLSearchParams({id:pitchId});if(code)params.set('code',code);
      try{
        const quick=await api('/pitch-match-fast?'+params.toString(),9000);
        if(cur!==my)return;
        if(quick&&quick.match){
          my.data=mergeMatchSnapshot(my.data||my.base,quick.match);
          if(my.data&&my.data.fixture)my.data.fixture.id=my.id;
          my.err='';
          renderMatch();
        }
      }catch(error){
        // Keep the card's score/clock visible even if the provider fast endpoint fails.
        my.err='';
      }
      if(cur!==my)return;
      my.detailLoaded=!!my.data;my.detailLoading=false;
      if(!Array.isArray(my.data&&my.data.events)||!my.data.events.length)loadPitchMatchEvents(my,false);
      if(!my.pitchLookupPromise&&!my.pitchLoaded)loadPitchMatchEnrichment(my,false);
    }else{
      const j=await api(matchDetailUrl(my,true),9000);
      if(cur!==my)return;
      if(j&&j.match)my.data=mergeMatchSnapshot(my.data,j.match);
      my.err='';my.detailLoaded=!!my.data;my.detailLoading=false;
      renderMatch();
      // Request the slower enriched response separately; never make the initial match view wait for it.
      loadFullMatchEnrichment(my);
      const until=Date.parse((my.data||my.base).fixture.date)-Date.now();
      const liveNeedsRefresh=liveS(my.data.fixture.status.short)&&Date.now()-Number(my.pitchLookupAt||0)>=15e3;
      if((!my.pitchLoaded&&(!my.pitchLookupAt||Date.now()-Number(my.pitchLookupAt)>=12*60e3))||(DONE.includes(my.data.fixture.status.short)&&!(my.data.pitch&&my.data.pitch.analyticsChecked))||(matchInPredictedLineupWindow(my.data)&&!matchLineupsConfirmed(my.data)&&Date.now()-Number(my.pitchLookupAt||0)>=5*60e3)||liveNeedsRefresh)loadPitchMatchEnrichment(my,liveNeedsRefresh||DONE.includes(my.data.fixture.status.short)&&!(my.data.pitch&&my.data.pitch.analyticsChecked));
    }
    if(cur!==my||!my.data)return;
    my.err='';
    const st=my.data.fixture.status;
    if(DONE.includes(st.short)){
      rememberFinalMatch(my.data);
      const listed=state.data.find(x=>String(x.fixture.id)===String(my.id));
      if(listed){listed.fixture.status={...(listed.fixture.status||{}),short:st.short,elapsed:null};listed.goals=mergeMatchGoals(my.data.goals,listed.goals)}
    }else if(liveS(st.short)){setLiveMinute(my.id,st.elapsed,st.short);loadPitchLiveAnalytics(my,false)}
    loadPitchLivePhotos(my,false);
    render();
  }catch(e){if(cur!==my)return;my.err=e.message||String(e);my.detailLoaded=!!my.data;my.detailLoading=false}
  renderMatch();clearTimeout(timer);
  if(cur===my&&my.data){
    const m=my.data,st=m.fixture.status,short=st.short;
    if(liveS(short))timer=setTimeout(loadMatch,10000);
    else if(NS.includes(short)&&my.tab==='iddaa'){
      const until=Date.parse(m.fixture.date)-Date.now();
      const delay=Number.isFinite(until)&&until>5*60e3?30000:7000;
      timer=setTimeout(loadMatch,delay);
    }
    else if(NS.includes(short)&&(!matchLineupsConfirmed(m)||String(my.id).startsWith('p:'))){
      const until=Date.parse(m.fixture.date)-Date.now();
      const staleAfterKickoff=String(my.id).startsWith('p:')&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(m.fixture.date))&&Date.now()>Date.parse(m.fixture.date)+2.25*3600e3;
      const delay=staleAfterKickoff?5*60e3:until>48*3600e3?Math.min(until-48*3600e3,24*3600e3):until>6*3600e3?30*60e3:until>60*60e3?10*60e3:until>=-2*3600e3?5*60e3:0;
      if(delay)timer=setTimeout(()=>{if(cur===my){if(matchInPredictedLineupWindow(my.data)||staleAfterKickoff)loadPitchMatchEnrichment(my,true);else loadMatch()}},delay);
    }
  }
}

function stText(s,date,id){
  if(HT.includes(s.short))return 'Devre Arası';
  if(LIVE.includes(s.short)){const e=id?minOf(id,s.elapsed):s.elapsed;return (e==null||e===''?'':e+"' · ")+'Canlı'}
  if(DONE.includes(s.short))return s.short==='FT'?'Maç Sonu':s.short;
  if(NS.includes(s.short))return new Date(date).toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  return ({PST:'Ertelendi',CANC:'İptal',SUSP:'Durdu',ABD:'Yarıda'})[s.short]||s.short;
}
function evIcon(e){
  const detail=String(e&&e.detail||'').toLowerCase(),rawType=String(e&&(e.type||e.event_type)||'').toLowerCase();
  const type=/goal|penalty/.test(rawType)?'goal':/substitution|subst/.test(rawType)?'subst':/yellow|red|card/.test(rawType+' '+detail)?'card':rawType;
  if(type==='goal')return `<svg class="sh-event-svg sh-event-ball" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="9" fill="#fff" stroke="#d9e3dc" stroke-width="1.2"/><path d="m12 7 3.2 2.3-1.2 3.8h-4L8.8 9.3zM12 7l-2-2M15.2 9.3l3.8-1M14 13.1l2.4 3.1M10 13.1l-2.4 3.1M8.8 9.3 5 8.3" fill="#263b2c" stroke="#263b2c" stroke-width="1.1" stroke-linejoin="round"/><path d="m12 7 3.2 2.3-1.2 3.8h-4L8.8 9.3z" fill="#263b2c"/></svg>`;
  if(type==='card')return `<span class="sh-event-card ${/red|second/.test(rawType+' '+detail)?'red':'yellow'}" aria-label="${/red|second/.test(rawType+' '+detail)?'Kırmızı kart':'Sarı kart'}"></span>`;
  if(type==='subst')return `<svg class="sh-event-svg sh-event-sub" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 7h14l-3-3m3 3-3 3M20 17H6l3 3m-3-3 3-3" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>`;
  if(type==='var')return `<svg class="sh-event-svg sh-event-var" viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><rect x="3" y="5" width="18" height="13" rx="2" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.8"/><path d="M8 21h8m-4-3v3M10 9l5 2.5-5 2.5z" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg>`;
  return '<span class="sh-event-dot"></span>';
}

function evHTML(m){
  const E=mergeMatchEventHistory([],m.events||[]),home=m.teams&&m.teams.home||{},away=m.teams&&m.teams.away||{};
  const eventMinute=e=>{const t=e&&e.time||{},raw=t.elapsed??(e&&e.elapsed)??(e&&e.minute)??null;const n=typeof raw==='string'?Number(String(raw).split('+')[0]):Number(raw);return Number.isFinite(n)?Math.max(0,Math.min(120,n)):null};
  const eventMinuteLabel=e=>{const t=e&&e.time||{},raw=t.elapsed??(e&&e.elapsed)??(e&&e.minute)??null,extra=Number(t.extra??(e&&e.minute_added)??(e&&e.added_time)??0)||0;if(typeof raw==='string'&&/^\s*\d+\s*\+\s*\d+\s*$/.test(raw))return String(raw).replace(/\s/g,'');const base=eventMinute(e);return base==null?'—':String(base)+(extra>0?'+'+extra:'')};
  const eventType=e=>{const t=[e&&e.type,e&&e.event_type,e&&e.detail,e&&e.description,e&&e.comments].filter(Boolean).join(' ').toLowerCase();return /substitution|subst/.test(t)?'subst':/yellow|red|card/.test(t)?'card':/goal|penalty/.test(t)?'goal':/var/.test(t)?'var':t};
  const eventSide=e=>{const team=e&&e.team||{};if(team.id!=null&&away.id!=null&&String(team.id)===String(away.id))return 'away';if(team.id!=null&&home.id!=null&&String(team.id)===String(home.id))return 'home';const name=String(team.name||'');if(name&&pitchTeamNameMatches(name,away.name||''))return 'away';if(name&&pitchTeamNameMatches(name,home.name||''))return 'home';return 'home'};
  const photoMap=new Map();
  for(const row of matchPlayerSource(m)){const p=row&& (row.player||row.person)||row||{},name=p.name||p.full_name||row&&row.player_name||'',photo=p.image_url||p.photo||p.logo||row&&row.image_url||'';if(name&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(String(photo)))photoMap.set(playerNameKey(name),photo)}
  for(const side of (m.lineups||[]))for(const item of [...(side.startXI||[]),...(side.substitutes||[])]){const p=item&& (item.player||item)||{},name=p.name||p.full_name||'',photo=p.image_url||p.photo||p.logo||'';if(name&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(String(photo))&&!photoMap.has(playerNameKey(name)))photoMap.set(playerNameKey(name),photo)}
  const actor=(value)=>value&&typeof value==='object'?value:{name:typeof value==='string'?value:''};
  const actorName=p=>p&&(p.name||p.full_name||p.short_name||p.player_name)||'';
  const actorPhoto=p=>{const own=p&&(p.image_url||p.photo||p.logo)||'';return /^https?:\/\//i.test(String(own))?own:photoMap.get(playerNameKey(actorName(p)))||''};
  const pitchCode=matchLeagueCode(m);
  const eventDetailTR=value=>{const raw=String(value||'').trim(),key=raw.toLowerCase().replace(/[_-]+/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ');if(!key)return '';if(/second yellow|2nd yellow/.test(key))return 'İkinci Sarı Kart';if(/red card|straight red|kirmizi kart/.test(key))return 'Kırmızı Kart';if(/yellow card|sarı kart|yellow/.test(key))return 'Sarı Kart';if(/substitution|substitute/.test(key))return 'Oyuncu Değişikliği';if(/own goal/.test(key))return 'Kendi Kalesine Gol';if(/penalty/.test(key))return 'Penaltı Golü';if(/normal goal|regular goal|goal/.test(key))return 'Gol';if(/var/.test(key))return 'VAR İncelemesi';return raw};
  const marker=(e)=>{const minute=eventMinute(e);if(minute==null)return '';const t=eventType(e),side=eventSide(e),icon=evIcon(e);return `<span class="match-time-marker ${t==='goal'?'goal':t==='card'?'card':t==='subst'?'sub':'other'}" style="left:${Math.min(100,minute/90*100)}%" title="${eventMinuteLabel(e)}' · ${esc(actorName(actor(e.player))||'Maç olayı')}">${icon}</span>`};
  const ticks=Array.from({length:10},(_,i)=>`<span class="match-flow-tick" style="left:${i*10}%"><i></i><b>${i*10}</b></span>`).join('');
  const homeMarkers=E.filter(e=>eventSide(e)==='home').map(marker).join(''),awayMarkers=E.filter(e=>eventSide(e)==='away').map(marker).join('');
  const timeline=`<section class="match-reference-scoreflow"><div class="match-flow-side home"><div class="match-flow-team">${img(home.logo)}<b>${esc(home.name||'Ev Sahibi')}</b></div><div class="match-flow-lane">${homeMarkers}</div></div><div class="match-flow-axis">${ticks}</div><div class="match-flow-side away"><div class="match-flow-lane">${awayMarkers}</div><div class="match-flow-team">${img(away.logo)}<b>${esc(away.name||'Deplasman')}</b></div></div><div class="match-flow-key"><span><i class="key-ball"></i> Gol</span><span><i class="key-card"></i> Kart</span><span><i class="key-sub">↔</i> Oyuncu değişikliği</span></div></section>`;
  const eventPhoto=(p,name)=>{const url=actorPhoto(p);return /^https?:\/\//i.test(String(url))?avatar(url,name):`<span class="match-event-avatar" aria-hidden="true">${esc((trPlayerName(name||'').trim().split(/\s+/).map(x=>x[0]).slice(0,2).join('')||'•').toLocaleUpperCase('tr-TR'))}</span>`};
  const link=(name,team,pos,extraClass='')=>name?`<span class="p-link ${extraClass}" data-player-profile="${esc(name)}" data-player-team="${esc(team||'')}" data-player-pos="${esc(pos||'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(pitchCode)}">${esc(trPlayerName(name))}</span>`:'';
  if(!E.length)return timeline+'<section class="match-reference-events"><h3>Maç Olayları</h3><div class="empty">'+(NS.includes(m.fixture.status.short)?'Maç henüz başlamadı.':'Bu maç için olay bilgisi yok.')+'</div></section>';
  const ordered=E.map((e,i)=>({e,i,minute:eventMinute(e),minuteLabel:eventMinuteLabel(e),side:eventSide(e)})).sort((a,b)=>(a.minute??999)-(b.minute??999)||a.i-b.i);
  let runningScore={home:0,away:0};
  const finalHomeRaw=m.goals&&m.goals.home,finalAwayRaw=m.goals&&m.goals.away,finalHome=Number(finalHomeRaw),finalAway=Number(finalAwayRaw);
  const validFinal=finalHomeRaw!=null&&finalAwayRaw!=null&&finalHomeRaw!==''&&finalAwayRaw!==''&&Number.isFinite(finalHome)&&Number.isFinite(finalAway)&&finalHome>=0&&finalAway>=0;
  const isFailedGoalEvent=e=>/miss|saved|save|failed|disallow|cancel|void|no goal|penalty awarded|awarded penalty|penalty not scored|missed penalty|penalty saved|goal ruled out|ruled out|overturned/i.test(String([e&&e.detail,e&&e.comments,e&&e.description,e&&e.type,e&&e.event_type].filter(Boolean).join(' ')));
  const goalItems=ordered.filter(item=>eventType(item.e)==='goal'&&!isFailedGoalEvent(item.e));
  const resolvedSides=new Map();let knownHome=0,knownAway=0;
  for(const item of goalItems){const e=item.e,team=e&&e.team||{},actorObj=e&&(e.player||e.scorer)||{};let side='';
    const ids=[team.id,e&&e.team_id,e&&e.teamId,actorObj.team_id,actorObj.teamId,actorObj.team&&actorObj.team.id].filter(v=>v!=null).map(String);
    if(ids.some(id=>home.id!=null&&id===String(home.id)))side='home';else if(ids.some(id=>away.id!=null&&id===String(away.id)))side='away';
    const names=[team.name,e&&e.team_name,actorObj.team_name,actorObj.teamName,actorObj.team&&actorObj.team.name].filter(Boolean).map(String);
    if(!side&&names.some(n=>pitchTeamNameMatches(n,home.name||'')))side='home';if(!side&&names.some(n=>pitchTeamNameMatches(n,away.name||'')))side='away';
    if(!side&&item.side&&item.side!=='home')side=item.side;
    const own=/own goal|own-goal|own_goal|kendi kalesine gol/i.test(String([e.detail,e.comments,e.description,e.type,e.event_type].filter(Boolean).join(' ')));if(own&&side)side=side==='home'?'away':'home';
    if(side){resolvedSides.set(item,side);if(side==='home')knownHome++;else knownAway++;}
  }
  let remainingHome=validFinal?Math.max(0,finalHome-knownHome):0,remainingAway=validFinal?Math.max(0,finalAway-knownAway):0;
  for(const item of goalItems){
    // Do not trust provider score snapshots embedded in events: some feeds repeat the
    // pre-goal score (e.g. 0-0 on a goal that makes it 2-1). Rebuild the score in order.
    let side=resolvedSides.get(item)||'';
    if(!side&&validFinal){
      if(remainingHome>0&&(remainingHome>=remainingAway||remainingAway===0)){side='home';remainingHome--;}
      else if(remainingAway>0){side='away';remainingAway--;}
    }
    if(!side)side=item.side==='away'?'away':'home';
    item.resolvedSide=side;
    runningScore[side]++;
    item.scoreText=runningScore.home+'–'+runningScore.away;
  }
  let currentHalf='';
  const rows=ordered.map(item=>{
    const e=item.e,t=eventType(e),side=item.resolvedSide||item.side,team=side==='away'?away.name:home.name;
    const minute=item.minuteLabel||'—';
    const p=actor(e.player),assist=actor(e.assist),incoming=actor(e.sub_in_player||e.player_in||e.substitute||e.in_player||e.assist);
    const name=actorName(p),assistName=actorName(assist),incomingName=actorName(incoming);
    let title='',sub='',displayActor=p,displayName=name;
    if(t==='goal'){title=link(name,team,p.position,'event-player-white');sub=assistName?`Asist · ${link(assistName,team,assist.position,'event-player-white')}`:'';if((e.detail||'').toLowerCase().includes('missed'))sub='Kaçan penaltı';}
    else if(t==='subst'){displayActor=incomingName?incoming:p;displayName=actorName(displayActor);title=link(displayName,team,displayActor.position,'event-sub-in')+`<small class="event-sub-out">↓ ${link(name,team,p.position,'event-sub-out-link')}</small>`;sub='Oyuncu değişikliği';}
    else {const detail=eventDetailTR(e.detail||e.comments||e.type||e.event_type||'Maç olayı');title=link(name,team,p.position,t==='card'?'event-player-white':'')||esc(detail);sub=t==='card'?esc(detail):esc(eventDetailTR(e.detail||e.comments||''));}
    const photo=eventPhoto(displayActor,displayName),half=item.minute!=null&&item.minute>45?'İkinci Yarı':'İlk Yarı';
    let separator='';if(half!==currentHalf){currentHalf=half;separator=`<div class="match-half-divider"><span>${half}</span></div>`;}
    const eventMarker=evIcon(e),scoreBadge=t==='goal'&&item.scoreText&&!isFailedGoalEvent(e)?`<strong class="match-goal-score">${esc(item.scoreText)}</strong>`:'';
    return separator+`<div class="match-event-row ${side} ${t}"><span class="match-event-minute">${minute}'</span><span class="match-event-icon">${eventMarker}</span>${photo}<div class="match-event-copy"><b>${title||'—'}${scoreBadge}</b>${sub?`<small>${sub}</small>`:''}</div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  return timeline+`<section class="match-reference-events"><div class="match-events-heading"><h3>Maç Olayları</h3><div><b>${esc(home.name||'')}</b><span>·</span><b>${esc(away.name||'')}</b></div></div>${rows}</section>`;
}

const plRow=p=>`<div class="pl"><span class="no">${p.number==null?'':p.number}</span><span>${esc(trPlayerName(p.name))}</span><span class="pos">${esc(posTR(p.pos))}</span></div>`;
const nk=n=>String(n||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();
// Turkish player-name normalization: repair ASCII transliterations and split compound given names.
const playerNameKey=n=>String(n||'').toLocaleLowerCase('tr-TR').replace(/ı/g,'i').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();
const TR_PLAYER_WORDS={
  bardakci:'Bardakçı',muldur:'Müldür',yokuslu:'Yokuşlu',elmali:'Elmalı',zeki:'Zeki',celik:'Çelik',akturkoglu:'Aktürkoğlu',caglar:'Çağlar',caglayan:'Çağlayan',cagri:'Çağrı',ozcan:'Özcan',ozdemir:'Özdemir',ozkacar:'Özkacar',ozkan:'Özkan',ozgur:'Özgür',ozkaya:'Özkaya',ozel:'Özel',ozil:'Özil',koc:'Koç',celik:'Çelik',sahin:'Şahin',yilmaz:'Yılmaz',yildiz:'Yıldız',guler:'Güler',gunes:'Güneş',unal:'Ünal',under:'Ünder',kokcu:'Kökçü',calhanoglu:'Çalhanoğlu',kadioglu:'Kadıoğlu',soyuncu:'Söyüncü',demiral:'Demiral',bayindir:'Bayındır',cakir:'Çakır',yuksek:'Yüksek',kilicsoy:'Kılıçsoy',kilic:'Kılıç',omur:'Ömür',yazici:'Yazıcı',ugur:'Uğur',ugurcan:'Uğurcan',ismail:'İsmail',irfan:'İrfan',ibrahim:'İbrahim',omer:'Ömer',oguz:'Oğuz',oguzhan:'Oğuzhan',cenk:'Cenk',baris:'Barış',enes:'Enes',arda:'Arda',kerem:'Kerem',abdulkerim:'Abdülkerim',ferdi:'Ferdi',orkun:'Orkun',salih:'Salih',mert:'Mert',semih:'Semih',kaan:'Kaan',muci:'Muçi',topcu:'Topçu',aydin:'Aydın',akaydin:'Akaydın',gokhan:'Gökhan',gokce:'Gökçe',yagiz:'Yağız',yigit:'Yiğit',ilkay:'İlkay',ilker:'İlker',ilhan:'İlhan',isik:'Işık',unlu:'Ünlü',genc:'Genç',gok:'Gök',gokturk:'Göktürk',goksel:'Göksel',cetin:'Çetin',cicek:'Çiçek',cimen:'Çimen',sener:'Şener',sule:'Şule',tunc:'Tunç',tufan:'Tufan',uzun:'Uzun',oz:'Öz'
};
const TR_PLAYER_JOIN={'emir han':'Emirhan','oguz han':'Oğuzhan','abdul kerim':'Abdülkerim'};
const TR_PLAYER_FULL={'emir han':'Emirhan','emir han topcu':'Emirhan Topçu','emirhan topcu':'Emirhan Topçu','bardakci':'Bardakçı','abdulkerim bardakci':'Abdülkerim Bardakçı','kerem akturkoglu':'Kerem Aktürkoğlu','emirhan':'Emirhan','baris alper yilmaz':'Barış Alper Yılmaz','ferdi kadioglu':'Ferdi Kadıoğlu','caglar soyuncu':'Çağlar Söyüncü','hakan calhanoglu':'Hakan Çalhanoğlu','orkun kokcu':'Orkun Kökçü','ugurcan cakir':'Uğurcan Çakır','ismail yuksek':'İsmail Yüksek','irfan can kahveci':'İrfan Can Kahveci','enes unal':'Enes Ünal','yusuf yazici':'Yusuf Yazıcı','semih kilicsoy':'Semih Kılıçsoy','arda guler':'Arda Güler','salih ozcan':'Salih Özcan','can uzun':'Can Uzun','kaan ayhan':'Kaan Ayhan','merih demiral':'Merih Demiral','ozan kabak':'Ozan Kabak'};
function trPlayerName(value){
  const raw=String(value==null?'':value).replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(!raw)return raw;
  const key=playerNameKey(raw);if(TR_PLAYER_FULL[key])return TR_PLAYER_FULL[key];
  const words=raw.split(' '),out=[];
  for(let i=0;i<words.length;){
    if(i+1<words.length){const pair=playerNameKey(words[i]+' '+words[i+1]);if(TR_PLAYER_JOIN[pair]){out.push(TR_PLAYER_JOIN[pair]);i+=2;continue}}
    const k=playerNameKey(words[i]);out.push(TR_PLAYER_WORDS[k]||words[i]);i++;
  }
  return out.join(' ');
}
const posGrp=p=>{
  const x=String(p||'').toUpperCase().trim().replace(/[._-]+/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
  if(x==='K'||/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/.test(x)||/GOALKEEPER|GOALIE/.test(x))return 0;
