  // Midfield must be checked before defender words: DM starts with D but is a midfielder.
  if(/^(OS|M|MF|MID|MIDFIELDER|DM|CDM|RDM|LDM|CM|LCM|RCM|AM|CAM|RAM|LAM|LM|RM)$/.test(x)||/MIDFIELD|MIDFIELDER|DEFENSIVE MIDFIELDER|HALF BACK/.test(x))return 2;
  if(/^(D|DF|DEF|DEFENDER|CB|LCB|RCB|LB|RB|LWB|RWB|WB|SW)$/.test(x)||/WING.?BACK|CENTRE BACK|CENTER BACK|CENTRAL DEFENDER|DEFENDER|DEFENCE|DEFENSE|BACK$|SWEEPER/.test(x))return 1;
  if(/^(F|FW|FWD|LW|RW|LWF|RWF|ST|CF|RCF|LCF|SS|FORWARD|STRIKER|ATTACKER)$/.test(x)||/WINGER|FORWARD|STRIKER|ATTACKER/.test(x))return 3;
  return -1;
};
const XI_ROLE_TEMPLATES={
      '4-2-3-1':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LDM','RDM'],['LW','CAM','RW'],['ST']],
      '4-3-3':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LCM','CM','RCM'],['LW','ST','RW']],
      '4-4-2':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LM','LCM','RCM','RM'],['LCF','RCF']],
      '4-1-4-1':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['CDM'],['LM','LCM','RCM','RM'],['ST']],
      '4-4-1-1':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LM','LCM','RCM','RM'],['CAM'],['ST']],
      '4-2-2-2':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LDM','RDM'],['LAM','RAM'],['LCF','RCF']],
      '4-3-1-2':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LCM','CM','RCM'],['CAM'],['LCF','RCF']],
      '4-3-2-1':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LCM','CM','RCM'],['LAM','RAM'],['ST']],
      '4-5-1':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LM','LCM','CM','RCM','RM'],['ST']],
      '4-1-2-1-2':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['CDM'],['LCM','RCM'],['CAM'],['LCF','RCF']],
      '5-3-2':[['LWB','LCB','CB','RCB','RWB'],['LCM','CM','RCM'],['LCF','RCF']],
      '5-4-1':[['LWB','LCB','CB','RCB','RWB'],['LM','LCM','RCM','RM'],['ST']],
      '5-2-3':[['LWB','LCB','CB','RCB','RWB'],['LDM','RDM'],['LW','ST','RW']],
      '3-5-2':[['LCB','CB','RCB'],['LM','LCM','CM','RCM','RM'],['LCF','RCF']],
      '3-4-3':[['LCB','CB','RCB'],['LM','LCM','RCM','RM'],['LW','ST','RW']],
      '3-4-2-1':[['LCB','CB','RCB'],['LWB','LCM','RCM','RWB'],['LAM','RAM'],['ST']],
      '3-4-1-2':[['LCB','CB','RCB'],['LM','LCM','RCM','RM'],['CAM'],['LCF','RCF']],
      '3-2-4-1':[['LCB','CB','RCB'],['LDM','RDM'],['LM','LAM','RAM','RM'],['ST']],
      '3-1-4-2':[['LCB','CB','RCB'],['CDM'],['LM','LCM','RCM','RM'],['LCF','RCF']],
      '4-2-1-3':[['LB','LCB','RCB','RB'],['LDM','RDM'],['CAM'],['LW','ST','RW']]
    };
function detectPitchDepthAxis(players,formation,explicitKeeper){
  const list=(players||[]).filter(Boolean);
  const val=(p,axis)=>{
    const raw=Number(axis==='x'?p.pitchX:p.pitchY);
    if(!Number.isFinite(raw))return null;
    const n=Math.abs(raw)>1.5?raw/100:raw;
    return n>=0&&n<=1?n:null;
  };
  const wanted=String(formation||'').match(/\d+/g)?.map(Number).filter(n=>n>0)||[];
  const same=(a,b)=>a.length===b.length&&a.every((n,i)=>n===b[i]);
  const scoreAxis=axis=>{
    const points=list.map(p=>({p,v:val(p,axis)})).filter(x=>x.v!=null).sort((a,b)=>a.v-b.v);
    if(points.length!==11)return {axis,score:-1,keeper:null,reverse:false,formationMatch:false};
    const clusters=[];
    for(const point of points){
      const last=clusters[clusters.length-1];
      if(!last||point.v-last[last.length-1].v>.075)clusters.push([point]);
      else last.push(point);
    }
    const sizes=clusters.map(row=>row.length);
    // PitchAPI formation string counts outfield lines only. A goalkeeper is an
    // additional singleton at one end: e.g. 1-4-2-3-1 for a 4-2-3-1.
    const lowKeeperMatch=wanted.length&&wanted.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)===10&&same(sizes.slice(1),wanted);
    const highKeeperMatch=wanted.length&&wanted.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)===10&&same(sizes.slice(0,-1).reverse(),wanted);
    let candidate=null;
    if(lowKeeperMatch)candidate={p:points[0].p,edge:points[0].v,gap:points[1].v-points[0].v,reverse:false};
    else if(highKeeperMatch)candidate={p:points[10].p,edge:1-points[10].v,gap:points[10].v-points[9].v,reverse:true};
    else{
      const low=points[0],next=points[1],prev=points[9],high=points[10];
      const lowCandidate=low.v<=.22?{p:low.p,edge:low.v,gap:next.v-low.v,reverse:false}:null;
      const highCandidate=high.v>=.78?{p:high.p,edge:1-high.v,gap:high.v-prev.v,reverse:true}:null;
      if(lowCandidate&&highCandidate)candidate=lowCandidate.gap>=highCandidate.gap?lowCandidate:highCandidate;
      else candidate=lowCandidate||highCandidate;
    }
    const formationMatch=!!(lowKeeperMatch||highKeeperMatch);
    const edgeScore=candidate?Math.max(0,1-candidate.edge*3)*.2+Math.min(.2,candidate.gap):0;
    const score=(formationMatch?5:0)+edgeScore+(sizes.length>=3&&sizes.length<=6?.05:0);
    return {axis,score,keeper:candidate&&candidate.p||null,reverse:!!(candidate&&candidate.reverse),formationMatch,sizes};
  };
  const sx=scoreAxis('x'),sy=scoreAxis('y');
  const explicit=explicitKeeper||list.find(p=>/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/i.test(String(p.pos||'')));
  let chosen;
  if(sx.formationMatch!==sy.formationMatch)chosen=sx.formationMatch?sx:sy;
  else if(explicit){
    const ex=val(explicit,'x'),ey=val(explicit,'y');
    if(ex==null)chosen=sy;
    else if(ey==null)chosen=sx;
    else chosen=Math.min(ex,1-ex)<Math.min(ey,1-ey)?sx:sy;
  }else chosen=sx.score>sy.score?sx:sy;
  return {axis:chosen.axis||'y',keeper:explicit||chosen.keeper||null,reverse:chosen.reverse,formationMatch:chosen.formationMatch};
}
function xiRows(t,home=true){
  let list=(t.startXI||[]).map(x=>x.player||x).filter(Boolean);
  // PitchAPI diziliş motorunun ürettiği satır/slot bilgisi varsa HTML tarafında
  // oyuncuları ikinci kez sınıflandırma. Önceki hatanın ana sebebi iki farklı
  // katmanın aynı XI üzerinde farklı kaleci ve hat tahmini yapmasıydı.
  const pitchLayout=detectPitchDepthAxis(list,t&&t.formation,list.find(p=>/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/i.test(String(p.pos||''))));
  const depthAxis=(t&&t.pitchDepthAxis==='x'||t&&t.pitchDepthAxis==='y')?t.pitchDepthAxis:(pitchLayout.axis||'y');
  const depthCoord=p=>Number(depthAxis==='x'?p.pitchX:p.pitchY);
  const canonical=list.length===11&&list.every(p=>p.formationLine!=null&&p.formationLine!==''&&p.formationSlot!=null&&p.formationSlot!==''&&Number.isInteger(Number(p.formationLine))&&Number.isInteger(Number(p.formationSlot)));
  if(canonical){
    const gk=list.find(p=>Number(p.formationLine)===-1)||list.find(p=>/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/i.test(String(p.pos||'')));
    const byLine=new Map();
    list.filter(p=>Number(p.formationLine)>=0).forEach(p=>{const line=Number(p.formationLine);if(!byLine.has(line))byLine.set(line,[]);byLine.get(line).push(p)});
    const rows=[...byLine.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]).map(([,row])=>row.sort((a,b)=>Number(a.formationSlot)-Number(b.formationSlot)));
    if(gk&&rows.length&&rows.flat().length===10)return {gk,rows,byPlace:false,exact:true,grid:null,players:[gk,...rows.flat()],canonical:true,depthAxis};
  }
  const normDepth=v=>{const n=Number(v);return Math.abs(n)>1.5?n/100:n;};
  const hasFullCoords=list.length===11&&list.every(p=>p.pitchX!=null&&p.pitchX!==''&&p.pitchY!=null&&p.pitchY!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(p.pitchX))&&Number.isFinite(Number(p.pitchY)));
  const hasGrid=list.length===11&&list.every(p=>p.grid&&Number(p.grid.row)>0&&Number(p.grid.column)>0);
  const rawFormation=String(t.formation||'').match(/\d+/g)||[];
  const formation=rawFormation.map(Number).filter(n=>n>0);
  const validFormation=formation.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)===10;
  let gi=-1;
  // A single explicit goalkeeper label is stronger evidence than guessing a coordinate extreme.
  const explicitKeeperIndexes=list.map((p,i)=>/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/i.test(String(p.pos||''))?i:-1).filter(i=>i>=0);
  if(explicitKeeperIndexes.length===1)gi=explicitKeeperIndexes[0];
  // If no reliable label exists, use the normalized goalkeeper end of the formation.
  if(gi<0&&hasFullCoords&&pitchLayout.keeper){
    // Use the formation-pattern decision. In an away-side lineup the keeper may
    // be at the HIGH end of the coordinate axis; always selecting the LOW end
    // mistakes the striker for the goalkeeper and rotates the entire XI.
    gi=list.indexOf(pitchLayout.keeper);
  }
  if(gi<0&&hasFullCoords){
    const ordered=list.map((p,i)=>({p,i,x:normDepth(depthCoord(p))})).sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);
    if(ordered[0].x<=.22&&ordered[10].x>=.78)gi=ordered[0].i;
    else if(ordered[0].x<=.22)gi=ordered[0].i;
    else if(ordered[10].x>=.78)gi=ordered[10].i;
  }else if(gi<0&&hasGrid){
    const firstRow=Math.min(...list.map(p=>Number(p.grid.row)));
    const keeper=list.filter(p=>Number(p.grid.row)===firstRow).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.grid.column)-Number(b.grid.column))[0];
    gi=list.indexOf(keeper);
  }
  if(gi<0)gi=list.findIndex(p=>/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/i.test(String(p.pos||'')));
  const byPlace=list.length>0&&list.every(p=>Number(p.place)>0);
  const coordinateKeeper=gi>=0?list[gi]:null;
  if(byPlace)list.sort((a,b)=>Number(a.place)-Number(b.place));
  if(coordinateKeeper){const keeperIndex=list.indexOf(coordinateKeeper);if(keeperIndex>=0)list.unshift(list.splice(keeperIndex,1)[0])}
  else if(!hasFullCoords)list=list.map((p,i)=>({p,i})).sort((a,b)=>(posGrp(a.p.pos)-posGrp(b.p.pos))||(a.i-b.i)).map(o=>o.p);
  const gk=gi>=0?list[0]:null;
  if(gk&&!/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/i.test(String(gk.pos||'')))gk.pos='GK';
  const rest=gk?list.slice(1):list;
  let rows=[],exact=false,usedTemplateRows=false;
  // With no provider coordinates, do not sort every winger into the last line.
  // Use the declared formation's position rows first (e.g. 4-2-3-1 is 4/2/3/1, not 4/3/3).
  if(!hasFullCoords&&!hasGrid&&validFormation&&rest.length===10){
    const template=XI_ROLE_TEMPLATES[formation.join('-')];
    if(template&&template.length===formation.length&&template.every((row,i)=>row.length===formation[i])){
      const family=pos=>{
        const x=String(pos||'').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g,'');
        if(/^(D|DF|DEF|DEFENDER|CB|LCB|RCB|LB|RB|LWB|RWB|WB|SW)$/.test(x)||/DEFENDER|DEFENCE|DEFENSE|BACK/.test(x))return 'def';
        if(/^(DM|CDM|RDM|LDM|OS)$/.test(x)||/DEFENSIVEMIDFIELDER|HOLDINGMIDFIELDER/.test(x))return 'hold';
        if(/^(CM|LCM|RCM|LM|RM|M|MF|MID|MIDFIELDER)$/.test(x)||/CENTRALMIDFIELDER|MIDFIELD/.test(x))return 'mid';
        if(/^(AM|CAM|LAM|RAM)$/.test(x)||/ATTACKINGMIDFIELDER/.test(x))return 'am';
        if(/^(LW|RW|LWF|RWF)$/.test(x)||/WINGER/.test(x))return 'wing';
        if(/^(ST|CF|RCF|LCF|SS|F|FW|FWD|FORWARD|STRIKER|ATTACKER)$/.test(x)||/STRIKER|FORWARD|ATTACKER/.test(x))return 'striker';
        if(x==='')return 'unknown';
        return 'unknown';
      };
      const roleFamily=role=>family(role);
      const compatibility=(pos,role)=>{
        const p=String(pos||'').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g,'');
        const r=String(role||'').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g,'');
        if(!p)return 0;
        if(p===r)return 120;
        const pf=roleFamily(p),rf=roleFamily(r);
        if(pf===rf&&pf!=='unknown')return 95;
        if(pf==='hold'&&rf==='mid')return r==='CM'?82:66;
        if(pf==='mid'&&rf==='hold')return p==='CM'?82:66;
        if(pf==='am'&&rf==='mid'||pf==='mid'&&rf==='am')return 62;
        if(pf==='wing'&&rf==='mid'||pf==='mid'&&rf==='wing')return 58;
        if(pf==='wing'&&rf==='am'||pf==='am'&&rf==='wing')return 56;
        if(pf==='striker'&&rf==='wing'||pf==='wing'&&rf==='striker')return 42;
        if(p==='DF'||p==='DEF'||p==='D'||p==='DEFENDER')return rf==='def'?75:0;
        if(p==='MF'||p==='MID'||p==='M'||p==='MIDFIELDER')return ['hold','mid','am'].includes(rf)?52:0;
        if(p==='FW'||p==='FWD'||p==='F'||p==='FORWARD')return ['wing','striker'].includes(rf)?50:0;
        return 0;
      };
      const assigned=template.map(()=>[]);
      const ordered=rest.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
        const ap=String(a.pos||'').toUpperCase(),bp=String(b.pos||'').toUpperCase();
        const ag=/^(DF|DEF|D|MF|MID|M|FW|FWD|F)$/.test(ap)?0:1;
        const bg=/^(DF|DEF|D|MF|MID|M|FW|FWD|F)$/.test(bp)?0:1;
        return bg-ag||Number(a.place||0)-Number(b.place||0);
      });
      let possible=true;
      for(const player of ordered){
        let best=-1,bestScore=0;
        for(let rowIndex=0;rowIndex<template.length;rowIndex++){
          if(assigned[rowIndex].length>=template[rowIndex].length)continue;
          const score=Math.max(...template[rowIndex].map(role=>compatibility(player.pos,role)));
          if(score>bestScore){bestScore=score;best=rowIndex;}
        }
        if(best<0||bestScore<35){possible=false;break;}
        assigned[best].push(player);
      }
      if(possible&&assigned.every((row,i)=>row.length===template[i].length)){
        rows=assigned.map((row,rowIndex)=>row.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
          const slot=p=>{let bestIndex=0,bestScore=-1;template[rowIndex].forEach((role,index)=>{const score=compatibility(p.pos,role);if(score>bestScore){bestScore=score;bestIndex=index;}});return bestIndex;};
          return slot(a)-slot(b)||Number(a.place||0)-Number(b.place||0);
        }));
        rows.forEach((row,rowIndex)=>row.forEach((player,index)=>{
          const current=String(player.pos||'').toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z]/g,'');
          const generic=['D','DF','DEF','DEFENDER','M','MF','MID','MIDFIELDER','F','FW','FWD','FORWARD','ATTACKER','STRIKER'].includes(current);
          if(generic){player.pos=template[rowIndex][index];player.posSource='Formasyon şablonu (detaylı mevki yok)';}
        }));
        exact=true;usedTemplateRows=true;
      }
    }
  }
  if(!usedTemplateRows&&hasFullCoords&&validFormation&&rest.length===10){
    const reverseDepth=normDepth(gk.pitchY)>.5;const depth=rest.slice().sort((a,b)=>(normDepth(a.pitchY)-normDepth(b.pitchY))*(reverseDepth?-1:1));let offset=0;
    rows=formation.map(n=>{const row=depth.slice(offset,offset+n);offset+=n;return row});exact=true;
  }else if(!usedTemplateRows&&hasGrid&&validFormation&&rest.length===10){
    const depth=rest.slice().sort((a,b)=>Number(a.grid.row)-Number(b.grid.row)||Number(a.grid.column)-Number(b.grid.column));let offset=0;
    rows=formation.map(n=>{const row=depth.slice(offset,offset+n);offset+=n;return row});exact=true;
  }else if(!usedTemplateRows){
    const known=rest.filter(p=>/^(D|CB|LCB|RCB|LB|RB|LWB|RWB|WB|SW|DF|DEF|DEFENDER|OS|DM|CDM|RDM|LDM|CM|LCM|RCM|AM|CAM|RAM|LAM|LM|RM|M|MF|MID|MIDFIELDER|F|FW|FWD|LW|RW|LWF|RWF|ST|CF|RCF|LCF|SS|FORWARD|STRIKER|ATTACKER)/i.test(String(p.pos||''))).length;
    const byPos={1:[],2:[],3:[]};rest.forEach(p=>{const group=posGrp(p.pos);if(group>=1&&group<=3)byPos[group].push(p)});
    if(known>=7&&Object.values(byPos).reduce((n,r)=>n+r.length,0)===rest.length){rows=[byPos[1],byPos[2],byPos[3]].filter(r=>r.length);exact=true}
    else rows=[byPos[1],byPos[2],byPos[3]].filter(r=>r.length);
  }

  return {gk,rows,byPlace,exact,grid:hasGrid?list:null,players:list,depthAxis};
}
function evMap(m){
  const o={};const g=n=>o[nk(n)]||(o[nk(n)]={goal:0,og:0,y:0,r:0,out:null,inn:null});
  mergeMatchEventHistory([],m.events||[]).forEach(e=>{
    const t=String(e.type||'').toLowerCase(),d=String(e.detail||'').toLowerCase(),pn=e.player&&e.player.name,an=e.sub_in_player&&e.sub_in_player.name||e.assist&&e.assist.name;
    const min=(e.time&&e.time.elapsed!=null?e.time.elapsed:'')+(e.time&&e.time.extra?'+'+e.time.extra:'');
    if(t==='goal'&&pn&&!d.includes('missed')){d.includes('own')?g(pn).og++:g(pn).goal++}
    else if(t==='card'&&pn){d.includes('red')&&!d.includes('second')?g(pn).r++:d.includes('second')?(g(pn).y++,g(pn).r++):g(pn).y++}
    else if(t==='subst'||t==='substitution'){if(pn)g(pn).out=min;if(an)g(an).inn=min}
  });
  return o;
}
function badges(x){
  if(!x)return '';
  let a='',l='';
  if(x.goal)a+='⚽'+(x.goal>1?x.goal:'');
  if(x.og)a+='🔴';
  if(x.y&&!x.r)a+='🟨';
  if(x.r)a+='🟥';
  if(x.out!=null)l+=`<span class="bd l"><span class="bd-minute">${esc(x.out)}'</span><span class="bd-symbol">🔻</span></span>`;
  if(x.inn!=null)l+=`<span class="bd l sub-in"><span class="bd-minute">${esc(x.inn)}'</span><span class="bd-symbol">🔺</span></span>`;
  return (a?'<span class="bd '+(x.goal?'goal-badge':'')+'">'+a+'</span>':'')+l;
}
const surname=n=>{const p=String(n||'').trim().split(/\s+/);const s=p.length>1?p.slice(1).join(' '):p[0];return s.length>11?s.slice(0,10)+'…':s};
const PITCH_LINES='<svg class="ln" viewBox="0 0 68 105" preserveAspectRatio="none" fill="none" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.55)" stroke-width=".45"><rect x="1" y="1" width="66" height="103"/><line x1="1" y1="52.5" x2="67" y2="52.5"/><circle cx="34" cy="52.5" r="9"/><rect x="14" y="1" width="40" height="16"/><rect x="25" y="1" width="18" height="6"/><rect x="14" y="88" width="40" height="16"/><rect x="25" y="98" width="18" height="6"/></svg>';
function roleCoord(pos,index,count){
  const p=String(pos||'').toUpperCase();
  const x={GK:50,G:50,RB:82,RWB:82,LB:18,LWB:18,RCB:64,LCB:36,CB:50,CDM:50,DM:50,RDM:65,LDM:35,RCM:67,LCM:33,CM:50,RM:83,LM:17,CAM:50,AM:50,RAM:67,LAM:33,RW:82,RWF:82,LW:18,LWF:18,ST:50,CF:50,RCF:67,LCF:33,SS:50};
  // Her takım kendi yarı sahasında kalır; santraforlar orta çizgiden en az %22 uzakta.
  const y={GK:4,G:4,RB:12,LB:12,RCB:12,LCB:12,CB:12,RWB:15,LWB:15,CDM:20,DM:20,RDM:20,LDM:20,RCM:25,LCM:25,CM:25,RM:27,LM:27,CAM:29,AM:29,RAM:29,LAM:29,RW:34,RWF:34,LW:34,LWF:34,ST:38,CF:38,RCF:36,LCF:36,SS:31};
  if(x[p]!==undefined)return{x:x[p],y:y[p]};
  const group=posGrp(p);
  if(group<0)return {x:50,y:50};
  const fallbackY=group===0?5:group===1?18:group===2?31:43;
  return {x:(index+1)/(count+1)*100,y:fallbackY};
}
function playerRatingBarClass(value){if(value==null||value===''||!Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return '';const n=Number(value);return n<6?'rating-red':n<7?'rating-orange':n<=8?'rating-green':'rating-blue';}
function matchRatingClass(value){
  if(value==null||value===''||!Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return 'unavailable';
  const n=Number(value);
  if(n<6)return 'rating-red';
  if(n<7)return 'rating-orange';
  if(n<8)return 'rating-green';
  return 'rating-blue';
}
function pitchPlayerMetricNumber(row,wanted){
  const aliases=new Set((wanted||[]).map(x=>String(x).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'')));
  const stats=row&&row.stats,groups=Array.isArray(stats)?stats:stats&&typeof stats==='object'?Object.values(stats):[];
  for(const group of groups){
    const fields=group&&((group.stats&&typeof group.stats==='object')?group.stats:group.metrics||group.values);
    const entries=Array.isArray(fields)?fields.map((v,i)=>[String(i),v]):fields&&typeof fields==='object'?Object.entries(fields):[];
    for(const [label,item] of entries){if(!item)continue;const stat=item.stat&&typeof item.stat==='object'?item.stat:item;
      const keys=[label,item.key,item.title,item.name,stat.key,stat.title,stat.name].filter(Boolean).map(x=>String(x).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,''));
      if(!keys.some(k=>aliases.has(k)))continue;const value=stat.value??stat.total??item.value??item.total;
      if(value!=null&&value!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return Number(value);
    }
  }
  return null;
}
function matchPlayerRating(player){
  if(player&&player.rating!=null&&Number.isFinite(Number(player.rating)))return Number(player.rating);
  return pitchPlayerMetricNumber(player,['rating','rating_title','average_rating']);
}
function matchTopRating(m,L){
  const values=[];
  (m&&m.pitch&&Array.isArray(m.pitch.players)?m.pitch.players:[]).forEach(p=>{const rating=matchPlayerRating(p);if(rating!=null)values.push(rating)});
  (L||[]).forEach(t=>[...(t.startXI||[]),...(t.substitutes||[])].forEach(x=>{const p=x.player||x,rating=matchPlayerRating(p);if(rating!=null)values.push(rating)}));
  return values.length?Math.max(...values):null;
}
function separatedFormationPositions(t,r,home){
  const players=(r&&r.players||[]).filter(Boolean);
  if(players.length!==11)return null;
  const normDepth=v=>{const n=Number(v);return Math.abs(n)>1.5?n/100:n;};
  const depthAxis=r.depthAxis||t.pitchDepthAxis||detectPitchDepthAxis(players,t&&t.formation,r.gk).axis||'y';
  const depthValue=p=>normDepth(depthAxis==='x'?p.pitchX:p.pitchY);
  const lateralValue=p=>normDepth(depthAxis==='x'?p.pitchY:p.pitchX);
  const canonical=players.length===11&&players.every(p=>Number.isInteger(Number(p.formationLine))&&Number.isInteger(Number(p.formationSlot)));
  let keeper=null;
  // Worker'ın çizdiği kanonik saha yerleşimi varsa kaleciyi yalnızca formationLine=-1
  // üzerinden seç. pitch_y'nin uç değerleri deplasman kalecisini ev sahibi kalecisiyle karıştırabilir.
  if(canonical)keeper=players.find(p=>Number(p.formationLine)===-1)||null;
  if(!keeper)keeper=r.gk||players.find(p=>/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/i.test(String(p.pos||"")))||null;
  const hasDepth=players.length===11&&players.every(p=>p.pitchX!=null&&p.pitchX!==''&&p.pitchY!=null&&p.pitchY!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(p.pitchX))&&Number.isFinite(Number(p.pitchY)));
  if(!keeper&&hasDepth){
    const depth=players.map(p=>({p,x:depthValue(p)})).sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);
    const low=depth[0],high=depth.at(-1);
    if(low.x<=.22&&high.x>=.78)keeper=low.p;
    else if(low.x<=.22)keeper=low.p;
    else if(high.x>=.78)keeper=high.p;
  }
  if(!keeper)return null;
  if(canonical){
    const rowsMap=new Map();players.filter(p=>Number(p.formationLine)>=0).forEach(p=>{const line=Number(p.formationLine);if(!rowsMap.has(line))rowsMap.set(line,[]);rowsMap.get(line).push(p)});
    const rows=[...rowsMap.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]).map(([,row])=>row.sort((a,b)=>Number(a.formationSlot)-Number(b.formationSlot)));
    if(rows.flat().length!==10)return null;
    const depthRows={2:[.35,.88],3:[.25,.55,.88],4:[.20,.42,.65,.88],5:[.15,.33,.52,.70,.88],6:[.12,.27,.43,.58,.73,.88]};
    const depths=depthRows[rows.length]||Array.from({length:rows.length},(_,i)=>.20+.77*(i/Math.max(1,rows.length-1)));
    const positions=[{p:keeper,x:home?50:50,y:home?4.2:95.8,gk:true}];
    rows.forEach((row,rowIndex)=>{const count=row.length;const isDoublePivot=count===2&&row.every(p=>/^(DM|CDM|LDM|RDM)$/i.test(String(p.pos||'')));row.forEach((player,index)=>{const fraction=count===1?.5:index/(count-1);const x=isDoublePivot?40+20*fraction:count===2?34+32*fraction:8+84*fraction;const y=Math.min(39,4+depths[rowIndex]*38);positions.push({p:player,x:home?100-x:x,y:home?y:100-y,gk:false})})});
    return positions;
  }
  let outfield=players.filter(p=>p!==keeper);
  const formation=(String(t&&t.formation||'').match(/\d+/g)||[]).map(Number).filter(n=>n>0);
  let counts=formation.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)===10?formation:null;
  const coordinates=outfield.every(p=>p.pitchX!=null&&p.pitchX!==''&&p.pitchY!=null&&p.pitchY!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(p.pitchX))&&Number.isFinite(Number(p.pitchY)))&&Math.max(...outfield.map(p=>depthValue(p)))-Math.min(...outfield.map(p=>depthValue(p)))>.32;
  if(!counts&&coordinates){
    const reverse=depthValue(keeper)>.5;
    const depthSorted=outfield.slice().sort((a,b)=>(depthValue(a)-depthValue(b))*(reverse?-1:1));
    const clusters=[];
    for(const player of depthSorted){const depth=depthValue(player),last=clusters[clusters.length-1];if(!last||Math.abs(depth-depthValue(last[last.length-1]))>.095)clusters.push([player]);else last.push(player);}
    const inferred=clusters.map(row=>row.length);
    if(inferred.length>=2&&inferred.length<=5&&inferred.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)===10)counts=inferred;
  }
  if(!counts){
    const defs=outfield.filter(p=>posGrp(p.pos)===1);
    const mids=outfield.filter(p=>posGrp(p.pos)===2);
    const fwds=outfield.filter(p=>posGrp(p.pos)===3);
    const dm=mids.filter(p=>/^(DM|CDM|RDM|LDM)$/i.test(String(p.pos||'')));
    const cm=mids.filter(p=>!dm.includes(p));
    const strikers=fwds.filter(p=>/^(ST|CF|RCF|LCF|STRIKER|FORWARD)$/i.test(String(p.pos||'')));
    const support=fwds.filter(p=>!strikers.includes(p));
    const groups=[defs,dm,cm,support,strikers].filter(row=>row.length);
    if(groups.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.length,0)===10){
      const ordered=groups.flat();
      outfield=ordered;counts=groups.map(row=>row.length);
    }
  }
  if(!counts||counts.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)!==10)return null;
  const hasGrid=players.length===11&&players.every(p=>p.grid&&Number(p.grid.row)>0&&Number(p.grid.column)>0);
  if(!coordinates&&hasGrid&&counts&&counts.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)===10){
    const depthRows={2:[.47,.97],3:[.31,.64,.97],4:[.255,.495,.735,.975],5:[.20,.39,.58,.775,.97],6:[.17,.33,.49,.65,.81,.97]};
    const depths=depthRows[counts.length]||Array.from({length:counts.length},(_,i)=>.20+.77*(i/Math.max(1,counts.length-1)));
    const ordered=outfield.slice().sort((a,b)=>Number(a.grid.row)-Number(b.grid.row)||Number(a.grid.column)-Number(b.grid.column));
    const positions=[];let offset=0;
    positions.push({p:keeper,x:50,y:home?4.2:95.8,gk:true});
    counts.forEach((count,rowIndex)=>{const row=ordered.slice(offset,offset+count);offset+=count;row.sort((a,b)=>Number(a.grid.column)-Number(b.grid.column));row.forEach((player,index)=>{const fraction=count===1?.5:index/(count-1);const x=8+84*fraction;const y=Math.min(39,4+depths[rowIndex]*38);positions.push({p:player,x:home?100-x:x,y:home?y:100-y,gk:false})})});
    return positions;
  }
  if(coordinates){
    const keeperDepth=Number.isFinite(Number(depthAxis==='x'?keeper.pitchX:keeper.pitchY))?depthValue(keeper):.05;
    const reverse=keeperDepth>.5;
    outfield.sort((a,b)=>(depthValue(a)-depthValue(b))*(reverse?-1:1));
  }else if(outfield.every(p=>Number(p.place)>0))outfield.sort((a,b)=>Number(a.place)-Number(b.place));
  if(counts.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)!==outfield.length)return null;
  // Die Formationslinien sind klar getrennt. Der erste Block liegt deutlich
  // vor dem Torwart; insbesondere der letzte Stuermerblock bleibt vom
  // offensiven Mittelfeld/Schattenstuermer getrennt.
  const depthRows={
    2:[.45,.86],
    3:[.29,.59,.86],
    4:[.23,.43,.63,.86],
    5:[.18,.35,.53,.70,.86],
    6:[.15,.30,.45,.60,.74,.86]
  };
  const depths=depthRows[counts.length]||Array.from({length:counts.length},(_,i)=>.20+.77*(i/Math.max(1,counts.length-1)));
  const positions=[];let offset=0;
  const goalkeeperDepth=4.2;
  const keeperX=50;
  positions.push({p:keeper,x:home?keeperX:100-keeperX,y:home?goalkeeperDepth:100-goalkeeperDepth,gk:true});
  counts.forEach((count,rowIndex)=>{
    let row=outfield.slice(offset,offset+count);offset+=count;
    // Yatay yerleşimde sağ/sol etiketleri ekrandaki yönü ifade eder:
    // LB/LW solda, RB/RW sağda kalır. API koordinatları bu sıralamayı ters çeviremez.
    const lateralOrder=p=>({
      LB:0,LWB:0,LW:0,LWF:0,LM:0,LCM:1,LDM:1,LCB:1,CB:2,CM:2,CDM:2,DM:2,
      RDM:3,RCM:3,RCB:3,RM:3,RB:4,RWB:4,RW:4,RWF:4,
      LCF:1,RCF:3,CF:2,ST:2,SS:2,CAM:2,AM:2,LAM:1,RAM:3
    })[String(p.pos||'').toUpperCase()]??2;
    row.sort((a,b)=>lateralOrder(a)-lateralOrder(b)||Number(a.place||0)-Number(b.place||0));
    row.forEach((player,index)=>{
      const fraction=count===1?.5:index/(count-1);
      const isDoublePivot=count===2&&row.every(p=>/^(DM|CDM|LDM|RDM)$/i.test(String(p.pos||'')));
      const attackingLeftToRight=isDoublePivot?40+20*fraction:count===2?34+32*fraction:8+84*fraction;
      const x=home?100-attackingLeftToRight:attackingLeftToRight;
      const rawY=Math.min(39,4+depths[rowIndex]*36);
      positions.push({p:player,x,y:home?rawY:100-rawY,gk:false});
    });
  });
  return positions;
}
function pitchHTML(m,L,isPredicted=false){
  const ev=evMap(m),parts=[],topRating=matchTopRating(m,L);
  const unit=v=>{const n=Number(v);return Math.max(0,Math.min(1,n>1?n/100:n))};
  const place=(t,home)=>{
    const r=xiRows(t,home);
    if(!r.players||r.players.length!==11){
      parts.push(`<div class="pitch-layout-warning">PitchAPI bu takım için 11 oyuncunun tamamını henüz göndermedi.</div>`);
      return;
    }
    const side=home?'h':'a';
    const pitchCode=matchLeagueCode(m);
    const put=(p,x,y,gk,labelWidth=66,labelDx=0,labelDy=0,compact=false)=>{const rating=matchPlayerRating(p),rc=matchRatingClass(rating),star=rating!=null&&topRating!=null&&rating===topRating,photoKey=String(t.team.id||t.team.name)+':'+matchPlayerKey(p.name||''),rawPhoto=String(p.logo||p.photo||p.image_url||''),photo=pitchPhotoSrcCache.get(photoKey)||(/^https:\/\//i.test(rawPhoto)?rawPhoto:''),label=(p.number!=null?String(p.number)+' ':'')+surname(trPlayerName(p.name)),source=p.ratingSource==='SkorHane'?'SkorHane istatistiklerinden hesaplanan puan':p.ratingSource==='API-Football'?'API-Football puanı':p.ratingSource==='PitchAPI'?'PitchAPI oyuncu puanı':'Maç puanı';parts.push(`<div class="pp${compact?' compact':''}" data-player-profile="${esc(p.name)}" data-match-player-id="${esc(p.pitchId||p.id||'')}" data-player-team="${esc(t.team.name||'')}" data-player-pos="${esc(p.pos||'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(pitchCode)}" style="left:${x.toFixed(1)}%;top:${Math.max(2,Math.min(98,y+(home?2.5:-2.5))).toFixed(1)}%" title="Maç istatistiklerini aç: ${esc(trPlayerName(p.name))}"><div class="dot ${gk?'gk':side}">${photo?`<img data-pitch-photo="${esc(photoKey)}" data-photo-fallback="${esc(p.number==null?'•':p.number)}" src="${esc(photo)}" alt="" loading="eager" fetchpriority="high" decoding="async" referrerpolicy="no-referrer">`:`<span class="player-fallback">${p.number==null?'•':esc(p.number)}</span>`}${badges(ev[nk(p.name)])}</div><span class="rating ${rc}" title="${source}">${rating!=null?Number(rating).toFixed(1):'—'}${star?'<i class="rating-star" aria-label="Maçın en yüksek puanı">★</i>':''}</span><div class="pn" style="width:${labelWidth.toFixed(0)}px;max-width:${labelWidth.toFixed(0)}px;transform:translate(${labelDx.toFixed(0)}px,${labelDy.toFixed(0)}px)">${esc(label)}</div></div>`) };
    // Sadece metin etiketlerini birbirinden ayır; x/y saha koordinatları aynen korunur.
    const putLabelSeparated=positions=>{
      if(!home){
        const rows=[];
        for(const point of positions){let row=rows.find(items=>Math.abs(items[0].y-point.y)<=7.5);if(!row){row=[];rows.push(row)}row.push(point)}
        for(const row of rows){if(row.length<2)continue;row.sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);const span=row[row.length-1].x-row[0].x;if(span<Math.min(60,(row.length-1)*15)){const left=Math.max(13,50-Math.min(36,(row.length-1)*8)),right=100-left;row.forEach((point,index)=>{point.x=left+(right-left)*index/(row.length-1)})}}
      }
      const groups=[];
      for(const point of positions.slice().sort((a,b)=>a.y-b.y||a.x-b.x)){
        let group=groups.find(g=>Math.abs(g[0].y-point.y)<=7.5);
        if(!group){group=[];groups.push(group)}group.push(point);
      }
      const boardWidth=Math.max(280,Math.min(704,(Number(window.innerWidth)||360)-16));
      for(const group of groups){
        group.sort((a,b)=>a.x-b.x);
        group.forEach((point,index)=>{
          const left=index?point.x-group[index-1].x:Infinity;
          const right=index<group.length-1?group[index+1].x-point.x:Infinity;
          const nearest=Math.min(left,right);
          const width=Number.isFinite(nearest)?Math.max(26,Math.min(56,nearest*boardWidth/100-3)):56;
          const crowded=Number.isFinite(nearest)&&nearest<13;
          const dx=crowded?(index%2===0?-3:3):0;
          const dy=crowded?(index%2===0?-9:9):0;
          put(point.p,point.x,point.y,point.gk,width,dx,dy,groups.length>=5);
        });
      }
    };
    // PitchAPI starters already contain their own exact normalized positions. Do not replace
    // them with a guessed formation grid: draw these 11 positions first, unchanged in order.
    const pitchPlayers=(r.players||[]).filter(Boolean);
    const validPitchPoint=p=>p&&p.pitchX!=null&&p.pitchX!==''&&p.pitchY!=null&&p.pitchY!==''&&
      Number.isFinite(Number(p.pitchX))&&Number.isFinite(Number(p.pitchY))&&!(Number(p.pitchX)===0&&Number(p.pitchY)===0);
    const hasPitchApiCoordinates=pitchPlayers.length===11&&pitchPlayers.every(validPitchPoint);
    const coordinateKeeper=r.gk||pitchPlayers.find(p=>/^(GK|G|GOALKEEPER|GOALIE|KEEPER|KALECI)$/i.test(String(p.pos||'')))||null;
    const roleSpecificCount=pitchPlayers.filter(p=>/^(RB|RWB|LB|LWB|RCB|LCB|CB|CDM|DM|RDM|LDM|RCM|LCM|CM|RM|LM|CAM|AM|RAM|LAM|RW|RWF|LW|LWF|ST|CF|RCF|LCF|SS)$/i.test(String(p.pos||''))).length;
    if(roleSpecificCount>=7&&coordinateKeeper){
      const xi=[coordinateKeeper,...pitchPlayers.filter(p=>p!==coordinateKeeper)],coords=xi.map((p,i)=>({...roleCoord(p.pos,i,xi.length),i}));
      coords.forEach(c=>{c.x=home?100-c.x:c.x});
      putLabelSeparated(coords.map(c=>({p:xi[c.i],x:c.x,y:home?c.y:100-c.y,gk:xi[c.i]===coordinateKeeper})));
      parts.push(`<div class="pfm" style="${home?'top':'bottom'}:6px">${esc(t.formation||'')}</div>`);return;
    }
    if(hasPitchApiCoordinates&&coordinateKeeper){
      const depthAxis=r.depthAxis||t.pitchDepthAxis||detectPitchDepthAxis(pitchPlayers,t&&t.formation,coordinateKeeper).axis||'y';
      const rawDepth=p=>depthAxis==='x'?p.pitchX:p.pitchY;
      const rawLateral=p=>depthAxis==='x'?p.pitchY:p.pitchX;
      const keeperDepth=unit(rawDepth(coordinateKeeper));
      const reverseDepth=keeperDepth>.5;
      const canonicalLines=pitchPlayers.length===11&&pitchPlayers.every(p=>
        Number.isInteger(Number(p.formationLine))&&Number.isInteger(Number(p.formationSlot))
      )&&pitchPlayers.some(p=>Number(p.formationLine)===-1);
      let exactPositions;
      if(canonicalLines){
        const rowMap=new Map();
        pitchPlayers.filter(p=>Number(p.formationLine)>=0).forEach(p=>{
          const line=Number(p.formationLine);if(!rowMap.has(line))rowMap.set(line,[]);rowMap.get(line).push(p);
        });
        const rows=[...rowMap.entries()].sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]).map(([,row])=>row.sort((a,b)=>Number(a.formationSlot)-Number(b.formationSlot)));
        const lineDepths={
          2:[19,39],
          3:[13,25,39],
          4:[11,20,29,39],
          5:[9,16,23,31,39],
          6:[8,14,20,26,32,39]
        };
        const depths=lineDepths[rows.length]||Array.from({length:rows.length},(_,i)=>14+33*i/Math.max(1,rows.length-1));
        exactPositions=[{p:coordinateKeeper,x:50,y:home?4.2:95.8,gk:true}];
        rows.forEach((row,rowIndex)=>{
          const count=row.length;
          const margin=count===1?50:count===2?32:count===3?15:count===4?9:count===5?8:7;
          row.forEach((player,index)=>{
            const slot=Number(player.formationSlot);
            // Üstteki takım aşağı, alttaki takım yukarı hücum eder.
            // Takım-solundan-sağına gelen slotu yalnızca üst yarıda aynala.
            const screenSlot=home?(count-1-slot):slot;
            const x=count===1?50:margin+(100-2*margin)*screenSlot/Math.max(1,count-1);
            const ownY=Math.max(6,(depths[rowIndex]??47)-(rowIndex===0?2:0));
            exactPositions.push({p:player,x,y:home?ownY:100-ownY,gk:false});
          });
        });
      }else{
        exactPositions=pitchPlayers.map(p=>{
          const isKeeper=p===coordinateKeeper;
          const lateral=isKeeper?50:8+unit(rawLateral(p))*84;
          let depth=unit(rawDepth(p));
          if(reverseDepth)depth=1-depth;
          // Keep both XIs inside their own half. The back line sits closer to its keeper,
          // with the midfield line drawn slightly deeper instead of spreading too far forward.
          const ownHalfY=isKeeper?4.2:Math.min(39,8+depth*34-(posGrp(p.pos)===1?1.8:0));
          const screenLateral=isKeeper?50:(home?100-lateral:lateral);
          return {p,x:screenLateral,y:home?ownHalfY:100-ownHalfY,gk:isKeeper};
        });
      }
      putLabelSeparated(exactPositions);
      parts.push(`<div class="pfm" style="${home?'top':'bottom'}:6px">${esc(t.formation||'')}</div>`);
      return;
    }
    if(!r.gk||!r.exact||!r.rows.length||r.rows.flat().length!==10){
      parts.push(`<div class="pitch-layout-warning">PitchAPI bu maç için güvenilir saha koordinatı veya tam mevki eşleşmesi göndermedi. Yanlış diziliş göstermemek için tahminî yerleşim çizilmedi.</div>`);
      return;
    }
    const arranged=separatedFormationPositions(t,r,home);
    if(arranged){putLabelSeparated(arranged);parts.push(`<div class="pfm" style="${home?'top':'bottom'}:6px">${esc(t.formation||'')}</div>`);return;}
    const Y=y=>home?y:100-y;
    if(r.grid){
      const maxRow=Math.max(...r.grid.map(p=>Number(p.grid.row)||1)),rows=new Map();
      r.grid.forEach(p=>{const k=Number(p.grid.row)||1;if(!rows.has(k))rows.set(k,[]);rows.get(k).push(p)});
      const gridPositions=[];
      for(const [row,players] of rows){players.sort((a,b)=>Number(a.grid.column)-Number(b.grid.column));players.forEach((p,i)=>{const localX=players.length===1?50:15+i*70/(players.length-1),x=home?100-localX:localX,y=5+(row-1)*35/Math.max(1,maxRow-1);gridPositions.push({p,x,y:Y(y),gk:row===1})})}
      putLabelSeparated(gridPositions);
      parts.push(`<div class="pfm" style="${home?'top':'bottom'}:6px">${esc(t.formation||'')}</div>`);return;
    }
    const specific=r.rows.flat().filter(p=>/^(RB|RWB|LB|LWB|RCB|LCB|CB|CDM|DM|RDM|LDM|RCM|LCM|CM|RM|LM|CAM|AM|RAM|LAM|RW|RWF|LW|LWF|ST|CF|RCF|LCF|SS)$/i.test(String(p.pos||''))).length;
    if(specific>=7){
      const xi=[r.gk,...r.rows.flat().filter(p=>p!==r.gk)],coords=xi.map((p,i)=>({...roleCoord(p.pos,i,xi.length),i})),lines=new Map();
      coords.forEach(c=>{const k=c.y;if(!lines.has(k))lines.set(k,[]);lines.get(k).push(c)});
      for(const line of lines.values())if(line.length>1&&Math.max(...line.map(c=>c.x))-Math.min(...line.map(c=>c.x))<12){const center=line.reduce((n,c)=>n+c.x,0)/line.length;line.sort((a,b)=>a.i-b.i).forEach((c,i)=>{c.x=Math.max(8,Math.min(92,center+(i-(line.length-1)/2)*14))})}
      putLabelSeparated(coords.map(c=>{const p=xi[c.i];return {p,x:home?100-c.x:c.x,y:Y(c.y),gk:p===r.gk}}));
      parts.push(`<div class="pfm" style="${home?'top':'bottom'}:6px">${esc(t.formation||'')}</div>`);return;
    }
    const fallbackPositions=[];
    if(r.gk)fallbackPositions.push({p:r.gk,x:50,y:Y(5),gk:true});
    const n=r.rows.length;
    r.rows.forEach((row,i)=>{
      const y=n>1?13+i*(26/(n-1)):34;
      const arr=row;
      arr.forEach((p,k)=>{const localX=(k+1)/(arr.length+1)*100;fallbackPositions.push({p,x:home?100-localX:localX,y:Y(y),gk:false})});
    });
    putLabelSeparated(fallbackPositions);
    parts.push(`<div class="pfm" style="${home?'top':'bottom'}:6px">${esc(t.formation||'')}</div>`);
  };
  const h=L.find(t=>t.team.id===m.teams.home.id)||L[0],a=L.find(t=>t.team.id===m.teams.away.id)||L[1];
  if(h)place(h,true);if(a&&a!==h)place(a,false);
  const est=[h,a].some(t=>t&&!xiRows(t).byPlace&&!xiRows(t).grid);
  const coachLine=t=>t&&t.coach&&t.coach.name?`<div class="pitch-coach">Teknik direktör: ${esc(t.coach.name)}</div>`:'';
  const teamAverage=t=>{const values=xiRows(t).players.map(matchPlayerRating).filter(v=>v!=null&&Number.isFinite(Number(v)));return values.length?(values.reduce((sum,v)=>sum+Number(v),0)/values.length).toFixed(2):null};
  return `<div class="ph">${img(m.teams.home.logo)}${esc(m.teams.home.name)}${teamAverage(h)!=null?`<b class="team-score ${matchRatingClass(teamAverage(h))}">${teamAverage(h)}</b>`:''}<span>${esc((h&&h.formation)||'')}</span></div>${coachLine(h)}
    <div class="pitch${isPredicted?' pitch-predicted':''}">${PITCH_LINES}${parts.join('')}</div>
    <div class="ph">${img(m.teams.away.logo)}${esc(m.teams.away.name)}${teamAverage(a)!=null?`<b class="team-score ${matchRatingClass(teamAverage(a))}">${teamAverage(a)}</b>`:''}<span>${esc((a&&a.formation)||'')}</span></div>${coachLine(a)}
    ${est?'<div class="pnote">Oyuncuların saha içindeki yeri konumlarına göre tahmin edilir; satır sırası kesin değildir.</div>':''}`;
}
function pitchUnwrapClient(v){let x=v;for(let i=0;i<3&&x&&x.data!=null;i++)x=x.data;return x||{}}
function pitchMetricText(stat){
  if(stat==null)return '—';
  if(typeof stat==='object'){
    if(stat.value!=null&&stat.total!=null)return `${stat.value}/${stat.total}`;
    if(stat.value!=null)return String(stat.value)+(stat.percentage!=null?` (${stat.percentage}%)`:'');
    if(stat.total!=null)return String(stat.total);
  }
  return String(stat);
}
const PITCH_PLAYER_STAT_TR={
  rating:'Maç Puanı',rating_title:'Maç Puanı',minutes_played:'Oynadığı Dakika',goals:'Gol',assists:'Asist',
  xg_and_xa:'Beklenen Gol ve Asist (xG + xA)',shot_accuracy:'Şut İsabeti',accurate_passes:'İsabetli Pas',

function pitchPlayerLabelTR(raw){
  const text=String(raw||'').trim();if(!text)return '';
  const key=text.replace(/([a-z0-9])([A-Z])/g,'$1_$2').toLowerCase().replace(/[\s-]+/g,'_').replace(/[()]/g,'').replace(/__+/g,'_');
  const known=PITCH_PLAYER_STAT_TR[key]||PITCH_PLAYER_STAT_TR_EXTRA[key]||PITCH_PLAYER_STAT_TR[key.replace(/_total$/,'')]||PITCH_PLAYER_STAT_TR_EXTRA[key.replace(/_total$/,'')]||statLabelTR(text);
  if(known)return known;
  // Provider keys can expand without notice. Translate known building blocks; never leak
  // a newly introduced English key into the Turkish UI.
  const PARTS={
    total:'Toplam',successful:'Başarılı',accurate:'İsabetli',attempted:'Denenen',attempts:'Deneme',
    passes:'Pas',pass:'Pas',progressive:'İlerletici',long:'Uzun',short:'Kısa',key:'Kilit',through:'Ara',
    crosses:'Orta',cross:'Orta',shots:'Şut',shot:'Şut',target:'İsabetli',on:'İsabetli',off:'İsabetsiz',
    blocked:'Engellenen',inside:'İçinden',outside:'Dışından',box:'Ceza Sahası',penalty:'Penaltı',
    goals:'Gol',goal:'Gol',assists:'Asist',assist:'Asist',minutes:'Dakika',played:'Oynanan',
    tackles:'Top Kapma',tackle:'Top Kapma',interceptions:'Pas Arası',clearances:'Uzaklaştırma',
    blocks:'Blok',recoveries:'Top Kazanma',recovery:'Top Kazanma',pressures:'Baskı',pressure:'Baskı',
    duels:'İkili Mücadele',duel:'İkili Mücadele',won:'Kazanılan',lost:'Kaybedilen',aerial:'Hava Topu',
    ground:'Yer',dribbles:'Çalım',dribble:'Çalım',carries:'Top Sürme',carry:'Top Sürme',
    distance:'Mesafe',touches:'Topla Buluşma',touch:'Topla Buluşma',fouls:'Faul',foul:'Faul',
    yellow:'Sarı',red:'Kırmızı',cards:'Kart',card:'Kart',saves:'Kurtarış',save:'Kurtarış',
    goalkeeper:'Kaleci',keeper:'Kaleci',clean:'Gol Yemeden',sheets:'Maç',offsides:'Ofsayt',offside:'Ofsayt',
    corners:'Korner',corner:'Korner',free:'Serbest',kicks:'Vuruş',throws:'Taç',ins:'Atışı',
    expected:'Beklenen',rating:'Puan',average:'Ortalama',percentage:'Yüzdesi',accuracy:'İsabeti',
    possession:'Topa Sahip Olma',lost_possession:'Top Kaybı',dispossessed:'Topu Kaptırma',
    chances:'Fırsat',created:'Yaratılan',missed:'Kaçırılan',big:'Net',small:'Küçük',
    speed:'Hız',top:'En Yüksek',sprint:'Sprint',covered:'Katedilen',
    own:'Kendi Kalesine',conceded:'Yenilen',prevented:'Önlenen',errors:'Hata',leading:'Yol Açan',
    claims:'Hava Topu Kontrolü',won_claims:'Alınan Hava Topu',distribution:'Dağıtım',distributions:'Dağıtım',
    launches:'Uzun Kullanım',claim:'Hava Topu Kontrolü',rate:'Oran',actions:'Aksiyon',
    vaep:'VAEP',xt:'xT',xag:'xAG',xg:'xG',pv:'Top Değeri',offensive:'Hücum',defensive:'Savunma',
    chain:'Zinciri',buildup:'Oyun Kurulumu',sca:'Şut Yaratan Aksiyon',gca:'Gol Yaratan Aksiyon',
    final:'Son',third:'Üçüncü Bölge',into:'İçine',sequence:'Hücum Sekansı',time:'Süre',
    tackles_won:'Başarılı Top Kapma',passes_into:'Bölgeye Pas'
  };
  const translated=key.split('_').filter(Boolean).map(part=>PARTS[part]||'').filter(Boolean);
  return translated.length?translated.join(' '):'Diğer İstatistik';
}
function pitchPlayerStatItems(row){
  const out=[],seen=new Set(),root=row&&(row.stats??row.statistics??row.player_stats??row.playerStats);
  const add=(rawKey,node)=>{
    if(node==null)return;
    const stat=node&&typeof node==='object'&&node.stat&&typeof node.stat==='object'?node.stat:node;
    let value=typeof node==='number'?node:(stat&&typeof stat==='object'?(stat.value??stat.total??stat.count??node.value??node.total??node.count):node);
    if(value==null||typeof value==='boolean'||(typeof value==='string'&&!value.trim()))return;
    if(typeof value==='object')return;
    const key=String((node&&typeof node==='object'&&(node.key||node.title||node.name||node.label))||rawKey||'').trim();
    if(!key)return;
    const label=pitchPlayerLabelTR(key);
    if(!label||seen.has(label))return;
    seen.add(label);out.push({label,value:pitchMetricText(stat&&typeof stat==='object'?stat:value)});
  };
  const walk=(node,path='',depth=0)=>{
    if(node==null||depth>8)return;
    if(Array.isArray(node)){node.forEach((v,i)=>walk(v,path||String(i),depth+1));return;}
    if(typeof node!=='object')return;
    if(node.stat&&typeof node.stat==='object'){
      add(node.key||node.title||node.name||node.label||path,node);
      return;
    }
    if(['value','total','count'].some(k=>node[k]!=null)){
      add(node.key||node.title||node.name||node.label||path,node);
      return;
    }
    for(const [key,value] of Object.entries(node)){
      if(['type','format_type','description','unit','id'].includes(key.toLowerCase()))continue;
      walk(value,key,depth+1);
    }
  };
  walk(root,'',0);
  return out;
}
function matchPlayerEventFallbackStats(match,player,lineupPlayer){
  if(!match||!player&&!lineupPlayer)return [];
  const target=player||lineupPlayer,name=String(target&&target.name||''),team=String((lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.team&&lineupPlayer.team.name)||cur&&cur.matchPlayerTeam||'');
  const events=Array.isArray(match.events)?match.events:[],status=match.fixture&&match.fixture.status||{};
  let elapsed=Math.max(0,Number(status.elapsed)||0);
  if(!elapsed)elapsed=events.reduce((n,event)=>Math.max(n,Number(event.time&&event.time.elapsed)||0),0);
  if(!elapsed&&DONE.includes(status.short))elapsed=['AET','PEN'].includes(status.short)?120:90;
  const isStarter=(match.lineups||[]).some(side=>(side.startXI||[]).some(entry=>{const candidate=entry.player||entry;return matchPlayerKey(candidate.name)===matchPlayerKey(name)}));
  let entered=isStarter,startMinute=entered?0:null,endMinute=entered?elapsed:null,goals=0,assists=0,yellow=0,red=0;
  const same=n=>!!n&&matchPlayerKey(n)===matchPlayerKey(name);
  for(const ev of events){
    const minute=Number(ev.time&&ev.time.elapsed)||0,who=ev.player&&ev.player.name||'',helper=ev.assist&&ev.assist.name||'',type=String(ev.type||'').toLowerCase(),detail=String(ev.detail||'').toLowerCase();
    if(same(who)){
      if(type==='goal')goals++;
      if(type==='card'){if(detail.includes('red'))red++;else yellow++;}
      if(type==='subst'&&endMinute!=null)endMinute=Math.max(0,minute);
    }
    if(same(helper)&&type==='goal')assists++;
    if(type==='subst'&&same(helper)){entered=true;startMinute=minute;endMinute=elapsed;}
  }
  const minutes=entered?Math.max(0,(endMinute==null?elapsed:endMinute)-(startMinute==null?0:startMinute)):0;
  const values=[['minutes_played',minutes],['goals',goals],['assists',assists],['yellow_cards',yellow],['red_cards',red]];
  if(!entered&&minutes===0&&goals===0&&assists===0&&yellow===0&&red===0)return [];
  return [{key:'match_events',stats:Object.fromEntries(values.map(([key,value])=>[key,{key,stat:{type:'integer',value}}]))}];
}
function matchPlayerKey(name){return playerNameKey(name||'')}
function matchPlayerSource(m){return m&&m.pitch&&Array.isArray(m.pitch.players)?m.pitch.players:[]}
async function loadPitchMatchPlayer(my,playerId,playerName,teamName=''){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='match'||(!playerId&&!playerName)||my.matchPlayerLoading)return;
  my.matchPlayerLoading=true;my.matchPlayerError='';renderMatch();
  try{
    let matchId=String(my.data&&my.data.pitch&&my.data.pitch.id||String(my.id||'').replace(/^p:/,''));
    if(!/^m_[A-Za-z0-9]{6}$/.test(matchId)){
      await loadPitchMatchEnrichment(my,true);
      if(cur!==my)return;
      matchId=String(my.data&&my.data.pitch&&my.data.pitch.id||String(my.id||'').replace(/^p:/,''));
    }
    if(!/^m_[A-Za-z0-9]{6}$/.test(matchId))throw new Error('Bu karşılaşma PitchAPI ile eşleştirilemedi; maç verisi yenilenemedi.');
    const localRows=matchPlayerSource(my.data||{});
    const localRow=localRows.find(x=>String(x.id||x.player_id||'')===String(playerId||''))||localRows.find(x=>playerName&&matchPlayerIdentityEqual(x,{name:playerName}))||{};
    const requestPlayer=String(playerId||localRow.id||localRow.player_id||'');
    const params=new URLSearchParams({id:matchId,name:String(playerName||localRow.name||'')});
    if(/^p_[A-Za-z0-9]+$/.test(requestPlayer))params.set('player',requestPlayer);
    if(teamName)params.set('team',String(teamName));
    let result=null,requestError=null;
    try{result=await api('/pitch-match-player?'+params.toString())}catch(error){requestError=error}
    if(cur!==my)return;
    let resultRoot=result&&(result.data&&typeof result.data==='object'?result.data:result)||{};
    let raw=resultRoot.player||{};
    let rawStats=resultRoot.stats&&(Array.isArray(resultRoot.stats)?resultRoot.stats:Object.keys(resultRoot.stats).length)?resultRoot.stats:(raw.stats||raw.statistics||[]);
    const hasStats=value=>pitchPlayerStatItems({stats:value}).length>0;
    // A provider ID mismatch or a transient endpoint failure must not block a name-and-team lookup.
    if((!hasStats(rawStats)||resultRoot&&resultRoot.matched===false||requestError)&&playerName){
      const retry=new URLSearchParams({id:matchId,name:String(playerName)});if(teamName)retry.set('team',String(teamName));
      try{const byName=await api('/pitch-match-player?'+retry.toString());if(cur!==my)return;const byNameRoot=byName&&(byName.data&&typeof byName.data==='object'?byName.data:byName)||{};const candidateStats=byNameRoot.stats&&(Array.isArray(byNameRoot.stats)?byNameRoot.stats:Object.keys(byNameRoot.stats).length)?byNameRoot.stats:(byNameRoot.player&&(byNameRoot.player.stats||byNameRoot.player.statistics)||[]);if(hasStats(candidateStats)){result=byName;resultRoot=byNameRoot;raw=resultRoot.player||{};rawStats=candidateStats;requestError=null;}}catch(_){}
    }
    const mergedStats=hasStats(rawStats)?rawStats:(Array.isArray(localRow.stats)?localRow.stats:[]);
    my.matchPlayerData={player:{...localRow,...raw,stats:mergedStats,rating:raw.rating??(resultRoot&&resultRoot.rating)??localRow.rating??null},advanced:resultRoot&&resultRoot.advanced||localRow.advanced||null};
    if(!hasStats(mergedStats))my.matchPlayerError=requestError&&requestError.message||'Bu oyuncunun maç istatistikleri sağlayıcıdan alınamadı. Oyuncu kadroda görünüyor ancak bu maç için istatistik satırı yayımlanmamış olabilir.';
  }catch(error){if(cur!==my)return;my.matchPlayerError=error&&error.message||String(error)}
  finally{if(cur===my){my.matchPlayerLoading=false;renderMatch()}}
}
function matchPlayerPanel(m){
  const name=cur.matchPlayerName||'';
  const players=matchPlayerSource(m);
  let p=players.find(x=>String(x.id||x.player_id||'')===String(cur.matchPlayerId))||players.find(x=>name&&matchPlayerKey(x.name)===matchPlayerKey(name));
  let lineupPlayer=null;
  for(const side of (m.lineups||[]))for(const entry of [...(side.startXI||[]),...(side.substitutes||[])]){
    const candidate=entry.player||entry;
    if(String(candidate.pitchId||candidate.id||'')===String(cur.matchPlayerId)||(name&&matchPlayerKey(candidate.name)===matchPlayerKey(name)))lineupPlayer=candidate;
  }
  const fetched=cur.matchPlayerData&&cur.matchPlayerData.player;
  if(fetched){
    const existing=p||{};
    p={...existing,...fetched,stats:Array.isArray(fetched.stats)&&fetched.stats.length?fetched.stats:(existing.stats||[]),rating:fetched.rating??matchPlayerRating(existing)??matchPlayerRating(fetched),advanced:cur.matchPlayerData.advanced||existing.advanced};
  }
  if(!p&&lineupPlayer)p={...lineupPlayer,stats:[],rating:lineupPlayer.rating,logo:lineupPlayer.logo};
  const title=p&&p.name||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.name||name||'Oyuncu';
  const rating=matchPlayerRating(p)??(lineupPlayer&&matchPlayerRating(lineupPlayer)),photo=(p&&(p.logo||p.image_url||p.photo))||(lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.logo)||'';
  const team=lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.team&&lineupPlayer.team.name||cur.matchPlayerTeam||'';
  const rc=matchRatingClass(rating);
  let items=p?pitchPlayerStatItems(p):[];
  if(!items.length){const fallbackStats=matchPlayerEventFallbackStats(m,p,lineupPlayer);if(fallbackStats.length)items=pitchPlayerStatItems({stats:fallbackStats});}
  const adv=p&&p.advanced||{},flat=[];
  const walk=(o,prefix='')=>{if(!o||typeof o!=='object')return;Object.entries(o).forEach(([k,v])=>{if(v&&typeof v==='object')walk(v,prefix?prefix+'_'+k:k);else if(v!=null&&typeof v!=='boolean'&&Number.isFinite(Number(v))){const raw=(prefix?prefix+'_':'')+k;const label=pitchPlayerLabelTR(raw);if(label)flat.push({label,value:String(v)})}})};
  walk(adv);
  const all=[...items,...flat].filter((x,i,a)=>a.findIndex(y=>y.label===x.label)===i).slice(0,40);
  if(cur.matchPlayerLoading&&!all.length)return `<div class="match-player-detail"><button class="match-player-back" data-match-player-back>← Kadroya dön</button><div class="match-player-head">${photo?`<img src="${esc(photo)}" alt="${esc(title)}" loading="lazy">`:`<div class="avatar">${esc(String(title).slice(0,1).toUpperCase())}</div>`}<div><h2>${esc(trPlayerName(title))}</h2><div class="team">${esc(team)}</div><div class="match-score">Maç puanı <b class="${rc}" title="${(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='SkorHane'?'SkorHane istatistiklerinden hesaplanmış puan':(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='API-Football'?'İkinci veri kaynağından alınan maç puanı':'Resmî maç puanı'}">${rating==null?'—':Number(rating).toFixed(1)}</b>${(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='SkorHane'?'<small class="pitch-source-note">İstatistiklerden hesaplandı</small>':''}</div></div></div><div class="empty">Maç istatistikleri getiriliyor…</div></div>`;
  if(!p||!all.length)return `<div class="match-player-detail"><button class="match-player-back" data-match-player-back>← Kadroya dön</button><div class="match-player-head">${photo?`<img src="${esc(photo)}" alt="${esc(title)}" loading="lazy">`:`<div class="avatar">${esc(String(title).split(/\s+/).slice(-1)[0].slice(0,1).toUpperCase())}</div>`}<div><h2>${esc(trPlayerName(title))}</h2><div class="team">${esc(team)}</div><div class="match-score">Maç puanı <b class="${rc}" title="${(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='SkorHane'?'SkorHane istatistiklerinden hesaplanmış puan':(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='API-Football'?'İkinci veri kaynağından alınan maç puanı':'Resmî maç puanı'}">${rating==null?'—':Number(rating).toFixed(1)}</b>${(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='SkorHane'?'<small class="pitch-source-note">İstatistiklerden hesaplandı</small>':''}</div></div></div><div class="empty">Bu oyuncu için maç istatistikleri şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</div></div>`;
  return `<div class="match-player-detail"><button class="match-player-back" data-match-player-back>← Kadroya dön</button><div class="match-player-head">${photo?`<img src="${esc(photo)}" alt="${esc(title)}" loading="lazy">`:`<div class="avatar">${esc(String(title).split(/\s+/).slice(-1)[0].slice(0,1).toUpperCase())}</div>`}<div><h2>${esc(trPlayerName(title))}</h2><div class="team">${esc(team)}</div><div class="match-score">Maç puanı <b class="${rc}" title="${(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='SkorHane'?'SkorHane istatistiklerinden hesaplanmış puan':(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='API-Football'?'İkinci veri kaynağından alınan maç puanı':'Resmî maç puanı'}">${rating==null?'—':Number(rating).toFixed(1)}</b>${(p&&p.ratingSource||lineupPlayer&&lineupPlayer.ratingSource)==='SkorHane'?'<small class="pitch-source-note">İstatistiklerden hesaplandı</small>':''}</div></div></div><div class="sec">Maç istatistikleri</div><div class="match-player-stats">${all.map(x=>`<div class="pstat"><small>${esc(x.label)}</small><b>${esc(x.value)}</b></div>`).join('')}</div>${!pitchPlayerStatItems(p||{}).length&&all.length?'<div class="pitch-source-note">Bu temel değerler maç olaylarından hesaplandı; sağlayıcının ayrıntılı oyuncu verileri henüz gelmedi.</div>':''}${cur.matchPlayerLoading?'<div class="pitch-source-note">Ek maç verileri güncelleniyor…</div>':''}</div>`;
}
function pitchArray(v,key){const x=pitchUnwrapClient(v);if(Array.isArray(x))return x;if(Array.isArray(x[key]))return x[key];if(Array.isArray(x.data&&x.data[key]))return x.data[key];return []}
function pitchAnalyticsNote(m,key){const pitch=m.pitch||{},errors=pitch.analyticsErrors||{};if(pitch.analyticsLive&&errors[key])return `<div class="pitch-source-note">PitchAPI yanıtı: ${esc(String(errors[key]))}</div>`;if(pitch.analyticsLive)return '<div class="pitch-source-note">Canlı analiz verisi kontrol edildi; sağlayıcı henüz bu bölüm için veri yayımlamamış olabilir.</div>';return "<div class=\"pitch-source-note\">Canlı analiz PitchAPI’den isteniyor…</div>"}
function pitchHeatmapHTML(m){
  const heat=pitchUnwrapClient(m.pitch&&m.pitch.heatmaps),teams=heat.teams||heat.team_heatmaps||heat.teamHeatmaps||[];
  if(!teams.length)return '<div class="empty">Isı haritası verisi henüz gelmedi.</div>'+pitchAnalyticsNote(m,'heatmaps');
  const side=cur.heatSide||'home',item=teams.find(x=>(x.side||'')===side)||teams[0],cells=item.cells||[];
  if(!cells.length)return '<div class="empty">Bu takım için ısı haritası verisi henüz yayımlanmamış.</div>'+pitchAnalyticsNote(m,'heatmaps');
  const max=Math.max(...cells.map(x=>Number(x[2])||0),1);
  return `<div class="metric-pills"><button data-hs="home" class="${side==='home'?'on':''}">${esc(m.teams.home.name)}</button><button data-hs="away" class="${side==='away'?'on':''}">${esc(m.teams.away.name)}</button></div><div class="pitch-board">${cells.map(c=>`<span class="hm-cell" style="left:${(Number(c[1])+0.5)/12*100}%;top:${(Number(c[0])+0.5)/16*100}%;opacity:${Math.max(.12,Math.min(.9,(Number(c[2])||0)/max))};background:rgb(255,${Math.round(90+(1-(Number(c[2])||0)/max)*90)},30)"></span>`).join('')}</div><div class="pitch-source-note">${esc(item.team&&item.team.name||m.teams[side].name)} · 16 × 12 hücre · hareket yoğunluğu</div>`;
}
function pitchDeepMetricValue(root,aliases){
  const wanted=new Set(aliases.map(x=>String(x).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'')));
  const visit=value=>{
    if(!value||typeof value!=='object')return null;
    if(Array.isArray(value)){for(const item of value){const found=visit(item);if(found!=null)return found}return null}
    for(const [key,item] of Object.entries(value)){
      const normalized=String(key).toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'');
      if(wanted.has(normalized)){
        const raw=item&&typeof item==='object'?((item.value!=null?item.value:(item.total!=null?item.total:(item.stat&&item.stat.value)))):item;
        if(raw!=null&&raw!==''&&Number.isFinite(Number(raw)))return Number(raw);
      }
      const found=visit(item);if(found!=null)return found;
    }
    return null;
  };
  return visit(root);
}
function pitchAdvancedHTML(m){
  const a=pitchUnwrapClient(m.pitch&&m.pitch.advanced),teams=a.teams||a.team_stats||a.teamStats||[];
  if(!teams.length){
    const live=pitchUnwrapClient(m.pitch&&m.pitch.live),liveTeams=live.teams||[live.home_team||live.home,live.away_team||live.away].filter(Boolean);
    const ppda=liveTeams.map(team=>pitchDeepMetricValue(team,['ppda','passes_per_defensive_action','passes_allowed_per_defensive_action']));
    if(liveTeams.length&&ppda.some(value=>value!=null))return `<div class="pitch-stat-cards"><div class="pitch-stat-card"><small>PPDA · canlı veri</small><b>${ppda[0]==null?'—':ppda[0]}</b><span>${esc(m.teams.home.name)}</span><b>${ppda[1]==null?'—':ppda[1]}</b><span>${esc(m.teams.away.name)}</span></div></div><div class="pitch-source-note">PPDA, PitchAPI canlı takım istatistiklerinden okundu.</div>`;
    return '<div class="empty">PPDA ve gelişmiş takım analizi henüz alınamadı.</div>'+pitchAnalyticsNote(m,'advanced');
  }
  const home=teams.find(x=>x.team&&pitchSameSide(x.team,m.teams.home))||teams[0],away=teams.find(x=>x.team&&pitchSameSide(x.team,m.teams.away))||teams[1];
  const metrics=[['PPDA',x=>(x.defending&&x.defending.ppda)??x.ppda],['Saha hakimiyeti',x=>x.territory&&x.territory.field_tilt],['xT',x=>x.possession_value&&x.possession_value.xt_total],['VAEP',x=>x.possession_value&&x.possession_value.vaep_total],['Pas',x=>x.passing&&x.passing.passes],['İleri pas',x=>x.passing&&x.passing.progressive_passes],['Şut hazırlama aksiyonu',x=>x.creation&&x.creation.sca],['Top kazanma',x=>x.defending&&x.defending.recoveries]];
  const cards=metrics.filter(([label,get])=>get(home)!=null||get(away)!=null).map(([label,get])=>`<div class="pitch-stat-card"><small>${label}</small><b>${get(home)==null?'—':esc(String(get(home)))}</b><span>${esc(m.teams.home.name)}</span><b>${get(away)==null?'—':esc(String(get(away)))}</b><span>${esc(m.teams.away.name)}</span></div>`).join('');
  return cards?`<div class="pitch-stat-cards">${cards}</div><div class="pitch-source-note">PPDA: rakip paslarına göre savunma aksiyonu yoğunluğu. Bazı metrikler yalnızca analiz üretilen maçlarda bulunur.</div>`:'<div class="empty">Bu maç için görüntülenebilir gelişmiş metrik yok.</div>';
}
function pitchNetworkHTML(m){
  const d=pitchUnwrapClient(m.pitch&&m.pitch.network),teams=d.networks||d.teams||[];
  const side=cur.networkSide||'home',net=teams[side==='away'?1:0]||teams[0];
  if(!net||!Array.isArray(net.nodes)||!net.nodes.length)return '<div class="empty">Pas ağı verisi henüz yayımlanmamış.</div>'+pitchAnalyticsNote(m,'network');
  const nodes=net.nodes,edges=net.edges||[],byId=new Map(nodes.map(n=>[String(n.player&&n.player.id||n.player_id||n.id),n]));
  const x=n=>Math.max(3,Math.min(65,Number(n.avg_y)||34)),y=n=>Math.max(3,Math.min(102,Number(n.avg_x)||52.5));
  const lines=edges.map(e=>{const a=byId.get(String(e.from&&typeof e.from==='object'?e.from.id:e.from)),b=byId.get(String(e.to&&typeof e.to==='object'?e.to.id:e.to));return a&&b?`<line x1="${x(a)}" y1="${y(a)}" x2="${x(b)}" y2="${y(b)}" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.48)" stroke-width="${Math.max(.4,Math.min(2,Number(e.passes||1)/5))}"/>`:''}).join('');
  return `<div class="metric-pills"><button data-ns="home" class="${side==='home'?'on':''}">Ev Sahibi</button><button data-ns="away" class="${side==='away'?'on':''}">Deplasman</button></div><div class="pitch-source-note">${esc(net.team&&net.team.name||'Takım')} · ilk 11 pas ağı</div><svg class="pitch-network" viewBox="0 0 68 105" role="img">${lines}${nodes.map(n=>`<circle cx="${x(n)}" cy="${y(n)}" r="${Math.max(1.5,Math.min(3.3,1.2+Number(n.passes||0)/25))}" fill="#f3c945" stroke="#fff" stroke-width=".4"/><text x="${x(n)}" y="${y(n)-3}" font-size="2.5" text-anchor="middle" fill="#fff">${esc((n.player&&n.player.name||'').split(' ').slice(-1)[0]||'')}</text>`).join('')}</svg>`;
}
function pitchMomentumHTML(m){
  const d=pitchUnwrapClient(m.pitch&&m.pitch.momentum),points=Array.isArray(d.points)?d.points:Array.isArray(d)?d:[];
  if(points.length<2)return '<div class="empty">Momentum verisi henüz yayımlanmamış.</div>'+pitchAnalyticsNote(m,'momentum');
  const rawValues=points.map(p=>Number(p.value)||0),unitScale=Math.max(...rawValues.map(Math.abs))<=1.5?45:0.45;const coords=points.map(p=>`${Math.max(0,Math.min(120,Number(p.minute)||0))/120*100},${Math.max(4,Math.min(96,50-(Math.max(-100,Math.min(100,Number(p.value)||0))*unitScale)))}`).join(' ');
  return `<svg class="pitch-momentum" viewBox="0 0 100 100" preserveAspectRatio="none"><line x1="0" y1="50" x2="100" y2="50" stroke="rgba(255,255,255,.45)" stroke-dasharray="2 2"/><polyline points="${coords}" fill="none" stroke="#f5ce4b" stroke-width="1.8"/><text x="2" y="8" font-size="4" fill="#fff">Ev sahibi baskısı</text><text x="2" y="96" font-size="4" fill="#fff">Deplasman baskısı</text></svg>`;
}
function pitchShotsHTML(m){
  const d=pitchUnwrapClient(m.pitch&&m.pitch.shots),periods=Array.isArray(d.periods)?d.periods:[],shots=periods.flatMap(p=>Array.isArray(p.shots)?p.shots:[]);
  if(!shots.length)return '<div class="empty">Şut haritası verisi henüz yayımlanmamış.</div>'+pitchAnalyticsNote(m,'shots');
  const clamp=(v,max)=>Math.max(0,Math.min(max,Number(v)||0));
  const markers=shots.map(sh=>{
    const x=clamp(sh.y,68)/68*100,y=clamp(sh.x,105)/105*100;
    const label=(sh.player&&sh.player.name)||'Bilinmeyen oyuncu';
    const xg=sh.expected_goals==null?'—':Number(sh.expected_goals).toFixed(2);
    return `<span class="pitch-shot-dot" title="${esc(label)} · xG ${esc(xg)}" style="left:${x}%;top:${y}%"></span>`;
  }).join('');
  const rows=shots.slice().sort((a,b)=>(Number(a.minute)||0)-(Number(b.minute)||0)).map(sh=>{
    const minute=sh.minute==null?'—':String(sh.minute)+"'";
    const player=(sh.player&&sh.player.name)||'Bilinmeyen oyuncu';
    const kind=sh.event_type||sh.shot_type||sh.result||'Şut';
    const xg=sh.expected_goals==null?'—':Number(sh.expected_goals).toFixed(2);
    return `<div class="pitch-shot-row"><span>${esc(minute)}</span><b>${esc(trPlayerName(player))}</b><span>${esc(String(kind))}</span><strong>xG ${esc(xg)}</strong></div>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<div class="pitch-board">${markers}</div><div class="pitch-source-note">${shots.length} şut · Oyuncu, dakika ve xG ayrıntıları</div><div class="pitch-shot-list">${rows}</div>`;
}
function hasMatchH2HData(value){
  const raw=pitchUnwrapClient(value)||{};
  const rows=Array.isArray(raw)?raw:(Array.isArray(raw.recent_matches)?raw.recent_matches:Array.isArray(raw.matches)?raw.matches:Array.isArray(raw.fixtures)?raw.fixtures:Array.isArray(raw.results)?raw.results:Array.isArray(raw.items)?raw.items:[]);
  const summary=!Array.isArray(raw)&&(Number(raw.total_matches)>0||Number(raw.home_wins)+Number(raw.draws)+Number(raw.away_wins)>0);
  const finishedRows=rows.some(x=>{if(!x||x.finished===false)return false;const s=x.score||x.scores||x.result||{};const sh=x.score_home??x.home_score??s.home??s.home_score;const sa=x.score_away??x.away_score??s.away??s.away_score;return sh!=null&&sa!=null&&sh!==''&&sa!=='';});
  return !!(summary||finishedRows);
}
function pitchH2HHTML(m,override){
  if(override&&override.h2hLoading&&!override.h2h)return '<div class="empty">İkili rekabet kayıtları yükleniyor…</div>';
  if(override&&override.h2hError&&!override.h2h)return '<div class="empty">'+esc(override.h2hError)+'</div>';
  const raw=pitchUnwrapClient((override&&override.h2h)||(m.pitch&&m.pitch.h2h))||{};
  const rawRows=Array.isArray(raw)?raw:(Array.isArray(raw.recent_matches)?raw.recent_matches:Array.isArray(raw.matches)?raw.matches:Array.isArray(raw.fixtures)?raw.fixtures:Array.isArray(raw.results)?raw.results:Array.isArray(raw.items)?raw.items:[]);
  const rows=rawRows.filter(x=>{if(!x||x.finished===false)return false;const score=x.score||x.scores||x.result||{};const sh=x.score_home??x.home_score??score.home??score.home_score;const sa=x.score_away??x.away_score??score.away??score.away_score;return sh!=null&&sa!=null&&sh!==''&&sa!==''});
  const h=Array.isArray(raw)?{}:raw;
  const hasSummary=Number(h.total_matches)>0||Number(h.home_wins)+Number(h.draws)+Number(h.away_wins)>0;
  if(!hasSummary&&!rows.length)return `<div class="h2h-empty"><b>${esc(m.teams.home.name)} – ${esc(m.teams.away.name)}</b><p>İkili rekabet bölümü bu maçta da kullanılabilir. Ancak bağlı veri sağlayıcısı henüz geçmiş karşılaşma kaydı döndürmedi. Bu durum takımların daha önce hiç karşılaşmadığını kanıtlamaz.</p></div>`;
  const norm=x=>playerNameKey(x||'');let hw=Number(h.home_wins)||0,dr=Number(h.draws)||0,aw=Number(h.away_wins)||0;
  if(h.home_wins==null||h.draws==null||h.away_wins==null){hw=0;dr=0;aw=0;rows.forEach(x=>{const scoreObj=x.score||x.scores||{},shRaw=x.score_home??x.home_score??scoreObj.home,saRaw=x.score_away??x.away_score??scoreObj.away;if(shRaw==null||saRaw==null||shRaw===''||saRaw===''||x.finished===false)return;const sh=Number(shRaw),sa=Number(saRaw);if(!Number.isFinite(sh)||!Number.isFinite(sa))return;const hn=x.home&&x.home.name||x.home_team&&x.home_team.name||'',an=x.away&&x.away.name||x.away_team&&x.away_team.name||'';if(sh===sa)dr++;else if((norm(hn)===norm(m.teams.home.name)&&sh>sa)||(norm(an)===norm(m.teams.home.name)&&sa>sh))hw++;else if((norm(hn)===norm(m.teams.away.name)&&sh>sa)||(norm(an)===norm(m.teams.away.name)&&sa>sh))aw++})}
  const total=Number(h.total_matches)||rows.length;
  const cards=rows.map(x=>{const home=x.home&&x.home.name||x.home_team&&x.home_team.name||x.homeTeam&&x.homeTeam.name||'',away=x.away&&x.away.name||x.away_team&&x.away_team.name||x.awayTeam&&x.awayTeam.name||'';const scoreObj=x.score||x.scores||x.result||{},sh=x.score_home??x.home_score??scoreObj.home??scoreObj.home_score,sa=x.score_away??x.away_score??scoreObj.away??scoreObj.away_score;const score=sh==null||sa==null?'—':`${sh} - ${sa}`;const date=x.time_utc||x.date||x.match_date||x.kickoff;const d=date?new Date(date):null;return `<div class="h2h-row"><div class="h2h-team">${esc(home)}<small>${d&&!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())?d.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR'):''}</small></div><div class="h2h-score">${esc(score)}</div><div class="h2h-team away">${esc(away)}</div></div>`}).join('');
  return `<div class="h2h-summary"><div><b>${hw}</b><small>${esc(m.teams.home.name)} galibiyet</small></div><div><b>${dr}</b><small>Beraberlik</small></div><div><b>${aw}</b><small>${esc(m.teams.away.name)} galibiyet</small></div></div><div class="pitch-source-note">Toplam ${total} karşılaşma${override&&override.h2hSource?' · Kaynak: '+esc(override.h2hSource):''}</div>${cards||'<div class="empty">Geçmiş maçların skor listesi şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</div>'}`;
}
function luHTML(m){
  const L=m.lineups||[];
  if(liveS(m.fixture&&m.fixture.status&&m.fixture.status.short)&&cur&&cur.kind==='match'&&!cur.pitchAnalyticsPromise&&(!m.pitch||!m.pitch.analyticsLive))loadPitchLiveAnalytics(cur,false);
  const views=[['kadro','İlk 11'],['heat','Takım ısı haritası'],['ppda','PPDA / Analiz'],['network','Pas ağı'],['momentum','Momentum'],['shots','Şut haritası']];
  cur.kview=cur.kview||'kadro';
  const nav=`<div class="pitch-views">${views.map(([k,l])=>`<button data-kview="${k}" class="${cur.kview===k?'on':''}">${l}</button>`).join('')}</div>${liveS(m.fixture&&m.fixture.status&&m.fixture.status.short)?'<div class="pitch-source-note">Canlı analizler PitchAPI’den ayrı olarak güncellenir. <button data-pitch-analytics-retry style="border:0;background:none;color:var(--acc);font:inherit;font-weight:700">Şimdi yenile</button></div>':''}`;
  if(cur.kview==='heat')return nav+pitchHeatmapHTML(m);
  if(cur.kview==='ppda')return nav+pitchAdvancedHTML(m);
  if(cur.kview==='network')return nav+pitchNetworkHTML(m);
  if(cur.kview==='momentum')return nav+pitchMomentumHTML(m);
  if(cur.kview==='shots')return nav+pitchShotsHTML(m);
  if(!L.some(t=>Array.isArray(t.startXI)&&t.startXI.length))return nav+'<div class="empty">Kadro bilgisi şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</div><button class="match-player-back" data-pitch-lineup-retry>↻ Kadroyu yeniden kontrol et</button>'+(cur.pitchErr?'<div class="pitch-source-note">Şu anda bazı maç detayları görüntülenemiyor.</div>':'');
  const ev=evMap(m),pitchCode=matchLeagueCode(m);
  const h=L.find(t=>pitchSameSide(t.team,m.teams.home))||L[0],a=L.find(t=>pitchSameSide(t.team,m.teams.away))||L[1]||L[0];
  const homeKey=String(h&&h.team&&(h.team.id||'')||'home'),awayKey=String(a&&a.team&&(a.team.id||'')||'away');
  const teamTabs=`<div class="bench-team-picker"><button class="bench-team-btn ${String(cur.benchSide||homeKey)===homeKey?'on':''}" data-bench-side="${esc(homeKey)}">${img(h.team.logo)}<span>${esc(h.team.name)}</span></button>${a&&a!==h?`<button class="bench-team-btn ${String(cur.benchSide||homeKey)===awayKey?'on':''}" data-bench-side="${esc(awayKey)}">${img(a.team.logo)}<span>${esc(a.team.name)}</span></button>`:''}</div>`;
  const confirmed=[h,a].filter(Boolean).every(t=>t.confirmed!==false);
  const predicted=[h,a].some(t=>t&&t.confirmed===false);
  const predictionTypes=[h,a].filter(t=>t&&t.confirmed===false).map(t=>t.lineupType).filter(Boolean);
  const benchSection=side=>{if(!side)return '';const bench=Array.isArray(side.substitutes)?side.substitutes:[];const manager=side.coach&&side.coach.name||'Teknik direktör bilgisi yok';const rows=bench.map(entry=>{const p=entry.player||entry,rating=matchPlayerRating(p),photoKey=String(side.team.id||side.team.name)+':'+matchPlayerKey(p.name||''),photo=pitchPhotoSrcCache.get(photoKey)||String(p.logo||p.photo||p.image_url||''),safePhoto=/^https:\/\//i.test(photo)?photo:'',rc=matchRatingClass(rating);return `<div class="bench-player">${safePhoto?`<img data-pitch-photo="${esc(photoKey)}" data-photo-fallback="${p.number==null?'•':p.number}" src="${esc(safePhoto)}" alt="" loading="lazy" decoding="async" referrerpolicy="no-referrer">`:`<div class="bench-avatar">${p.number==null?'•':esc(p.number)}</div>`}<span class="bench-no">${p.number==null?'—':esc(p.number)}</span><span class="bench-name" data-player-profile="${esc(p.name||'')}" data-match-player-id="${esc(p.pitchId||p.id||'')}" data-player-team="${esc(side.team.name||'')}" data-player-pos="${esc(p.pos||'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(pitchCode)}">${esc(trPlayerName(p.name||'İsimsiz oyuncu'))}<small class="bench-pos">${esc(posTR(p.pos||''))}</small></span><b class="rating ${rc}">${rating!=null?Number(rating).toFixed(1):'—'}</b></div>`}).join('');return `<section class="match-bench-under-xi"><div class="bench-title">${esc(side.team.name||'Takım')} · YEDEKLER (${bench.length})</div><div class="bench-list">${rows||'<div class="bench-empty">Yedek oyuncu kaydı bulunamadı.</div>'}</div><div class="bench-manager"><span class="bench-manager-avatar">T</span><div><small>TEKNİK DİREKTÖR</small><b>${esc(manager)}</b></div></div></section>`};
  const selectedBenchKey=String(cur.benchSide||homeKey),selectedBench=selectedBenchKey===awayKey?a:h;
  return nav+(predicted?'<div class="pitch-source-note">Tahmini kadro · '+esc([...new Set(predictionTypes)].join(' / ')||'Resmî ilk 11 henüz kesinleşmedi')+'</div>':!confirmed?'<div class="pitch-source-note">Resmî kadro verisi henüz doğrulanmadı.</div>':'')+pitchHTML(m,L,predicted)+teamTabs+benchSection(selectedBench)+(cur.pitchErr?'<div class="pitch-source-note">Şu anda bazı maç detayları görüntülenemiyor.</div>':'');
}
async function loadMatchH2H(view){
  const m=view&&(view.data||view.base);if(!m||view.h2hLoading||view.h2hLoaded)return;
  view.h2hLoading=true;view.h2hError='';if(cur===view)renderMatch();
  try{const q='/match-h2h?home='+encodeURIComponent(m.teams.home.name)+'&away='+encodeURIComponent(m.teams.away.name)+'&code='+encodeURIComponent(matchLeagueCode(m))+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(matchSeasonYear(m));const j=await api(q);if(cur!==view)return;view.h2h=j.h2h||null;view.h2hSource=j.source||'';view.h2hLoaded=true;if(!hasMatchH2HData(view.h2h))view.h2hError='Bu eşleşme için bağlı veri sağlayıcılarında doğrulanmış geçmiş maç kaydı bulunamadı. Bu, takımların daha önce hiç karşılaşmadığı anlamına gelmez.';}
  catch(e){view.h2hError='İkili rekabet verisi alınamadı: '+String(e.message||e).slice(0,120);}
  finally{view.h2hLoading=false;if(cur===view)renderMatch();}
}
function statHTML(m){
  if(NS.includes(stat(m))){if(cur&&!cur.matchPreviewLoaded&&!cur.matchPreviewLoading&&!cur.matchPreviewError)loadMatchPreviewStats(cur);return matchPreviewHTML(m,cur||{});}
  const all=m.statistics||[];
  const side=(team,index)=>{const id=team&&team.id;if(id!=null&&id!==''){const found=all.find(x=>x.team&&x.team.id!=null&&String(x.team.id)===String(id));if(found)return found}return all.find(x=>String(x.team&&x.team.id)===(index?'a':'h'))||all[index]||{statistics:[]}};
  const hs=side(m.teams.home,0).statistics||[],as=side(m.teams.away,1).statistics||[];
  const collect=list=>{const out=new Map();for(const x of list){const label=statLabelTR(x.type||x.name);let value=x.value??x.displayValue;if(value&&typeof value==='object')value=value.displayValue??value.value??'';if(label&&value!=null&&value!==''&&!out.has(label))out.set(label,value)}return out};
  const hm=collect(hs),am=collect(as);
  const order=['Topa Sahip Olma','Toplam Şut','İsabetli Şut','İsabetsiz Şut','Engellenen Şut','Ceza Sahası İçi Şut','Ceza Sahası Dışı Şut','Korner','Faul','Ofsayt','Sarı Kart','Kırmızı Kart','Kaleci Kurtarışı','Toplam Pas','İsabetli Pas','Pas Başarısı','Top Kapma','Top Çalma','Uzaklaştırma','Beklenen Gol (xG)'];
  const names=[...order.filter(n=>hm.has(n)||am.has(n)),...new Set([...hm.keys(),...am.keys()].filter(n=>!order.includes(n)))].slice(0,18);
  if(!names.length)return '<div class="empty">Bu karşılaşma için şu anda takım istatistiği yok.</div>';
  const num=v=>{const n=parseFloat(String(v==null?'':v).replace(',','.').replace(/[^\d.-]/g,''));return Number.isFinite(n)?n:null};
  const fmt=(v,label)=>{if(v==null||v==='')return '—';const s=String(v);return /%/.test(s)||/%|yüzde|topa sahip|pas başar/i.test(label)?(/%/.test(s)?s:s+'%'):s};
  const rows=names.map(label=>{
    const h=hm.get(label),a=am.get(label);
    const hn=num(h),an=num(a),sum=(hn!=null?Math.max(0,hn):0)+(an!=null?Math.max(0,an):0);
    const width=sum?Math.max(4,Math.min(100,Math.round((hn||0)/sum*100))):50;
    return `<div class="statrow"><span>${esc(fmt(h,label))}</span><div><div class="label">${esc(label)}</div><div class="statbar"><span style="width:${width}%"></span></div></div><span>${esc(fmt(a,label))}</span></div>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<div class="statrows">${rows}</div>${m.statisticsSource?'<div class="pnote">'+esc(m.statisticsSource)+'.</div>':''}`;
}
function matchPreviewHTML(m,view){
  const data=view&&view.matchPreview;
  if(view&&view.matchPreviewLoading&&!data)return '<div class="empty">Maç öncesi takım ve oyuncu istatistikleri yükleniyor…</div>';
  if(view&&view.matchPreviewError&&!data)return '<div class="empty">'+esc(view.matchPreviewError)+'</div>';
  if(!data)return '<div class="empty">Bu maçın sezon öncesi istatistikleri hazırlanıyor…</div>';
  const playerRows=(team,key)=>{const rows=(team.players||[]).slice().sort((a,b)=>(Number(b[key])||0)-(Number(a[key])||0)).filter(p=>(Number(p[key])||0)>0).slice(0,5);return rows.length?rows.map((p,i)=>`<div class="preview-player"><span class="preview-rank">${i+1}</span><span class="preview-player-name">${esc(trPlayerName(p.name))}</span><b>${Number(p[key])||0}</b></div>`).join(''):'<div class="preview-empty">Henüz veri yok</div>'};
  const card=(team,title,logo)=>`<div class="preview-card"><div class="preview-team">${logo?img(logo):''}<b>${esc(title)}</b></div><h4>En çok gol atan 5 oyuncu</h4>${playerRows(team,'goals')}<h4>En çok asist yapan 5 oyuncu</h4>${playerRows(team,'assists')}</div>`;
  const summary=(t,label)=>`<div class="preview-summary-card"><b>${esc(label)}</b><div class="preview-summary-grid"><span>Oynanan maç <strong>${t.matches??'—'}</strong></span><span>Atılan gol <strong>${t.goalsFor??'—'}</strong></span><span>Yenilen gol <strong>${t.goalsAgainst??'—'}</strong></span><span>Galibiyet <strong>${t.wins??'—'}</strong></span><span>Beraberlik <strong>${t.draws??'—'}</strong></span><span>Mağlubiyet <strong>${t.losses??'—'}</strong></span><span>Penaltı golü <strong>${t.penaltyEventsAllKnown?t.penaltyGoals:'—'}</strong></span><span>Gol/maç <strong>${t.matches?((t.goalsFor||0)/t.matches).toFixed(2):'—'}</strong></span></div></div>`;
  const scope=data.scope||'Bu ligde, bu sezondaki tamamlanmış maçların verileri';
  return `<div class="match-preview"><div class="preview-heading"><b>Maç öncesi istatistikleri</b><small>${esc(scope)}</small></div><div class="preview-columns">${card(data.home,m.teams.home.name,m.teams.home.logo)}${card(data.away,m.teams.away.name,m.teams.away.logo)}</div><h4 class="preview-section-title">Takım karşılaştırması</h4><div class="preview-columns">${summary(data.home,m.teams.home.name)}${summary(data.away,m.teams.away.name)}</div>${data.truncated?'<div class="pnote">İstatistik taraması sağlayıcı istek sınırına ulaştı; değerler incelenen maçlarla sınırlıdır.</div>':''}<div class="pnote">Penaltı golü yalnızca maç olayları eksiksiz alındığında gösterilir; eksik kaynak verisi sıfır kabul edilmez.</div></div>`;
}
async function loadMatchPreviewStats(view){
