  const m=view&&(view.data||view.base);if(!m||view.matchPreviewLoading||view.matchPreviewLoaded||view.matchPreviewError)return;
  const code=matchLeagueCode(m),season=matchSeasonYear(m);if(!code){view.matchPreviewError='Bu turnuva için sezon istatistiği sağlayıcı eşleştirmesi bulunamadı.';if(cur===view)renderMatch();return;}
  view.matchPreviewLoading=true;if(cur===view)renderMatch();
  const loadSide=async team=>{
    let offset=0,done=false,pages=0;const aggregate={matches:0,wins:0,draws:0,losses:0,goalsFor:0,goalsAgainst:0,points:0,cleanSheets:0,penaltyGoals:0,penaltyEventsKnownMatches:0,penaltyEventsAllKnown:false};const players=new Map();
    while(!done&&pages<3){const q='/pitch-team-stats?code='+encodeURIComponent(code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)+'&team='+encodeURIComponent(team.name)+'&offset='+offset+'&limit=12';const res=await api(q);const t=res.team||{};for(const k of Object.keys(aggregate))if(typeof aggregate[k]==='number')aggregate[k]+=Number(t[k])||0;
      for(const p of res.players||[]){const id=String(p.id||matchPlayerKey(p.name));if(!players.has(id))players.set(id,{...p,goals:0,assists:0,apps:0});const row=players.get(id);row.goals+=Number(p.goals)||0;row.assists+=Number(p.assists)||0;row.apps+=Number(p.apps)||0;if(!row.photo&&p.photo)row.photo=p.photo;}
      pages++;offset=Number(res.nextOffset||offset+20);done=!!res.done;if(!res.players&&!res.team)throw new Error('Takım istatistikleri boş döndü');
    }
    aggregate.penaltyEventsAllKnown=aggregate.matches>0&&aggregate.penaltyEventsKnownMatches===aggregate.matches;
    return {...aggregate,players:[...players.values()],truncated:!done};
  };
  try{const [home,away]=await Promise.all([loadSide(m.teams.home),loadSide(m.teams.away)]);if(cur!==view)return;view.matchPreview={home,away,truncated:!!(home.truncated||away.truncated),scope:'Bu ligde, bu sezonda tamamlanmış maçlardan hesaplanır'};view.matchPreviewLoaded=true;}
  catch(e){view.matchPreviewError='Maç öncesi istatistikler şu anda alınamadı. Bu lig için PitchAPI sezon verisi bulunmuyor olabilir.';}
  finally{view.matchPreviewLoading=false;if(cur===view)renderMatch();}
}
function iddaaStatusLabel(m,data){if(data&&data.preMatchSnapshot)return 'Maç önü arşivi · canlı oran yok';if(data&&data.isLive)return 'Maç başladı · canlı oran yok';if(liveS(stat(m))||HT.includes(stat(m)))return 'Maç başladı · canlı oran yok';return DONE.includes(stat(m))?'Maç tamamlandı':'Maç önü oran';}
function iddaaHTML(m,view){
  const data=view&&view.iddaa;
  const tabs=[['all','Tümü'],['special','Bana Özel'],['ms','MS'],['goals','Gol'],['half','Devre'],['score','Skor'],['combo','Kombo'],['corners','Korner'],['cards','Kart']];
  const selected=view&&view.matchIddaaCategory||'all';
  const tabHtml=tabs.map(([key,label])=>`<button type="button" class="match-iddaa-tab${selected===key?' active':''}" data-match-iddaa-tab="${key}">${label}</button>`).join('');
  if(view&&view.iddaaLoading&&!data)return `<div class="match-iddaa-shell"><nav class="match-iddaa-tabs">${tabHtml}</nav><div class="empty">Resmî İddaa marketleri yükleniyor…</div></div>`;
  if(view&&view.iddaaError)return `<div class="iddaa-unavailable"><b>Resmî oranlar alınamadı</b><p>${esc(view.iddaaError)}</p><a href="https://www.iddaa.com/program/futbol" target="_blank" rel="noopener">İddaa resmî futbol bültenini aç</a></div>`;
  if(!data||!Array.isArray(data.markets)||!data.markets.length){const msg=data&&data.message?data.message:'Bu maç için OddsPapi üzerinden İddaa marketi bulunamadı.';return `<div class="match-iddaa-shell"><nav class="match-iddaa-tabs">${tabHtml}</nav><div class="iddaa-unavailable"><div class="iddaa-brand"><span class="iddaa-brand-mark">iddaa</span><b>RESMÎ İDDAA</b></div><p>${esc(msg)}</p><p>Kaynak: OddsPapi · İddaa</p></div></div>`;}
  const markets=data.markets.filter(market=>selected==='all'||selected==='special'&&/maç sonucu|mac sonucu|match result/i.test(String(market.name||''))||selected!=='special'&&selected!=='all'&&iddaaMarketCategories(market).includes(selected));
  const cards=markets.map((market,index)=>{
    const name=String(market.name||'Bahis türü');
    const outcomeList=market.outcomes||[];const outcomes=outcomeList.map(out=>`<div class="match-iddaa-choice"><span>${esc(out.label)}</span>${view&&view.iddaaHideOdds?'':`<b>${Number.isFinite(Number(out.odd))?Number(out.odd).toFixed(2):'—'}</b>`}</div>`).join('');
    return `<section class="match-iddaa-market"><header class="match-iddaa-market-head"><span class="match-iddaa-number">${index+1}</span><b>${esc(name)}</b><button type="button" class="match-iddaa-info" aria-label="Market bilgisi" title="${esc(market.details||name)}">i</button><span class="match-iddaa-trend" aria-hidden="true">↕</span><button type="button" class="match-iddaa-collapse" aria-label="Market alanını daralt" data-match-iddaa-collapse>⌃</button></header>${market.details?`<p class="match-iddaa-details">${esc(market.details)}</p>`:''}<div class="match-iddaa-choices${outcomeList.length===3?' three':''}">${outcomes||'<span class="iddaa-picker-empty">Seçenek bulunamadı</span>'}</div></section>`;
  }).join('');
  return `<div class="match-iddaa-shell"><nav class="match-iddaa-tabs" aria-label="İddaa market filtreleri">${tabHtml}</nav><div class="match-iddaa-market-list">${cards||'<div class="iddaa-picker-empty">Bu kategoride gösterilecek market bulunamadı.</div>'}</div><div class="match-iddaa-source">${view&&view.iddaaHideOdds?'Hızlı İddaa görünümü · Oranlar gösterilmez':'Kaynak: İddaa · OddsPapi. Oranlar sağlayıcıdan alınır ve değişebilir.'}</div></div>`;
}
async function loadIddaaOdds(view,force=false){
  const m=view&&(view.data||view.base);if(!m||view.iddaaLoading||(!force&&view.iddaaLoaded))return;
  view.iddaaLoading=true;view.iddaaError='';if(cur===view)renderMatch();
  try{const j=await requestIddaaOdds(m);if(cur!==view)return;view.iddaa=j;view.iddaaLoaded=true;view.iddaaRefreshAt=Date.now();}
  catch(e){if(cur===view)view.iddaaError='Oran servisi şu anda yanıt vermedi: '+String(e.message||e).slice(0,150);}
  finally{view.iddaaLoading=false;if(cur===view)renderMatch();}
}
async function requestIddaaOdds(m){
  // Maç önü marketi maç başladı/bitirdi/ertelendi diye silinmez.
  // Cihazda daha önce alınmış gerçek maç önü kaydı her statüde önce kullanılır.
  const snapshot=iddaaReadPrematchSnapshot(m);
  if(snapshot)return snapshot;
  const date=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Europe/Istanbul',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date(m.fixture.date));
  const q='/iddaa-odds?home='+encodeURIComponent(m.teams.home.name)+'&away='+encodeURIComponent(m.teams.away.name)+'&date='+encodeURIComponent(date)+'&status='+encodeURIComponent(stat(m));
  try{
    const data=await api(q,40000);
    if(data&&Array.isArray(data.markets)&&data.markets.length)iddaaSavePrematchSnapshot(m,data);
    if(!data?.markets?.length){const cached=iddaaReadPrematchSnapshot(m);if(cached)return cached;}
    return data;
  }catch(error){
    const cached=iddaaReadPrematchSnapshot(m);if(cached)return cached;
    throw error;
  }
}
const IDDAA_PREMATCH_CACHE_KEY='skorhane_iddaa_prematch_cache_v2';
function iddaaSnapshotMatchKey(m){
  const date=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-CA',{timeZone:'Europe/Istanbul',year:'numeric',month:'2-digit',day:'2-digit'}).format(new Date(m.fixture.date));
  return [date,nk(m.teams.home.name),nk(m.teams.away.name)].join('|');
}
function iddaaReadPrematchSnapshot(m){
  try{
    const all=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(IDDAA_PREMATCH_CACHE_KEY)||'{}'),entry=all[iddaaSnapshotMatchKey(m)];
    if(!entry||!Array.isArray(entry.markets)||!entry.markets.length||Number(entry.expiresAt)<Date.now())return null;
    return {...entry,preMatchSnapshot:true,isLive:liveS(stat(m))||HT.includes(stat(m)),historicalSnapshot:true};
  }catch(_){return null;}
}
function iddaaSavePrematchSnapshot(m,data){
  if(!data||!Array.isArray(data.markets)||!data.markets.length)return;
  try{
    const all=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(IDDAA_PREMATCH_CACHE_KEY)||'{}'),key=iddaaSnapshotMatchKey(m);
    all[key]={source:data.source||'iddaa',provider:data.provider||'OddsPapi',available:true,fixtureId:data.fixtureId||'',markets:data.markets,updatedAt:data.updatedAt||new Date().toISOString(),savedAt:Date.now(),expiresAt:Date.now()+3650*86400000};
    const sorted=Object.entries(all).sort((a,b)=>Number(b[1].savedAt||0)-Number(a[1].savedAt||0)).slice(0,250);
    localStorage.setItem(IDDAA_PREMATCH_CACHE_KEY,JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(sorted)));
  }catch(_){}
}
function iddaaMarketCategories(market){
  const name=String(market&&market.name||'').toLowerCase();
  const type=String(market&&market.type||'').toLowerCase();
  const details=String(market&&market.details||'').toLowerCase();
  const period=String(market&&market.period||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[ _-]/g,'');
  const value=[name,type,details,period].join(' ');
  const cats=new Set();
  const isHalf=/firsthalf|secondhalf|half|1sthalf|2ndhalf|ilk yar|ikinci yar|devre/.test(value);
  if(isHalf)cats.add('half');
  if(/card|cards|booking|yellow|red card|kart|sarı kart|sari kart|kırmızı kart|kirmizi kart|booking point/.test(value))cats.add('cards');
  if(/corner|corners|korner|köşe|kose/.test(value))cats.add('corners');
  if(/combo|combined|combination|ht\s*\/\s*ft|half.?time.?\s*\/?.?full.?time|devre.*maç|maç.*devre|kombin|half.?time.?full.?time/.test(value))cats.add('combo');
  if(/correct score|exact score|scoreline|doğru skor|dogru skor|skor tahmini/.test(value))cats.add('score');
  if(/goal|goals|gol|over|under|alt.?üst|alt.?ust|btts|both teams|team total|to score|scorer|golcü|golcu|toplam gol|clean sheet|win to nil|penalty|penaltı/.test(value))cats.add('goals');
  if(!isHalf&&(/1x2|match result|full.?time result|match winner|winner|moneyline|double chance|draw no bet|handicap|maç sonucu|mac sonucu|çifte şans|cifte sans|fulltime/.test(value)))cats.add('ms');
  if(!cats.size)cats.add('other');
  return [...cats];
}
function iddaaPickerHTML(m,view){
  const data=view&&view.iddaa;
  if(view&&view.iddaaLoading&&!data)return '<div class="iddaa-picker-empty"><b>İddaa marketleri aranıyor…</b><p>Maç önü marketleri yükleniyor. Ekran açık kalacak.</p></div>';
  if(view&&view.iddaaError&&!data)return `<div class="iddaa-picker-empty">${esc(view.iddaaError)}<p><button type="button" class="iddaa-picker-tab active" data-iddaa-retry>Yeniden dene</button></p></div>`;
  if(!data||!Array.isArray(data.markets)||!data.markets.length){
    const message=data&&data.message?data.message:'Bu karşılaşma için İddaa market bilgisi bulunamadı.';
    return `<div class="iddaa-picker-empty"><b>İddaa marketleri</b><p>${esc(message)}</p></div>`;
  }
  const category=oddsPickerCategory||'all';
  const filtered=data.markets.filter(market=>category==='all'||iddaaMarketCategories(market).includes(category));
  if(!filtered.length)return '<div class="iddaa-picker-empty">Bu sekmede gösterilecek İddaa marketi bu maç için sağlanmamış.</div>';
  return filtered.map(market=>{
    const marketName=String(market.name||'Bahis türü');
    const marketType=String(market.type||'').toLowerCase();
    const marketPeriod=String(market.period||'').toLowerCase();
    const isMatchResult=String(market.id||'')==='match-result'||
      (marketType==='1x2'&&(marketPeriod==='fulltime'||(!marketPeriod&&/maç sonucu|mac sonucu|match result/i.test(marketName))));
    const outcomes=(market.outcomes||[]).map(out=>{
      const label=String(out.label||out.id||'Seçim');
      const selectionId=String(out.id||label).trim();
      const payload={marketId:String(market.id||market.marketId||marketName),marketName,marketType,marketPeriod,isMatchResult,selectionId,selectionName:label,odd:Number(out.odd)};
      return `<button type="button" class="iddaa-picker-outcome trackable" data-iddaa-pick="${esc(JSON.stringify(payload))}" aria-label="${esc(marketName)} · ${esc(label)} seçimini takibe al">${esc(label)} <span class="iddaa-select-hint">Takibe al</span></button>`;
    }).join('');
    return `<section class="iddaa-picker-market"><h4>${esc(marketName)}</h4>${market.details?`<small>${esc(market.details)}</small>`:''}<div class="iddaa-picker-outcomes">${outcomes||'<span class="iddaa-picker-empty">Seçenek bulunamadı</span>'}</div></section>`;
  }).join('');
}
function normalizeOddsPickerMatch(raw,id){
  const source=raw&&raw.match&&raw.match.fixture?raw.match:raw;
  const fixture=source&&source.fixture||{},teams=source&&source.teams||{};
  const home=teams.home||source&&source.home||{},away=teams.away||source&&source.away||{};
  if(!home.name||!away.name)return null;
  const status=fixture.status&&typeof fixture.status==='object'?fixture.status:{short:String(fixture.status||source.status||'NS'),elapsed:null};
  return {...source,fixture:{...fixture,id:fixture.id==null?id:fixture.id,date:fixture.date||source.date||new Date().toISOString(),status},
    teams:{...teams,home:{...home,name:String(home.name),raw:home.raw||home.name},away:{...away,name:String(away.name),raw:away.raw||away.name}},
    goals:source.goals||{home:null,away:null}};
}
function ensureOddsPickerVisible(){
  const root=$('#iddaaPicker');
  if(!root)return null;
  let panel=root.querySelector('.iddaa-picker-panel');
  if(!panel){panel=document.createElement('section');panel.className='iddaa-picker-panel';root.appendChild(panel);}
  let body=$('#iddaaPickerBody');
  if(!body){body=document.createElement('div');body.id='iddaaPickerBody';panel.appendChild(body);}
  else if(body.parentElement!==panel)panel.appendChild(body);
  let backdrop=root.querySelector('.iddaa-picker-backdrop');
  if(!backdrop){backdrop=document.createElement('div');backdrop.className='iddaa-picker-backdrop';backdrop.dataset.iddaaClose='';root.insertBefore(backdrop,panel);}
  if(root.parentElement!==document.body)document.body.appendChild(root);
  root.hidden=false;root.removeAttribute('hidden');document.body.classList.add('iddaa-open');
  Object.assign(root.style,{position:'fixed',inset:'0',top:'0',right:'0',bottom:'0',left:'0',zIndex:'2147483647',display:'flex',visibility:'visible',opacity:'1',pointerEvents:'auto',alignItems:'stretch',justifyContent:'stretch',padding:'0',margin:'0',width:'100vw',minWidth:'100vw',height:'100vh',minHeight:'100vh',maxHeight:'100vh',overflow:'hidden',boxSizing:'border-box',touchAction:'none'});
  Object.assign(backdrop.style,{position:'absolute',top:'0',right:'0',bottom:'0',left:'0',zIndex:'0',display:'block'});
  Object.assign(panel.style,{position:'relative',zIndex:'1',display:'flex',flex:'1 1 0%',flexDirection:'column',width:'100%',minWidth:'100%',height:'100vh',maxHeight:'100vh',minHeight:'0',margin:'0',padding:'0',border:'0',borderRadius:'0',overflow:'hidden',boxSizing:'border-box',background:'var(--bg, #111827)'});
  Object.assign(body.style,{display:'flex',flex:'1 1 0%',flexDirection:'column',width:'100%',height:'100%',maxHeight:'100%',minWidth:'0',minHeight:'0',overflow:'hidden',boxSizing:'border-box',padding:'calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-top)) 10px calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom))'});
  if(window.CSS&&CSS.supports&&CSS.supports('height','100dvh')){root.style.height='100dvh';root.style.minHeight='100dvh';panel.style.height='100dvh';panel.style.maxHeight='100dvh';}
  return {root,panel,body,backdrop};
}
function renderOddsPicker(){
  const shell=ensureOddsPickerVisible();if(!shell)return false;const {root,body}=shell;
  const m=oddsPickerMatch;if(!m){root.hidden=true;return false;}
  try{
    const view={iddaa:oddsPickerData,iddaaLoading:oddsPickerLoading,iddaaError:oddsPickerError};
    const tabs=[['all','Tümü'],['ms','MS'],['goals','Gol'],['half','Devre'],['score','Skor'],['combo','Kombo'],['corners','Korner'],['cards','Kart']];
    const tabHtml=tabs.map(([key,label])=>`<button type="button" class="iddaa-picker-tab${oddsPickerCategory===key?' active':''}" data-iddaa-tab="${key}" aria-pressed="${oddsPickerCategory===key?'true':'false'}">${label}</button>`).join('');
    const short=String(m.fixture&&m.fixture.status&&m.fixture.status.short||'NS');
    const status=oddsPickerData&&oddsPickerData.preMatchSnapshot?'Maç öncesi kaydedilmiş marketler':liveS(short)||HT.includes(short)?'Canlı maç · yalnızca maç önü marketleri':'İddaa market sonuçları';
    body.innerHTML=`<div class="odds-picker-head"><div><b>${esc(m.teams.home.name)} – ${esc(m.teams.away.name)}</b><small>İddaa marketleri · oranlar gösterilmez</small></div><button class="ibtn" data-iddaa-close aria-label="Kapat">×</button></div><nav class="iddaa-picker-tabs" aria-label="İddaa market kategorileri">${tabHtml}</nav><div class="iddaa-picker-scroll"><div class="iddaa-picker-status">${esc(status)}</div>${iddaaPickerHTML(m,view)}</div><p class="odds-picker-foot">Bu ekranda yalnızca bahis türleri ve sonuç seçenekleri gösterilir; oran değerleri gizlidir.</p>`;
    ensureOddsPickerVisible();root.hidden=false;return true;
  }catch(error){
    body.innerHTML=`<div class="odds-picker-head"><b>İddaa marketleri</b><button class="ibtn" data-iddaa-close aria-label="Kapat">×</button></div><div class="iddaa-picker-scroll"><div class="iddaa-picker-empty">Bu maçın market ekranı hazırlanırken bir hata oluştu. ${esc(String(error&&error.message||error).slice(0,160))}</div></div>`;
    ensureOddsPickerVisible();root.hidden=false;return false;
  }
}
async function openOddsPicker(raw){
  const shell=ensureOddsPickerVisible();if(!shell)return;
  const {root,body}=shell;
  body.innerHTML='<div class="odds-picker-head"><b>İddaa marketleri</b><button class="ibtn" data-iddaa-close aria-label="Kapat">×</button></div><div class="iddaa-picker-scroll"><div class="iddaa-picker-empty">Maç bilgisi hazırlanıyor…</div></div>';
  ensureOddsPickerVisible();
  root.setAttribute('role','dialog');root.setAttribute('aria-modal','true');root.setAttribute('aria-label','İddaa marketleri');
  let m=null;
  try{m=normalizeOddsPickerMatch(raw,raw&&raw.fixture&&raw.fixture.id);}
  catch(error){showLongPressFallback('Maç bilgisi hazırlanamadı: '+String(error&&error.message||error).slice(0,120));return;}
  if(!m){showLongPressFallback('Bu maçın takım bilgileri bulunamadı. Maç satırını yenileyip tekrar dene.');return;}
  const requestId=++oddsPickerRequestId;oddsPickerMatch=m;oddsPickerData=null;oddsPickerLoading=true;oddsPickerError='';oddsPickerCategory='all';
  renderOddsPicker();
  let watchdog=null,progressTimer=null;
  try{
    const pending=requestIddaaOdds(m);
    const timeout=new Promise((_,reject)=>{watchdog=setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error('OddsPapi 40 saniye içinde yanıt vermedi. Bağlantıyı kontrol edip yeniden deneyebilirsin.')),41000);});
    progressTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
      if(requestId===oddsPickerRequestId&&oddsPickerLoading){
        oddsPickerError='';
        const status=$('#iddaaPickerBody .iddaa-picker-empty');
        if(status)status.innerHTML='<b>İstek sürüyor…</b><p>Maç önü İddaa arşivi sorgulanıyor. Sonuç gelene kadar ekran açık kalacak.</p>';
      }
    },3000);
    const result=await Promise.race([pending,timeout]);
    if(requestId!==oddsPickerRequestId)return;
    oddsPickerData=result;
    if(!oddsPickerData||!Array.isArray(oddsPickerData.markets)||!oddsPickerData.markets.length)oddsPickerError=oddsPickerData&&oddsPickerData.message||'Bu maç için İddaa marketi bulunamadı.';
  }catch(e){
    if(requestId===oddsPickerRequestId)oddsPickerError='İddaa marketleri alınamadı: '+String(e&&e.message||e).slice(0,160);
  }finally{
    clearTimeout(watchdog);clearTimeout(progressTimer);
    if(requestId===oddsPickerRequestId){oddsPickerLoading=false;renderOddsPicker();}
  }
}
function closeOddsPicker(){oddsPickerRequestId++;oddsPickerMatch=null;oddsPickerData=null;oddsPickerLoading=false;oddsPickerError='';suppressLongPressClickPending=false;const root=$('#iddaaPicker');if(root){root.hidden=true;root.style.display='none';}document.body.classList.remove('iddaa-open');}
function saveIddaaPick(data){
  const m=oddsPickerMatch||(cur&&cur.kind==='match'&&(cur.data||cur.base));if(!m||!data)return;
  const marketId=String(data.marketId||'');
  const selection=String(data.selectionId||'').trim();
  if(!selection){alert('Bu market seçimi okunamadı. Başka bir seçenek seç.');return;}
  const fixture=m.fixture||{},id=String(fixture.id||'');
  const exists=trackedPicks.some(p=>p.fixtureId===id&&p.selectionId===selection&&p.marketId===marketId);
  if(exists){alert('Bu seçim zaten takip listende var.');return;}
  const pick={id:String(Date.now())+'-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8),fixtureId:id,pitchMatchId:String(m.pitch&&m.pitch.id||''),leagueCode:matchLeagueCode(m),home:m.teams.home.name,away:m.teams.away.name,homeId:String(m.teams.home.id||''),awayId:String(m.teams.away.id||''),league:lgT(m.league).name,kickoff:fixture.date,marketId,marketName:data.marketName,marketType:String(data.marketType||''),marketPeriod:String(data.marketPeriod||''),isMatchResult:data.isMatchResult===true,selectionId:selection,selectionName:data.selectionName||selection,odd:Number(data.odd),resolved:false,createdAt:Date.now(),lastCheck:0};
  trackedPicks.push(pick);persistTrackedPicks();render();
  if(oddsPickerMatch)closeOddsPicker();
  if(cur&&cur.kind==='match'&&cur.tab==='iddaa')renderMatch();
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const tracker=document.querySelector('#list .tracked-picks');if(tracker){tracker.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'start'});tracker.classList.add('tracked-pick-added');setTimeout(()=>tracker.classList.remove('tracked-pick-added'),1800);}});
}
function normalizePickToken(value){return String(value||'').replace(/[ıİ]/g,'i').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9.x+-]+/g,' ').trim();}
function evaluateTrackedPick(p,home,away,match){
  const total=home+away,market=normalizePickToken([p.marketName,p.marketType,p.marketId].join(' ')),selection=normalizePickToken(p.selectionName||p.selectionId),selectionId=normalizePickToken(p.selectionId);
  const oneXtwo=()=>{const actual=home>away?'1':home<away?'2':'x';const candidates=[selection.replace(/\s/g,''),selectionId.replace(/\s/g,'')];for(let picked of candidates){if(/^(home|homewin|evsahibi)$/.test(picked))picked='1';if(/^(draw|tie|beraberlik)$/.test(picked))picked='x';if(/^(away|awaywin|deplasman)$/.test(picked))picked='2';if(['1','x','2'].includes(picked))return picked===actual;}return null;};
  if(p.isMatchResult||/match result|mac sonucu|1x2/.test(market))return oneXtwo();
  if(/double chance|cifte sans/.test(market)){const actual=home>away?'1':home<away?'2':'x',picked=selection.replace(/\s/g,'');if(picked==='1x'||picked==='1/x')return actual==='1'||actual==='x';if(picked==='x2'||picked==='x/2')return actual==='x'||actual==='2';if(picked==='12'||picked==='1/2')return actual==='1'||actual==='2';return null;}
  if(/both teams to score|karsilikli gol|kg var|both score/.test(market)){const yes=home>0&&away>0;if(/^(yes|evet|var)$/.test(selection))return yes;if(/^(no|hayir|yok)$/.test(selection))return !yes;return null;}
  if(/correct score|dogru skor|exact score/.test(market)){const found=selection.match(/(\d+)\s*[-:]\s*(\d+)/);return found?Number(found[1])===home&&Number(found[2])===away:null;}
  if(/odd even|tek cift|total goals odd|goals odd/.test(market)){if(/^(odd|tek)$/.test(selection))return total%2===1;if(/^(even|cift)$/.test(selection))return total%2===0;return null;}
  if(/over under|total goals|toplam gol|alt ust|goals total/.test(market)||/^(over|under|alt|ust)\s*\d/.test(selection)){
    const line=selection.match(/(?:over|under|alt|ust)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/)||market.match(/(?:over|under|alt|ust)\s*(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/)||selection.match(/(\d+\.\d+)/);
    if(!line)return null;const threshold=Number(line[1]);if(!Number.isFinite(threshold))return null;
    if(/\b(over|ust)\b/.test(selection))return total>threshold;
    if(/\b(under|alt)\b/.test(selection))return total<threshold;
    return null;
  }
  return null;
}
async function refreshTrackedPickResults(){
  const now=Date.now();const due=trackedPicks.filter(p=>!p.resolved&&Date.parse(p.kickoff)<=now&&now-(p.lastCheck||0)>25000);if(!due.length)return;
  await Promise.allSettled(due.map(async p=>{
    p.lastCheck=now;try{
      let url;if(p.pitchMatchId)url='/pitch-match-detail?id='+encodeURIComponent(p.pitchMatchId)+'&code='+encodeURIComponent(p.leagueCode||'');
      else url='/match?id='+encodeURIComponent(p.fixtureId)+'&home='+encodeURIComponent(p.home)+'&away='+encodeURIComponent(p.away)+'&date='+encodeURIComponent(p.kickoff);
      const j=await api(url),m=j.match||j,short=String(m.fixture&&m.fixture.status&&m.fixture.status.short||'').toUpperCase();
      if(['CANC','ABD','AWD','WO'].includes(short)){p.void=true;p.resolved=true;p.correct=false;p.score='İptal';}
      else if(DONE.includes(short)&&m.goals&&m.goals.home!=null&&m.goals.away!=null){
        const home=Number(m.goals.home),away=Number(m.goals.away);p.score=home+' - '+away;
        const evaluated=evaluateTrackedPick(p,home,away,m);
        if(evaluated===true||evaluated===false){p.correct=evaluated;p.resolved=true;p.resultUnknown=false;p.resolvedAt=Date.now();}
        else{p.resultUnknown=true;p.resolved=false;}
      }
    }catch(_){}
  }));persistTrackedPicks();render();
}
function matchGoalScorerSide(m,side){
  const team=m.teams&&m.teams[side];if(!team)return [];
  const byPlayer=new Map();
  mergeMatchEventHistory([],m.events||[]).forEach(e=>{
    if(String(e.type||'').toLowerCase()!=='goal')return;
    const detail=String(e.detail||'').toLowerCase();
    if(/missed|disallow|cancel/.test(detail))return;
    const eventTeam=e.team||{};let belongs=false;
    if(eventTeam.id!=null&&team.id!=null&&String(eventTeam.id)===String(team.id))belongs=true;
    if(!belongs&&(eventTeam.id==='h'&&side==='home'||eventTeam.id==='a'&&side==='away'))belongs=true;
    if(!belongs&&eventTeam.name&&team.name){
      const norm=x=>String(x||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();
      belongs=norm(eventTeam.name)===norm(team.name);
    }
    if(!belongs)return;
    const name=String(e.player&&e.player.name||'').trim();if(!name)return;
    const key=name.normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();
    const time=e.time||{},minute=time.elapsed==null?'':String(time.elapsed)+(time.extra? '+'+time.extra:'')+"'";
    if(!byPlayer.has(key))byPlayer.set(key,{name,minutes:[]});
    if(minute)byPlayer.get(key).minutes.push(minute);
  });
  const minuteOrder=value=>{const bits=String(value||'').replace(/'/g,'').split('+').map(Number);return (bits[0]||0)*100+(bits[1]||0)};
  return [...byPlayer.values()].map(x=>({...x,minutes:x.minutes.sort((a,b)=>minuteOrder(a)-minuteOrder(b))})).sort((a,b)=>minuteOrder(a.minutes[0])-minuteOrder(b.minutes[0]));
}
function matchGoalScorersHTML(m){
  const sideHTML=(side)=>matchGoalScorerSide(m,side).map(x=>`<div class="goal-scorer">${esc(trPlayerName(x.name))}${x.minutes.length?`<span class="goal-minutes">${esc(x.minutes.join(' '))}</span>`:''}</div>`).join('');
  return `<div class="goal-strip"><div class="goal-side home">${sideHTML('home')}</div><div class="goal-ball" aria-label="Gol">⚽</div><div class="goal-side away">${sideHTML('away')}</div></div>`;
}
const pitchPhotoSrcCache=new Map();
document.addEventListener('load',e=>{
  const img=e.target;
  if(!(img instanceof HTMLImageElement)||!img.matches('img[data-pitch-photo]')||!img.naturalWidth)return;
  const key=img.dataset.pitchPhoto||'';
  if(key){pitchPhotoSrcCache.set(key,img.currentSrc||img.src);if(pitchPhotoSrcCache.size>1500)pitchPhotoSrcCache.delete(pitchPhotoSrcCache.keys().next().value);delete img.dataset.cacheRetry}
},true);
document.addEventListener('error',e=>{
  const img=e.target;
  if(!(img instanceof HTMLImageElement)||!img.matches('img[data-pitch-photo]'))return;
  const key=img.dataset.pitchPhoto||'',cached=key&&pitchPhotoSrcCache.get(key);
  if(cached&&!img.dataset.cacheRetry&&(img.currentSrc||img.src)!==cached){img.dataset.cacheRetry='1';img.src=cached;return}
  const showFallback=()=>{
    img.dataset.photoFailed='1';
    const parent=img.parentElement;
    if(parent&&parent.classList.contains('dot')&&!parent.querySelector('.player-fallback')){
      const fallback=document.createElement('span');fallback.className='player-fallback';fallback.textContent=img.dataset.photoFallback||'•';parent.insertBefore(fallback,img.nextSibling);
    }
    if(parent&&parent.classList.contains('bench-player')&&!parent.querySelector('.bench-avatar')){
      const fallback=document.createElement('div');fallback.className='bench-avatar';fallback.textContent=img.dataset.photoFallback||'•';parent.insertBefore(fallback,img.nextSibling);
    }
    img.style.display='none';
  };
  // A HTTPS URL may still have gone stale upstream. Ask the Worker for one verified
  // replacement by player ID/name/team before falling back to the shirt number.
  if(!img.dataset.pitchResolveTried){
    const card=img.closest('.pp'),bench=img.closest('.bench-player'),nameNode=bench&&bench.querySelector('.bench-name');
    const name=card&&card.dataset.playerProfile||nameNode&&nameNode.dataset.playerProfile||'';
    const team=card&&card.dataset.playerTeam||nameNode&&nameNode.dataset.playerTeam||'';
    const code=card&&card.dataset.pitchCode||nameNode&&nameNode.dataset.pitchCode||'';
    const player=card&&card.dataset.matchPlayerId||nameNode&&nameNode.dataset.matchPlayerId||'';
    const pos=card&&card.dataset.playerPos||nameNode&&nameNode.dataset.playerPos||'';
    if(name&&team&&code){
      img.dataset.pitchResolveTried='1';img.style.opacity='0';
      const query='/pitch-player-photo?name='+encodeURIComponent(name)+'&team='+encodeURIComponent(team)+'&code='+encodeURIComponent(code)+'&player='+encodeURIComponent(player)+'&pos='+encodeURIComponent(pos);
      api(query).then(result=>{
        const photo=String(result&&result.photo||'');
        if(/^https:\/\//i.test(photo)&&(photo!==img.src||img.naturalWidth)){img.style.display='';img.style.opacity='1';img.src=photo;}
        else showFallback();
      }).catch(showFallback);
      return;
    }
  }
  showFallback();
},true);
async function loadMatchEventPhotos(my){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='match'||my.eventPhotoLoading)return;
  const m=my.data||my.base;if(!m||!Array.isArray(m.events)||!m.events.length)return;
  const code=matchLeagueCode(m);if(!code)return;
  const known=new Map();
  const addPhoto=(p)=>{if(!p||typeof p!=='object')return;const name=p.name||p.full_name||p.short_name||'';const photo=p.image_url||p.photo||p.logo||'';if(name&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(String(photo)))known.set(playerNameKey(name),photo)};
  for(const row of matchPlayerSource(m))addPhoto(row.player||row.person||row);
  for(const side of (m.lineups||[]))for(const item of [...(side.startXI||[]),...(side.substitutes||[])])addPhoto(item.player||item);
  const jobs=[],seen=new Set();
  for(const event of m.events){for(const key of ['player','assist','sub_in_player','player_in','substitute','in_player']){let p=event&&event[key];if(!p)continue;if(typeof p!=='object'){p={name:String(p)};event[key]=p}const actor=p,name=actor.name||actor.full_name||actor.short_name||event[key+'_name']||'';if(!name)continue;const k=playerNameKey(name);if(!k||seen.has(k))continue;seen.add(k);if(known.has(k)){actor.image_url=known.get(k);continue}if(actor.image_url||actor.photo||actor.logo)continue;jobs.push({actor,name,team:event.team&&event.team.name||'',id:actor.id||actor.player_id||event.player_id||'',pos:actor.position||''});}}
  const pending=jobs.filter(job=>!my.eventPhotoTried?.has(job.name));if(!pending.length)return;
  my.eventPhotoTried=my.eventPhotoTried||new Set();pending.forEach(job=>my.eventPhotoTried.add(job.name));my.eventPhotoLoading=true;
  try{
    let cursor=0;await Promise.all(Array.from({length:Math.min(4,pending.length)},async()=>{while(cursor<pending.length){const job=pending[cursor++];const team=job.team||((eventSideNameForPhoto(m,job.name,job.id))||'');if(!team)continue;const params=new URLSearchParams({name:job.name,team,code,season:String(matchSeasonYear(m)),player:String(job.id||''),pos:String(job.pos||'')});try{const result=await api('/pitch-player-photo?'+params.toString());const photo=String(result&&result.photo||'');if(/^https?:\/\//i.test(photo)&&cur===my){job.actor.image_url=photo;job.actor.photo=photo;}}catch(_){}}}));
  }finally{my.eventPhotoLoading=false;if(cur===my&&my.data===m&&jobs.some(job=>job.actor.image_url||job.actor.photo))renderMatch()}
}
function eventSideNameForPhoto(m,name,playerId=''){
  const key=playerNameKey(name),id=String(playerId||'');
  const same=(p)=>{const x=p&&typeof p==='object'?p:{};const pid=String(x.id||x.player_id||'');const pname=x.name||x.full_name||x.short_name||x.player_name||'';return (id&&pid===id)||(key&&pname&&playerNameKey(pname)===key)};
  const event=(m.events||[]).find(e=>['player','assist','sub_in_player','player_in','substitute','in_player'].some(field=>same(e&&e[field])));
  if(event&&event.team&&event.team.name)return event.team.name;
  for(const side of (m.lineups||[])){
    for(const item of [...(side.startXI||[]),...(side.substitutes||[])]){
      const p=item&& (item.player||item)||{};
      if(same(p))return side.team&&side.team.name||'';
    }
  }
  const home=m.teams&&m.teams.home||{},away=m.teams&&m.teams.away||{};
  for(const row of matchPlayerSource(m)){
    const p=row&& (row.player||row.person)||row||{};
    if(!same(p)&&!same(row))continue;
    const teamId=String(row&& (row.team_id||row.team&&row.team.id)||p.team_id||'');
    const teamName=row&&row.team&&row.team.name||row&&row.team_name||p.team&&p.team.name||'';
    if(teamId&&String(home.id||'')===teamId)return home.name||teamName;
    if(teamId&&String(away.id||'')===teamId)return away.name||teamName;
    if(teamName)return teamName;
  }
  return '';
}
function renderMatch(){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='match')return;
  const bodyEl=$('#shBody');
  const m=cur.data||cur.base;
  if(!m){$('#shBody').innerHTML='<div class="empty">'+(cur.err?nice(cur.err):'Yükleniyor…')+'</div>';return}
  if(cur.matchPlayerId){$('#shBody').innerHTML=matchPlayerPanel(m);return}
  const s=m.fixture.status||{},g=m.goals||{},statusConfirmed=!!cur.data,lv=statusConfirmed&&liveS(s.short);
  const started=statusConfirmed&&(lv||HT.includes(s.short)||DONE.includes(s.short)||['SUSP','ABD'].includes(s.short));
  const when=new Date(m.fixture.date).toLocaleString('tr-TR',{day:'numeric',month:'long',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'});
  const venue=m.fixture.venue&&m.fixture.venue.name?' · '+esc(m.fixture.venue.name):'';
  const belowScore=started?matchGoalScorersHTML(m):`<div class="sb-info">${esc(when)}${venue}${m.fallback?' · <small></small>':''}</div>`;
  const head=`<div class="sb"><div class="sb-lg">${esc(lgT(m.league).name)}${(()=>{const t=lgT(m.league);return t.country&&!t.name.toLowerCase().includes(t.country.toLowerCase())?' · '+esc(t.country):''})()}${m.league.round?' · '+esc(m.league.round):''}</div>
    <div class="sb-row"><div class="sb-t" data-team="${esc(teamKey(m,'home'))}">${img(m.teams.home.logo)}<div>${esc(m.teams.home.name)}</div></div>
    <div class="sb-s ${lv?'live':''}"><div class="big">${g.home==null?'-':g.home} - ${g.away==null?'-':g.away}</div><div class="sm">${esc(statusConfirmed?stText(s,m.fixture.date,m.fixture.id):'Durum doğrulanıyor…')}</div></div>
    <div class="sb-t" data-team="${esc(teamKey(m,'away'))}">${img(m.teams.away.logo)}<div>${esc(m.teams.away.name)}</div></div></div>
    ${belowScore}</div>`;
  const tabs=[['ozet','Özet'],['kadro','Kadro'],['stat','İstatistik'],['iddaa','İddaa'],['h2h','İkili Rekabet'],['bilgi','Maç Bilgisi'],['puan','Puan Durumu']].map(([k,l])=>`<div class="${cur.tab===k?'on':''}" data-mt="${k}">${l}</div>`).join('');
  let body;
  if(!cur.data)body='<div class="empty">'+(cur.err?nice(cur.err):'Detaylar yükleniyor…')+'</div>';
  else if(cur.tab==='ozet'){const hasEvents=Array.isArray(m.events)&&m.events.length>0;body=hasEvents?evHTML(m):(cur.detailLoading?'<div class="empty"><b>Maç bilgisi hazır.</b><p>Skor ve dakika yukarıda gösteriliyor. Maç olayları arka planda alınıyor.</p></div>':evHTML(m));}
  else if(cur.tab==='kadro')body=luHTML(m);
  else if(cur.tab==='stat')body=statHTML(m);
  else if(cur.tab==='iddaa')body=iddaaHTML(m,cur);
  else if(cur.tab==='h2h'){body=pitchH2HHTML(m,cur);if(!hasMatchH2HData(m.pitch&&m.pitch.h2h)&&!hasMatchH2HData(cur.h2h)&&!cur.h2hLoaded&&!cur.h2hLoading)loadMatchH2H(cur);}
  else if(cur.tab==='bilgi')body=matchInfoHTML(m);
  else body=matchStandingsHTML(m);
  bodyEl.innerHTML=head+(tabs?'<div class="stabs match-tabs">'+tabs+'</div>':'')+body+(cur.err&&cur.data?'<div class="empty"><small>Bu bölüm şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</small></div>':'');
  if(cur.tab==='ozet'&&cur.data&&Array.isArray(m.events)&&m.events.length)loadMatchEventPhotos(cur);
  if(cur.tab==='puan'&&cur.data&&!cur.matchStLoading&&!cur.matchStLoadedKey&&!cur.matchStErr)loadMatchStandings();
  if(cur.tab==='iddaa'&&cur.data&&!cur.iddaaLoading&&!cur.iddaaLoaded)loadIddaaOdds(cur);
}

/* --- Maç bilgisi ve maça ait puan tablosu --- */
function matchSeasonYear(m){
  const explicit=Number(m&&m.league&&m.league.season);if(Number.isInteger(explicit)&&explicit>=1900)return explicit;
  const d=new Date(m&&m.fixture&&m.fixture.date||'');if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return currentTeamSeason();
  return d.getUTCMonth()>=6?d.getUTCFullYear():d.getUTCFullYear()-1;
}
function matchLeagueCode(m){
  const l=m&&m.league||{};let code=String(l.code||'').trim();if(code)return code;
  const id=String(l.id||'');const af=String(l.afId||l.apiFootballId||'');
  if(/^\d+$/.test(af))return 'af-'+af;
  const idMatch=id.match(/^(?:a:)?(\d+)$/);if(idMatch)return 'af-'+idMatch[1];
  const n=playerNameKey(l.name||'');
  const aliases=[[/^(trendyol )?(turkish )?super lig$|^super lig$|^super league turkey$/,'tur.1'],[/^(trendyol )?(tff )?1 lig$|^first league turkey$/,'tur.2'],[/^(nesine )?(tff )?2 lig$/,'af-318'],[/^(nesine )?(tff )?3 lig$/,'af-321'],[/^(ziraat )?turkish cup$|^(ziraat )?turkiye kupasi$/,'af-320'],[/^(turkish )?super cup$|^super kupa$/,'af-323'],[/^premier league$|^english premier league$/,'eng.1'],[/^la liga$|^laliga$/,'esp.1'],[/^bundesliga$/,'ger.1'],[/^serie a$/,'ita.1'],[/^ligue 1$/,'fra.1'],[/^eredivisie$/,'ned.1'],[/^liga portugal$|^primeira liga$/,'por.1'],[/^champions league$|^uefa champions league$/,'uefa.champions'],[/^europa league$|^uefa europa league$/,'uefa.europa'],[/^conference league$|^uefa conference league$/,'uefa.europa.conf']];
  const found=aliases.find(([re])=>re.test(n));return found?found[1]:'';
}
const TR_TV_2026_27=[
  {re:/^(tur\.1|tur\.2|af-203|af-204|af-322|af-319)$/i,name:'beIN SPORTS / TOD'},
  {re:/^(tur\.cup|tur\.super_cup|af-206|af-320|af-323)$/i,name:'ATV / A Spor'},
  {re:/^(tur\.3|tur\.4|af-318|af-321|af-532)$/i,name:'Tivibu Spor'},
  {re:/^(eng\.1|fra\.1|af-39|af-61)$/i,name:'beIN SPORTS / TOD'},
  {re:/^(esp\.1|ger\.1|ita\.1|ned\.1|por\.1|sco\.1|ksa\.1|af-140|af-78|af-135|af-88|af-94|af-179|af-307)$/i,name:'S Sport / S Sport Plus'},
  {re:/^(uefa\.champions|uefa\.europa|uefa\.europa\.conf|uefa\.super_cup|af-2|af-3|af-848)$/i,name:'TRT / tabii'},
  {re:/^(bra\.1|arg\.1|conmebol\.libertadores|conmebol\.sudamericana|af-71|af-128)$/i,name:'D-Smart / Spor Smart'}
];
function matchTurkeyBroadcast(m){
  const rows=Array.isArray(m&&m.broadcasts)?m.broadcasts:[];
  const local=rows.filter(b=>{const c=playerNameKey(b.country||b.marketCountry||b.region||'');return /^(turkey|turkiye|tr|turkiye$)/.test(c)||c.includes('turkiye')||c.includes('turkey')});
  const names=local.map(b=>b.name||b.channel||'').filter(Boolean);
  if(names.length)return [...new Set(names)].join(' / ');
  const season=matchSeasonYear(m);const nowSeason=season===currentTeamSeason();
  if(nowSeason){const code=matchLeagueCode(m);const rule=TR_TV_2026_27.find(x=>x.re.test(code));if(rule)return rule.name;}
  return 'Bu maç için Türkiye yayın kanalı kaynağa eklenmemiş';
}
function matchInfoHTML(m){
  const f=m.fixture||{},v=f.venue||{},league=m.league||{};
  const date=new Date(f.date||'');const valid=!Number.isNaN(date.getTime());
  const dateText=valid?date.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{timeZone:'Europe/Istanbul',weekday:'long',day:'numeric',month:'long',year:'numeric'}):'—';
  const timeText=valid?date.toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR',{timeZone:'Europe/Istanbul',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}):'—';
  const referee=typeof m.referee==='string'?m.referee:(m.referee&& (m.referee.name||m.referee.displayName))||((m.officials||[]).find(o=>/referee|hakem/i.test(String(o.type||o.role||'')))||{}).name||'';
  const comp=[league.name||'Bilinmiyor',league.round||''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  const rows=[['Kulvar',comp],['Şehir',v.city||v.location||m.city||'—'],['Stadyum',v.name||m.stadium||'—'],['Tarih',dateText],['Saat (Türkiye)',timeText],['Türkiye yayın kanalı',matchTurkeyBroadcast(m)],['Hakem',referee||'—']];
  return '<div class="match-info">'+rows.map(([label,value])=>`<div class="match-info-row"><span>${esc(label)}</span><b>${esc(String(value))}</b></div>`).join('')+'<div class="match-info-note">Yayıncı organizasyonun 2026-27 haklarına göre tahmin edilir; maçın kesin TV kanalı yayın akışına göre değişebilir.</div></div>';
}
const matchStCache=new Map();
async function loadMatchStandings(){
  const my=cur;if(!my||my.kind!=='match'||my.tab!=='puan'||!my.data)return;
  const code=matchLeagueCode(my.data),season=matchSeasonYear(my.data),key=code+'|'+season;
  if(!code){my.matchStErr='Puan durumu şu anda görüntülenemiyor.';renderMatch();return;}
  if(my.matchStLoading||my.matchStLoadedKey===key)return;
  my.matchStCode=code;my.matchStSeason=season;my.matchStLoading=true;my.matchStErr='';my.matchStLoadedKey=key;renderMatch();
  try{let tables=matchStCache.get(key);if(!tables){const j=await api('/standings?league='+encodeURIComponent(code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season));tables=j.tables||[];if(tables.length)matchStCache.set(key,tables)}if(cur!==my)return;my.matchTables=tables;my.matchStLoading=false;}
  catch(e){if(cur!==my)return;my.matchStLoading=false;my.matchStErr=e.message||String(e);}
  renderMatch();
}
function matchStandingsHTML(m){
  if(cur.matchStLoading)return '<div class="empty">'+esc(m.league.name||'Kulvar')+' puan durumu yükleniyor…</div>';
  if(cur.matchStErr)return '<div class="empty">'+esc(cur.matchStErr)+'</div>';
  if(!cur.matchStLoadedKey)return '<div class="empty">Puan durumu yükleniyor…</div>';
  if(!cur.matchTables||!cur.matchTables.length)return '<div class="empty">Bu kulvar için lig tipi puan tablosu bulunmuyor. Kupa ve eleme maçlarında puan tablosu olmayabilir.</div>';
  return '<div class="match-standings-note">Bu maçın iki takımı puan tablosunda sarı renkle işaretlendi.</div>'+stTables(cur.matchTables,cur.matchStCode,[m.teams.home,m.teams.away]);
}

/* --- Puan durumu --- */
const stCache={};
async function openStandings(code){
  showSheet();
  cur={kind:'st',id:code||STL[0][0],tables:null,err:''};
  $('#shTitle').textContent='Puan Durumu';
  loadSt();
}
async function loadSt(){
  const my=cur;if(!my||my.kind!=='st')return;
  my.tables=null;my.err='';renderSt();
  try{
    let tables=stCache[my.id];
    if(!tables){const j=await api('/standings?league='+encodeURIComponent(my.id));tables=j.tables||[];if(tables.length)stCache[my.id]=tables}
    if(cur!==my)return;
    my.tables=tables;
  }catch(e){if(cur!==my)return;my.err=e.message||String(e)}
  renderSt();
}
function sameTeam(a,b){if(!a||!b)return false;if(a.id&&b.id&&String(a.id)===String(b.id))return true;const x=nk(a.raw||a.name),y=nk(b.raw||b.name);return !!x&&!!y&&(x===y||x.includes(y)||y.includes(x))}
function stTables(tables,code,me){return tables.map(t=>{
    const rows=t.rows.map(r=>{
      const d=(r.desc||'').toLowerCase();
      const c=/champions|promotion/.test(d)?'cl':/europa|conference|qualif/.test(d)?'eu':/relegation/.test(d)?'rl':'';
      const df=r.diff==null?'':(r.diff>0?'+':'')+r.diff;
      const isMe=(Array.isArray(me)?me:[me]).filter(Boolean).some(team=>sameTeam(r.team,team));
      return `<tr class="${c}${isMe?' me':''}"><td class="rk">${r.rank}</td><td class="l" data-team="${esc(JSON.stringify([r.team.id||'',code,r.team.raw||r.team.name,r.team.name,'',r.team.afId||'']))}">${img(r.team.logo)}${esc(r.team.name)}</td><td>${r.played==null?'':r.played}</td><td>${r.win==null?'':r.win}</td><td>${r.draw==null?'':r.draw}</td><td>${r.lose==null?'':r.lose}</td><td>${df}</td><td class="pt">${r.points==null?'':r.points}</td></tr>`;
    }).join('');
    return (t.name?`<div class="gt">${esc(t.name)}</div>`:'')+`<table class="tb"><tr><th>#</th><th class="l">Takım</th><th>O</th><th>G</th><th>B</th><th>M</th><th>Av</th><th>P</th></tr>${rows}</table>`;
  }).join('')+'<div class="lgd"><span style="color:var(--acc)">▌ Şampiyonlar Ligi / yükselme</span><span style="color:var(--blue)">▌ Avrupa</span><span style="color:var(--live)">▌ Küme düşme</span></div>';}
function renderSt(){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='st')return;
  const known=STL.some(x=>x[0]===cur.id);
  const list=known?STL:[[cur.id,cur.id],...STL];
  const chips='<div class="chips">'+list.map(([id,n])=>`<div class="chip ${id===cur.id?'on':''}" data-sl="${esc(id)}">${esc(n)}</div>`).join('')+'</div>';
  let body;
  if(cur.err)body='<div class="empty">'+nice(cur.err)+'</div>';
  else if(!cur.tables)body='<div class="empty">Yükleniyor…</div>';
  else if(!cur.tables.length)body='<div class="empty">Puan durumu şu anda görüntülenemiyor.<br><small>Kupa ve eleme turnuvalarında puan tablosu olmayabilir.</small></div>';
  else body=stTables(cur.tables,cur.id,null);
  $('#shBody').innerHTML=chips+body;
  const on=$('#shBody .chip.on');if(on&&on.scrollIntoView)on.scrollIntoView({inline:'center',block:'nearest'});
  const nm=(list.find(x=>x[0]===cur.id)||[])[1];
  $('#shTitle').textContent=(nm||'Puan Durumu')+' – Puan Durumu';
}
function loadLeagueData(my){
  const season=Number(my.season)||currentTeamSeason();
  api('/league?code='+encodeURIComponent(my.code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)).then(j=>{if(cur===my){my.data=j;my.seasons=j.seasons||my.seasons||[];renderLeague()}}).catch(e=>{if(cur===my){my.err=e.message||String(e);renderLeague()}});
  loadPitchFixtures(my);
}
async function loadPitchFixtures(my){
  if(!my||my.pitchFixturesLoading||my.pitchFixturesLoaded)return;
  const token=(my.pitchFixtureRequest||0)+1,season=Number(my.season)||currentTeamSeason();my.pitchFixtureRequest=token;
  my.pitchFixturesLoading=true;my.pitchFixturesError='';renderLeague();
  try{const j=await api('/pitch-fixtures?code='+encodeURIComponent(my.code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season));if(cur!==my||token!==my.pitchFixtureRequest||Number(my.season)!==season)return;my.pitchFixtures=j.fixtures||[];my.pitchFixturesLoaded=true;if(!my.pitchFixtures.length)my.pitchFixturesError='PitchAPI bu lig/sezon için fikstür döndürmedi.'}
  catch(e){if(cur!==my||token!==my.pitchFixtureRequest)return;my.pitchFixturesError=e&&e.message||String(e)}
  finally{if(cur===my&&token===my.pitchFixtureRequest){my.pitchFixturesLoading=false;renderLeague()}}
}

function mergePitchLeaderRows(map,rows){
  (rows||[]).forEach(r=>{
    const id=String(r.playerId||r.player||'').trim();if(!id)return;
    let old=map.get(id);
    if(!old){old={playerId:id,player:r.player||'',logo:r.logo||'',value:0,teams:new Set()};map.set(id,old)}
    old.value+=Number(r.value)||0;
    if(!old.player&&r.player)old.player=r.player;
    if(!old.logo&&r.logo)old.logo=r.logo;
    String(r.team||'').split(' / ').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(x=>old.teams.add(x));
  });
}
function pitchLeaderRows(map){return [...map.values()].map(r=>({playerId:r.playerId,player:r.player,logo:r.logo,team:[...r.teams].join(' / '),value:r.value,source:'PitchAPI'})).sort((a,b)=>b.value-a.value||String(a.player).localeCompare(String(b.player),'tr')).slice(0,50).map((r,i)=>({...r,rank:i+1}))}
async function loadPitchLeaders(my){
  if(!my||!my.data||my.pitchLoading||my.pitchLoaded)return;
  my.pitchLoading=true;my.pitchError='';my.pitchProgress='Sezon maçları hazırlanıyor…';
  my.pitchGoals=new Map();my.pitchAssists=new Map();
  renderLeague();
  let offset=0,total=0,failed=0,done=false,loops=0;
  try{
    while(!done&&loops++<40){
      const j=await api('/pitch-leaders?code='+encodeURIComponent(my.code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(Number(my.season)||currentTeamSeason())+'&offset='+offset);
      total=Number(j.totalMatches)||0;failed+=Number(j.failedMatches)||0;
      mergePitchLeaderRows(my.pitchGoals,j.goals);mergePitchLeaderRows(my.pitchAssists,j.assists);
      const goals=pitchLeaderRows(my.pitchGoals),assists=pitchLeaderRows(my.pitchAssists);
      if(goals.length)my.data.goals=goals;
      if(assists.length)my.data.assists=assists;
      my.pitchProgress=(Math.min(Number(j.nextOffset)||0,total))+' / '+total+' maç işlendi';
      done=!!j.done;
      if(!done){const next=Number(j.nextOffset);if(!Number.isInteger(next)||next<=offset)throw new Error('İstatistik sayfalaması ilerleyemedi.');offset=next}
    }
    if(!done)throw new Error('Bu sezonun maçları tek seferde tamamlanamayacak kadar fazla. Tekrar deneyin.');
    const goals=pitchLeaderRows(my.pitchGoals),assists=pitchLeaderRows(my.pitchAssists);
    if(goals.length){my.data.goals=goals;my.data.errors=my.data.errors||{};my.data.errors.goals=''}
    if(assists.length){my.data.assists=assists;my.data.errors=my.data.errors||{};my.data.errors.assists=''}
    if(failed)my.pitchError=failed+' maçın oyuncu istatistikleri kaynaktan alınamadı; sıralama eksik olabilir.';
    else if(!goals.length&&!assists.length)my.pitchError='Bu lig ve sezon için oyuncu istatistikleri şu anda görüntülenemiyor.';
    my.pitchLoaded=true;
  }catch(e){my.pitchError=e&&e.message||String(e)}
  finally{my.pitchLoading=false;my.pitchProgress='';renderLeague()}
}

const PITCH_LEAGUE_METRICS=[['goals','Gol'],['assists','Asist'],['rating','Ortalama puan'],['starts','İlk 11'],['minutes','Dakika'],['yellow','Sarı kart'],['red','Kırmızı kart'],['xg','xG'],['xa','xA'],['shots','Şut'],['keypasses','Kilit pas'],['tackles','Top kapma'],['interceptions','Top kesme'],['saves','Kurtarış']];
function mergePitchMetricBatch(map,rows,metric){
  for(const r of rows||[]){
    const id=String(r.playerId||'');if(!id)continue;
    let old=map.get(id);if(!old){old={playerId:id,player:r.player||'',logo:r.logo||'',value:0,teams:new Set(),appearances:0,ratingTotal:0};map.set(id,old)}
    const value=Number(r.value)||0,count=Math.max(1,Number(r.appearances)||1);
    if(metric==='rating'){old.ratingTotal+=value*count;old.appearances+=count;old.value=old.appearances?old.ratingTotal/old.appearances:0}
    else old.value+=value;
    if(metric!=='rating')old.appearances+=Number(r.appearances)||0;
    if(!old.player&&r.player)old.player=r.player;if(!old.logo&&r.logo)old.logo=r.logo;
    String(r.team||'').split(' / ').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean).forEach(x=>old.teams.add(x));
  }
}
function pitchMetricRowsFromMap(map,metric){
  return [...map.values()].map(r=>({playerId:r.playerId,player:r.player,logo:r.logo,team:[...r.teams].join(' / '),value:Number((r.value||0).toFixed(metric==='rating'?2:0)),source:'PitchAPI'}))
    .sort((a,b)=>b.value-a.value||String(a.player).localeCompare(String(b.player),'tr')).slice(0,50).map((r,i)=>({...r,rank:i+1}));
}
async function loadPitchStatLeaders(my,metric){
  if(!my||!my.data||!metric)return;
  my.pitchMetricLoadedByKey=my.pitchMetricLoadedByKey||{};my.pitchMetricLoadingByKey=my.pitchMetricLoadingByKey||{};my.pitchMetricRowsByKey=my.pitchMetricRowsByKey||{};my.pitchMetricErrorsByKey=my.pitchMetricErrorsByKey||{};
  if(my.pitchMetricLoadedByKey[metric]||my.pitchMetricLoadingByKey[metric])return;
  const season=Number(my.season)||currentTeamSeason(),requestToken=(my.pitchMetricTokens&&my.pitchMetricTokens[metric]||0)+1;
  my.pitchMetricTokens=my.pitchMetricTokens||{};my.pitchMetricTokens[metric]=requestToken;my.pitchMetricLoadingByKey[metric]=true;my.pitchMetricErrorsByKey[metric]='';my.pitchMetricRowsByKey[metric]=[];renderLeague();
  const map=new Map();let total=0,failed=0,offset=0,pageSize=10,done=false;
  const request=(off,limit=10)=>api('/pitch-stat-leaders?code='+encodeURIComponent(my.code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)+'&metric='+encodeURIComponent(metric)+'&offset='+off+'&limit='+limit);
  try{
    const first=await request(0,4);if(cur!==my||my.pitchMetricTokens[metric]!==requestToken||Number(my.season)!==season)return;
    total=Number(first.totalMatches)||0;failed+=Number(first.failedMatches)||0;mergePitchMetricBatch(map,first.rows||[],metric);offset=Number(first.nextOffset)||0;done=!!first.done;my.pitchMetricRowsByKey[metric]=pitchMetricRowsFromMap(map,metric);renderLeague();
    pageSize=10;
    while(!done&&offset<total){
      const starts=[];for(let i=0;i<3&&offset+i*pageSize<total;i++)starts.push(offset+i*pageSize);if(!starts.length)break;
      const results=await Promise.allSettled(starts.map(off=>request(off,10)));
      if(cur!==my||my.pitchMetricTokens[metric]!==requestToken||Number(my.season)!==season)return;
      let nextOffset=offset;
      for(let i=0;i<results.length;i++){
        let item=results[i];if(item.status!=='fulfilled'){try{item={status:'fulfilled',value:await request(starts[i],10)}}catch(_){failed++;continue}}
        const page=item.value;mergePitchMetricBatch(map,page.rows||[],metric);total=Math.max(total,Number(page.totalMatches)||0);failed+=Number(page.failedMatches)||0;nextOffset=Math.max(nextOffset,Number(page.nextOffset)||0);done=done||!!page.done;
      }
      if(nextOffset<=offset)break;offset=nextOffset;my.pitchMetricRowsByKey[metric]=pitchMetricRowsFromMap(map,metric);renderLeague();
    }
    if(cur!==my||my.pitchMetricTokens[metric]!==requestToken||Number(my.season)!==season)return;
    my.pitchMetricRowsByKey[metric]=pitchMetricRowsFromMap(map,metric);my.pitchMetricLoadedByKey[metric]=true;
    if(failed)my.pitchMetricErrorsByKey[metric]=failed+' maçtan istatistik alınamadı; liste kısmen eksik olabilir.';
    if(!my.pitchMetricRowsByKey[metric].length&&!my.pitchMetricErrorsByKey[metric])my.pitchMetricErrorsByKey[metric]='Bu kategori için oyuncu istatistiği bulunamadı.';
  }catch(error){if(cur===my&&my.pitchMetricTokens[metric]===requestToken)my.pitchMetricErrorsByKey[metric]=error&&error.message||String(error)}
  finally{if(cur===my&&my.pitchMetricTokens[metric]===requestToken){my.pitchMetricLoadingByKey[metric]=false;renderLeague()}}
}
function leagueTeamRateRows(fixtures){
  const map=new Map();
  const ensure=team=>{const key=String(team&&team.id||'')||pitchClientTeamKey(team&&team.name||'');if(!key)return null;if(!map.has(key))map.set(key,{id:key,name:team.name||'Takım',logo:team.logo||team.image_url||'',played:0,points:0,wins:0,draws:0,losses:0,scored:0,conceded:0,cleanSheets:0,failedToScore:0});const row=map.get(key);if(!row.logo)row.logo=team.logo||team.image_url||'';return row};
  for(const match of fixtures||[]){const goals=match&&match.goals||{},home=match&&match.teams&&match.teams.home||{},away=match&&match.teams&&match.teams.away||{};if(goals.home==null||goals.away==null||!home.name||!away.name)continue;const hg=Number(goals.home),ag=Number(goals.away);if(!Number.isFinite(hg)||!Number.isFinite(ag))continue;const h=ensure(home),a=ensure(away);if(!h||!a)continue;h.played++;a.played++;h.scored+=hg;h.conceded+=ag;a.scored+=ag;a.conceded+=hg;if(ag===0)h.cleanSheets++;if(hg===0)a.cleanSheets++;if(hg===0)h.failedToScore++;if(ag===0)a.failedToScore++;if(hg>ag){h.points+=3;h.wins++;a.losses++}else if(hg<ag){a.points+=3;a.wins++;h.losses++}else{h.points++;a.points++;h.draws++;a.draws++;}}
  const rows=[...map.values()].filter(x=>x.played>0).map(x=>({...x,goalDifference:x.scored-x.conceded,pointsRate:x.points/x.played,scoredRate:x.scored/x.played,concededRate:x.conceded/x.played}));
  const sorted=(fn)=>rows.slice().sort(fn);
  return {
    points:sorted((a,b)=>b.pointsRate-a.pointsRate||b.points-a.points),
    scored:sorted((a,b)=>b.scoredRate-a.scoredRate||b.scored-a.scored),
    conceded:sorted((a,b)=>a.concededRate-b.concededRate||a.conceded-b.conceded),
    difference:sorted((a,b)=>b.goalDifference-a.goalDifference||b.scored-a.scored),
    wins:sorted((a,b)=>b.wins-a.wins||b.points-a.points), draws:sorted((a,b)=>b.draws-a.draws||b.played-a.played),
    losses:sorted((a,b)=>b.losses-a.losses||b.played-a.played), cleanSheets:sorted((a,b)=>b.cleanSheets-a.cleanSheets||b.played-a.played),
    failedToScore:sorted((a,b)=>b.failedToScore-a.failedToScore||b.played-a.played), mostConceded:sorted((a,b)=>b.conceded-a.conceded), mostScored:sorted((a,b)=>b.scored-a.scored)
  };
}

function changeLeagueSeason(year){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='league')return;
  const my=cur;my.tab='hafta';my.season=Number(year)||currentTeamSeason();my.week='all';my.data=null;my.err='';my.pitchLoading=false;my.pitchLoaded=false;my.pitchError='';my.pitchProgress='';my.pitchGoals=null;my.pitchAssists=null;my.pitchFixtures=null;my.pitchFixturesLoaded=false;my.pitchMetricLoaded='';my.pitchMetricRows=[];my.pitchMetricError='';my.pitchMetricLoading=false;my.pitchMetricLoadedByKey={};my.pitchMetricLoadingByKey={};my.pitchMetricRowsByKey={};my.pitchMetricErrorsByKey={};my.pitchMetricTokens={};my.pitchFixtureRequest=(my.pitchFixtureRequest||0)+1;my.pitchFixtureLoading=false;my.pitchFixturesLoading=false;my.pitchMetricRequest=(my.pitchMetricRequest||0)+1;
  renderLeague();loadLeagueData(my);
}
function openLeague(name,code,meta=null){
  if(!code)return;
  showSheet();
  cur={kind:'league',prev:cur,name,code,logo:meta&&meta.logo||'',country:meta&&meta.team||'',af:meta&&meta.af||'',tab:'hafta',week:'all',season:currentTeamSeason(),seasons:[],data:null,err:'',pitchFixtures:null,pitchFixturesLoaded:false,pitchMetric:'goals',pitchMetricRows:[],leagueStatsMode:'team',leagueExpanded:{},pitchWeekTeam:null,pitchWeekTeamLoading:false,pitchWeekTeamRequest:0,pitchMetricLoadedByKey:{},pitchMetricLoadingByKey:{},pitchMetricRowsByKey:{},pitchMetricErrorsByKey:{},pitchMetricTokens:{}};
  renderLeague();
  loadLeagueLogos();
  loadLeagueData(cur);
}
function leagueFixture(m){
  const g=m.goals||{},id=String(m.fixture.id),s=stat(m),live=liveS(s);
  return `<div class="m" data-m="${esc(id)}" ${matchOddsAttrs(m)}>${statusCell(m)}<div><div class="tm">${img(m.teams.home.logo)}${esc(m.teams.home.name)}</div><div class="tm">${img(m.teams.away.logo)}${esc(m.teams.away.name)}</div></div><div class="sc ${live?'live':''}"><div>${g.home==null?'-':g.home}</div><div>${g.away==null?'-':g.away}</div></div><span></span></div>`;
}
function fixtureRoundValue(m){
  const candidates=[m&&m.fixture&&m.fixture.round,m&&m.league&&m.league.round,m&&m.round_name,m&&m.round,m&&m.matchday,m&&m.league&&m.league.round_name,m&&m.week];
  const hasNumber=value=>/(?:matchday|gameweek|week|hafta|round)\D*\d+|(?:regular season|league stage)\s*[-–:]\s*\d+|\d+\s*(?:matchday|gameweek|week|hafta|round)|^\d+$/i.test(String(value||'').trim());
  for(const value of candidates){const s=String(value==null?'':value).trim();if(s&&hasNumber(s))return s;}
  for(const value of candidates){const s=String(value==null?'':value).trim();if(s)return s;}
  return '';
}
function fixtureRoundLabel(value){
  const s=String(value||'').trim(),m=s.match(/(?:matchday|gameweek|week|hafta|round)\D*(\d+)|(?:regular season|league stage)\s*[-–:]\s*(\d+)/i)||s.match(/(\d+)\s*(?:matchday|gameweek|week|hafta|round)/i)||s.match(/^(\d+)$/);
  if(!m)return s;
  const n=Number(m[1]||m[2]);return n?`${n}. Hafta`:s;
}
function fixtureRoundOrder(value){const s=String(value||'').trim();const m=s.match(/(?:matchday|gameweek|week|hafta|round)\D*(\d+)|(?:^|\b)(\d+)\s*(?:\.?\s*)?(?:matchday|gameweek|week|hafta|round)\b|(?:regular season|league stage)\s*[-–:]\s*(\d+)|^(\d+)$/i);return m?Number(m[1]||m[2]||m[3]||m[4]):9999}
function fixtureWeekModel(fixtures){
  const list=fixtures||[],roundMatches=list.filter(m=>fixtureRoundValue(m));
  if(list.length>0&&roundMatches.length===list.length){
    const rounds=[...new Set(roundMatches.map(fixtureRoundValue))].sort((a,b)=>fixtureRoundOrder(a)-fixtureRoundOrder(b)||a.localeCompare(b,'tr'));
    return {mode:'round',options:rounds.map(value=>({key:'round:'+value,value,label:fixtureRoundLabel(value)}))};
  }
  const monday=date=>{const d=new Date(date);if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return '';const day=(d.getUTCDay()+6)%7;d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()-day);return d.toISOString().slice(0,10)};
  const dates=[...new Set(list.map(m=>monday(m&&m.fixture&&m.fixture.date)).filter(Boolean))].sort();
  return {mode:'calendar',options:dates.map((date,index)=>({key:'week:'+date,value:date,label:(index+1)+'. Hafta · '+new Date(date+'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'})}))};
}
function fixtureWeekKey(m,model){
  if(!model)return '';
  if(model.mode==='round'){const r=fixtureRoundValue(m);return r?'round:'+r:''}
  const d=new Date(m&&m.fixture&&m.fixture.date||'');if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return '';
  const day=(d.getUTCDay()+6)%7;d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate()-day);return 'week:'+d.toISOString().slice(0,10);
}
function fixtureWeekControl(fixtures){
  const model=fixtureWeekModel(fixtures);cur.weekModel=model;
  const options=model.options||[];
  if(!options.length){cur.week='all';return '<div class="weekbar"><label>Fikstür haftası</label><span>Hafta bilgisi henüz yok</span></div>'}
  if(cur.week!=='all'&&!options.some(item=>item.key===cur.week))cur.week='all';
  return `<div class="weekbar"><label for="leagueWeek">Fikstür haftası</label><select id="leagueWeek" data-lw><option value="all" ${cur.week==='all'?'selected':''}>Tüm haftalar</option>${options.map(item=>`<option value="${esc(item.key)}" ${cur.week===item.key?'selected':''}>${esc(item.label)}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`;
}
function leaderList(rows,kind,error,code,year){
  if(!rows||!rows.length)return '<div class="empty">Bu bölüm şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</div>';
  return rows.map((r,i)=>`<div class="leader" data-player-profile="${esc(r.player)}" data-player-team="${esc(r.team||'')}" data-pitch-id="${esc(r.playerId||'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(code||'')}" data-pitch-year="${esc(year||'')}"><span class="rank">${r.rank||i+1}</span>${avatar(r.logo,r.player)}<div>${esc(trPlayerName(r.player))}${r.team?'<small>'+esc(trTeam(r.team))+'</small>':''}</div><b>${esc(r.value)}</b></div>`).join('');
}
function leagueSelectedWeek(fixtures){
  const model=fixtureWeekModel(fixtures||[]),options=model.options||[];
  if(cur.week!=='all'&&options.some(x=>x.key===cur.week))return cur.week;
  for(let i=options.length-1;i>=0;i--){const matches=(fixtures||[]).filter(m=>fixtureWeekKey(m,model)===options[i].key&&DONE.includes(stat(m)));if(matches.length)return options[i].key;}
  return options.length?options[options.length-1].key:'';
}
async function loadPitchWeekTeam(my,weekKey){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='league'||!weekKey)return;
  const season=Number(my.season)||currentTeamSeason(),key=weekKey;
  const fixtures=my.pitchFixtures&&my.pitchFixtures.length?my.pitchFixtures:(my.data&&my.data.fixtures||[]),model=fixtureWeekModel(fixtures);
  const selected=fixtures.filter(match=>fixtureWeekKey(match,model)===key);
  const dateKeys=[...new Set(selected.map(match=>{const d=new Date(match&&match.fixture&&match.fixture.date||'');return Number.isNaN(d.getTime())?'':d.toISOString().slice(0,10)}).filter(Boolean))];
  const requestWeek=key.startsWith('round:')&&dateKeys.length?key+'|dates:'+dateKeys.join(','):key;
  my.pitchWeekTeamRequest=(my.pitchWeekTeamRequest||0)+1;const request=my.pitchWeekTeamRequest;
  my.pitchWeekTeamLoading=true;my.pitchWeekTeamError='';my.pitchWeekTeamKey=key;renderLeague();
  try{const j=await api('/pitch-week-team?code='+encodeURIComponent(my.code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)+'&week='+encodeURIComponent(requestWeek));if(cur!==my||my.pitchWeekTeamRequest!==request||Number(my.season)!==season)return;my.pitchWeekTeam=j;}
  catch(error){if(cur===my&&my.pitchWeekTeamRequest===request)my.pitchWeekTeamError=error&&error.message||String(error)}
  finally{if(cur===my&&my.pitchWeekTeamRequest===request){my.pitchWeekTeamLoading=false;renderLeague()}}
}
function leaguePlayerCard(r,i,metric,year,code){const v=metric==='rating'?Number(r.value||0).toFixed(2):Number(r.value||0);return `<div class="league-feature-row league-player-rank-row" data-player-profile="${esc(r.player)}" data-player-team="${esc(r.team||'')}" data-pitch-id="${esc(r.playerId||'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(code||'')}" data-pitch-year="${esc(year||'')}"><span class="rank">${i+1}</span>${avatar(r.logo,r.player)}<span><b>${esc(trPlayerName(r.player))}</b>${r.team?`<small>${esc(trTeam(r.team))}</small>`:''}</span><strong>${esc(v)}</strong></div>`}
function renderLeague(){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='league')return;
  const d=cur.data,name=(d&&d.name)||cur.name;$('#shTitle').textContent=name;
  const apiFootballLeagueId=Number(cur.af||(d&&(d.apiFootballId||d.afId||d.api_football_id))||0);
  const logo=LEAGUE_PNGS[cur.code]||LEAGUE_ID_PNGS[apiFootballLeagueId]||(apiFootballLeagueId?'https://media.api-sports.io/football/leagues/'+apiFootballLeagueId+'.png':(cur.code?'https://a.espncdn.com/i/leaguelogos/soccer/500/'+encodeURIComponent(cur.code)+'.png':''))||'';
  const head=`<div class="pinfo league">${avatarT(logo,name)}<div><h2>${esc(name)}</h2><small>${d?esc(d.season)+' sezonu':'Sezon bilgisi yükleniyor…'}</small></div></div>`;
  const seasonList=(cur.seasons&&cur.seasons.length?cur.seasons:Array.from({length:20},(_,i)=>currentTeamSeason()-i));
  const seasonOptions=[...new Set([Number(cur.season),...seasonList.map(Number)].filter(Number.isFinite))].sort((a,b)=>b-a).map(y=>`<option value="${y}" ${Number(cur.season)===y?'selected':''}>${String(y).slice(-2)}-${String(y+1).slice(-2)}</option>`).join('');
  const seasonBar=`<div class="team-seasonbar league-reference-seasonbar"><span>Sezon</span><select id="leagueSeason" aria-label="Lig sezonu">${seasonOptions}</select></div>`;
  const tabItems=[['hafta','Haftanın Takımı'],['maclar','Maçlar'],['puan','Puan Durumu'],['istat','İstatistikler'],['liderler','En İyi Oyuncular']];
  const tabs=`<div class="stabs league-main-tabs">${tabItems.map(([key,label])=>`<div class="${cur.tab===key?'on':''}" data-ltab="${key}">${label}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  let body='';
  if(cur.err&&!d)body='<div class="empty">'+nice(cur.err)+'</div>';
  else if(!d)body='<div class="empty">Lig verileri yükleniyor…</div>';
  else{
    const fixtures=cur.pitchFixtures&&cur.pitchFixtures.length?cur.pitchFixtures:(d.fixtures||[]);
    const model=fixtureWeekModel(fixtures);cur.weekModel=model;
    const weekbar=fixtureWeekControl(fixtures);
    const selectedWeek=leagueSelectedWeek(fixtures);
    if(cur.tab==='hafta'){
      const result=cur.pitchWeekTeam;
      body=weekbar+'<section class="league-week-team-card"><div class="league-week-team-head"><div><b>Haftanın En İyi 11’i</b><small>Seçilen haftada oynanan maçların gerçek maç puanları</small></div><span class="league-week-badge">XI</span></div>';
      if(cur.pitchWeekTeamLoading)body+='<div class="empty">Haftanın oyuncu puanları hesaplanıyor…</div>';
      else if(cur.pitchWeekTeamError)body+='<div class="empty">'+esc(cur.pitchWeekTeamError)+'</div>';
      else if(result&&result.bestEleven&&result.bestEleven.length){
        const roleLabel={GK:'Kaleci',DF:'Defans',MF:'Orta saha',FW:'Forvet'};
        body+='<div class="league-week-team-meta"><span>'+esc(result.league||name)+'</span><span>'+esc(String(result.week||selectedWeek).replace(/^round:/,'').replace(/^week:/,''))+'</span><span>'+Number(result.matchCount||0)+' maç</span></div>';
        const xiRoles=[['FW','Forvet'],['MF','Orta saha'],['DF','Defans'],['GK','Kaleci']];
        body+='<div class="league-week-pitch" aria-label="Haftanın takımı ilk on bir sahası"><div class="league-week-pitch-lines"><span class="league-week-center-circle"></span><span class="league-week-box top"></span><span class="league-week-box bottom"></span></div>';
        xiRoles.forEach(([role,label])=>{const group=result.bestEleven.filter(p=>String(p.role||'').toUpperCase()===role);if(!group.length)return;body+='<div class="league-week-pitch-line '+role.toLowerCase()+'" aria-label="'+label+'">'+group.map(p=>{const i=result.bestEleven.indexOf(p);return `<button type="button" class="league-week-player" data-player-profile="${esc(p.name)}" data-player-team="${esc(p.team||'')}" data-pitch-id="${esc(p.id||'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(cur.code)}" data-pitch-year="${esc(cur.season)}"><span class="league-week-player-rank">${i+1}</span>${avatar(p.photo,p.name)}<b>${esc(trPlayerName(p.name))}</b><small>${esc(p.team||'')}</small><strong title="${p.ratingSource==='SkorHane'?'SkorHane maç istatistiklerinden hesaplandı':p.ratingSource||'Maç puanı'}">${Number(p.rating||0).toFixed(2)}</strong>${p.ratingSource==='SkorHane'?'<small class="rating-source">Hesaplanan</small>':''}</button>`}).join('')+'</div>'});
        body+='</div>';
        if(result.failedMatches)body+='<div class="pnote">'+result.failedMatches+' maçın oyuncu verisi alınamadı; 11 eksik olabilir.</div>';
      }else body+='<div class="empty">'+esc(result&&result.message||'Bu hafta için oyuncu puanları henüz bulunamadı.')+'</div>';
      body+='</section>';
      if(!cur.pitchFixturesLoaded&&!cur.pitchFixturesLoading)loadPitchFixtures(cur);
      if(cur.pitchFixturesLoaded&&!cur.pitchWeekTeamLoading&&selectedWeek&&cur.pitchWeekTeamKey!==selectedWeek)loadPitchWeekTeam(cur,selectedWeek);
    }else if(cur.tab==='maclar'){
      const visible=cur.week==='all'?fixtures:fixtures.filter(m=>fixtureWeekKey(m,model)===cur.week);
      body=weekbar+'<div class="league-fixture-list">'+(visible.length?visible.slice().sort((a,b)=>String(a.fixture&&a.fixture.date||'').localeCompare(String(b.fixture&&b.fixture.date||''))).map(leagueFixture).join(''):'<div class="empty">Seçilen haftada maç bulunamadı.</div>')+'</div>';
      if(!cur.pitchFixturesLoaded&&!cur.pitchFixturesLoading)loadPitchFixtures(cur);
    }else if(cur.tab==='puan'){
      const tables=Array.isArray(d.tables)?d.tables:[];
      body=tables.length?tables.map(table=>'<div class="league-standings-title">'+esc(lgT({id:'x',name:table.name||name}).name)+'</div>'+stTables([table],cur.code,null)).join(''):'<div class="empty">Bu lig/sezon için puan durumu bulunamadı.</div>';
    }else if(cur.tab==='istat'){
      const mode=cur.leagueStatsMode||'team';
      const controls=`<div class="league-reference-controls"><div class="reference-segment"><button type="button" class="${mode==='team'?'on':''}" data-lstat="team">Takım</button><button type="button" class="${mode==='player'?'on':''}" data-lstat="player">Oyuncu</button></div></div>`;
      const rankSection=(title,key,format)=>{const rows=cur.leagueExpanded&&cur.leagueExpanded[key]?(ranks[key]||[]):(ranks[key]||[]).slice(0,3);return `<section class="league-stat-section reference-league-card"><button type="button" class="league-stat-heading" data-league-expand="${key}"><b>${title}</b><span>${cur.leagueExpanded&&cur.leagueExpanded[key]?'−':'›'}</span></button><div class="league-feature-list">${rows.length?rows.map((r,i)=>`<div class="league-feature-row league-team-feature-row"><span class="rank">${i+1}</span>${avatarT(r.logo,r.name)}<span><b>${esc(trTeam(r.name))}</b><small>${r.played} maç</small></span><strong>${format(r)}</strong></div>`).join(''):'<div class="empty">Bu istatistik için tamamlanmış maç verisi bulunamadı.</div>'}</div>${cur.leagueExpanded&&cur.leagueExpanded[key]?'<small class="league-rank-foot">Lig verisinde bulunan '+rows.length+' takım</small>':''}</section>`};
      let ranks=null;
      if(mode==='team'){
        ranks=leagueTeamRateRows(fixtures);
        const metrics=[['points','Maç başı puan',r=>r.pointsRate.toFixed(2)],['scored','Maç başı atılan gol',r=>r.scoredRate.toFixed(2)],['conceded','Maç başı yenilen gol',r=>r.concededRate.toFixed(2)],['difference','Averaj',r=>(r.goalDifference>0?'+':'')+r.goalDifference],['wins','Galibiyet',r=>r.wins],['draws','Beraberlik',r=>r.draws],['losses','Mağlubiyet',r=>r.losses],['cleanSheets','Gol yemeden bitirilen maç',r=>r.cleanSheets],['failedToScore','Gol atamadan bitirilen maç',r=>r.failedToScore],['mostConceded','En çok gol yiyen',r=>r.conceded],['mostScored','En çok gol atan',r=>r.scored]];
        body=controls+'<div class="league-stats-reference-title">Takım İstatistikleri</div>'+metrics.map(m=>rankSection(m[1],m[0],m[2])).join('');
        if(!cur.pitchFixturesLoaded&&!cur.pitchFixturesLoading)loadPitchFixtures(cur);
      }else{
        const metrics=[['rating','Oyuncu puanları','Puan'],['goals','Gol krallığı','Gol'],['assists','Asist krallığı','Asist'],['yellow','Sarı kartlar','Sarı kart'],['red','Kırmızı kartlar','Kırmızı kart'],['keypasses','En çok kilit pas verenler','Kilit pas'],['appearances','En çok maçta oynayanlar','Maç'],['minutes','En çok süre alanlar','Dakika']];
        const renderMetric=(metric,title)=>{const all=metric==='goals'?(cur.pitchLoaded?(cur.data&&cur.data.goals||pitchLeaderRows(cur.pitchGoals||new Map())):[]):(cur.pitchMetricRowsByKey&&cur.pitchMetricRowsByKey[metric]||[]),expanded=!!(cur.leagueExpanded&&cur.leagueExpanded['p_'+metric]),rows=all.slice(0,expanded?10:3),loading=metric==='goals'?!!cur.pitchLoading:(cur.pitchMetricLoadingByKey&&cur.pitchMetricLoadingByKey[metric]),error=metric==='goals'?cur.pitchError:(cur.pitchMetricErrorsByKey&&cur.pitchMetricErrorsByKey[metric]);return `<section class="league-stat-section reference-league-card"><button type="button" class="league-stat-heading" data-league-expand="p_${metric}"><b>${title}</b><span>${expanded?'−':'›'}</span></button><div class="league-feature-list">${rows.length?rows.map((r,i)=>leaguePlayerCard(r,i,metric,cur.season,cur.code)).join(''):loading?'<div class="empty">PitchAPI oyuncu verileri yükleniyor…</div>':`<div class="empty">${esc(error||'Bu kategori için veri bulunamadı.')}</div>`}</div>${expanded?'<small class="league-rank-foot">İlk 10 oyuncu</small>':''}</section>`};
        body=controls+'<div class="league-stats-reference-title">Oyuncu İstatistikleri</div>'+metrics.map(m=>renderMetric(m[0],m[1])).join('');
        loadPitchLeaders(cur);for(const [metric] of metrics)if(metric!=='goals')loadPitchStatLeaders(cur,metric);
      }
    }else if(cur.tab==='liderler'){
      const goals=cur.pitchLoaded?(d.goals||[]):cur.pitchGoals?pitchLeaderRows(cur.pitchGoals):[];
      const assists=cur.pitchLoaded?(d.assists||[]):cur.pitchAssists?pitchLeaderRows(cur.pitchAssists):[];
      const loadingMessage='<div class="empty">PitchAPI maç olayları ve oyuncu istatistikleri taranıyor…</div>';
      const goalBlock=goals.length?leaderList(goals.slice(0,20),'goals',d.errors&&d.errors.goals,cur.code,cur.season):cur.pitchLoading?loadingMessage:'<div class="empty">Gol istatistiği bulunamadı.</div>';
      const assistBlock=assists.length?leaderList(assists.slice(0,20),'assists',d.errors&&d.errors.assists,cur.code,cur.season):cur.pitchLoading?loadingMessage:'<div class="empty">Asist istatistiği bulunamadı.</div>';
      body='<div class="league-best-player-grid"><section class="league-best-player-card"><h3>Gol krallığı</h3>'+goalBlock+'</section><section class="league-best-player-card"><h3>Asist krallığı</h3>'+assistBlock+'</section></div>';
      if(!cur.pitchLoading&&!cur.pitchLoaded)loadPitchLeaders(cur);
      if(cur.pitchLoading&&cur.pitchProgress)body+='<div class="pnote">'+esc(cur.pitchProgress)+'</div>';
      if(cur.pitchError)body+='<div class="pnote">'+esc(cur.pitchError)+'</div>';
    }
  }
  $('#shBody').innerHTML=head+seasonBar+tabs+body;
}
