const textOriginals=new WeakMap(),attrOriginals=new WeakMap();
function translateText(value){if(currentLang()!=='en')return value;return UI_EN[String(value).trim()]!==undefined?String(value).replace(String(value).trim(),UI_EN[String(value).trim()]):value}
function translateTree(root=document){
  if(currentLang()!=='en'){
    const walk=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);let n;while((n=walk.nextNode()))if(textOriginals.has(n)&&n.nodeValue!==textOriginals.get(n))n.nodeValue=textOriginals.get(n);
    root.querySelectorAll?.('[placeholder],[title],[aria-label]').forEach(el=>{const saved=attrOriginals.get(el)||{};for(const key of ['placeholder','title','aria-label'])if(saved[key]!==undefined&&el.getAttribute(key)!==saved[key])el.setAttribute(key,saved[key])});
    return;
  }
  const walk=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);let n;while((n=walk.nextNode())){if(!textOriginals.has(n))textOriginals.set(n,n.nodeValue);const original=textOriginals.get(n),translated=translateText(original);if(n.nodeValue!==translated)n.nodeValue=translated;}
  root.querySelectorAll?.('[placeholder],[title],[aria-label]').forEach(el=>{let saved=attrOriginals.get(el);if(!saved){saved={};for(const key of ['placeholder','title','aria-label'])if(el.hasAttribute(key))saved[key]=el.getAttribute(key);attrOriginals.set(el,saved)}for(const key of ['placeholder','title','aria-label'])if(saved[key]!==undefined){const translated=translateText(saved[key]);if(el.getAttribute(key)!==translated)el.setAttribute(key,translated)}});
}
function applyLanguage(lang){localStorage.setItem('canliskor.language',lang==='en'?'en':'tr');document.documentElement.lang=lang==='en'?'en':'tr';translateTree();if(drawerOpen)renderDrawer();}
function applyTheme(theme){const value=theme==='light'?'light':'dark';localStorage.setItem('canliskor.theme',value);document.body.classList.toggle('light',value==='light');}
function openDrawer(){drawerOpen=true;drawerView='home';$('#drawerBackdrop').hidden=false;document.body.classList.add('drawer-open');requestAnimationFrame(()=>$('#drawerBackdrop').classList.add('open'));renderDrawer();if(!FAVS&&!favsLoading){favsLoading=true;api('/favs').then(j=>{FAVS=j;if(drawerOpen&&drawerView==='home')renderDrawer()}).catch(()=>{}).finally(()=>{favsLoading=false})}loadLeagueLogos()}
function closeDrawer(){const back=$('#drawerBackdrop');if(!drawerOpen)return;drawerOpen=false;back.classList.remove('open');document.body.classList.remove('drawer-open');setTimeout(()=>{if(!drawerOpen)back.hidden=true},290)}
function drawerRow(icon,label,sub,action){return `<button class="drawer-item" data-drawer-action="${action}"><span class="ico">${icon}</span><span>${label}${sub?`<small>${sub}</small>`:''}</span></button>`}
function renderDrawer(){
  if(!drawerOpen)return;
  const content=$('#drawerContent');
  if(drawerView==='settings'){
    content.innerHTML=`<div class="drawer-page"><button class="drawer-item" data-drawer-action="back"><span class="ico">←</span><span>Geri</span></button><h3>Ayarlar</h3><label class="drawer-field" for="themeSelect">Görünüm</label><select class="drawer-select" id="themeSelect"><option value="dark" ${currentTheme()==='dark'?'selected':''}>Koyu Tema</option><option value="light" ${currentTheme()==='light'?'selected':''}>Açık Tema</option></select><label class="drawer-field" for="languageSelect">Dil</label><select class="drawer-select" id="languageSelect"><option value="tr" ${currentLang()==='tr'?'selected':''}>Türkçe</option><option value="en" ${currentLang()==='en'?'selected':''}>İngilizce</option></select><label class="drawer-field" for="soundSelect">Ses Efektleri</label><select class="drawer-select" id="soundSelect"><option value="on" ${currentSoundSetting()==='on'?'selected':''}>Açık</option><option value="off" ${currentSoundSetting()==='off'?'selected':''}>Kapalı</option></select><p class="drawer-note" style="margin-top:12px">Dil, tema ve ses seçimi bu cihazda saklanır. Gol, düdük ve buton seslerini buradan kapatabilirsin.</p></div>`;
  }else if(drawerView==='simulate'){
    const teams=[...FAV_TEAMS.map(x=>x[0]),'Liverpool','Manchester City','Inter','Barcelona','Paris Saint-Germain'];
    content.innerHTML=`<div class="drawer-page"><button class="drawer-item" data-drawer-action="back"><span class="ico">←</span><span>Geri</span></button><h3>Maç Simülatörü</h3><label class="drawer-field" for="simHome">Ev Sahibi</label><select class="drawer-select" id="simHome">${teams.map((name,i)=>`<option value="${esc(name)}" ${i===0?'selected':''}>${esc(name)}</option>`).join('')}</select><label class="drawer-field" for="simAway">Deplasman</label><select class="drawer-select" id="simAway">${teams.map((name,i)=>`<option value="${esc(name)}" ${i===1?'selected':''}>${esc(name)}</option>`).join('')}</select><button class="drawer-primary" id="simRun">Simüle Et</button><div id="simResult" class="drawer-result">— : —<small>Bir maç simülasyonu başlat.</small></div><p class="drawer-note" style="margin-top:10px">Simülasyon sadece eğlence amaçlı rastgele skor üretir. Gerçek maç verisi veya tahmini değildir.</p></div>`;
  }else{
    const tf=raw=>FAVS&&FAVS.teams.find(x=>x.raw===raw)||{},pf=nm=>FAVS&&FAVS.players.find(x=>x.name===nm)||{};
    content.innerHTML=drawerRow('⚙️','Ayarlar','Tema ve dil','settings')+drawerRow('⚽','Simüle','Maç simülatörü','simulate')+'<div class="drawer-sep"></div><div class="drawer-section">Takımlar</div>'
      +FAV_TEAMS.map(t=>{const f=tf(t[2]);return `<button class="drawer-item" data-team="${esc(JSON.stringify([f.id||'',t[1],t[2],t[0]]))}">${avatarT(f.logo,t[0])}<span>${esc(t[0])}<small>Fikstür, kadro ve puan durumu</small></span></button>`}).join('')
      +'<div class="drawer-section">Ligler</div>'+FAV_LEAGUES.map(l=>`<button class="drawer-item" data-league="${esc(JSON.stringify(l))}">${avatarT(LEAGUE_PNGS[l[1]]||LEAGUE_LOGOS[l[1]]||leagueLogo({code:l[1]}),l[0])}<span>${esc(l[0])}<small>Maçlar ve puan durumu</small></span></button>`).join('')
      +'<div class="drawer-section">Oyuncular</div>'+FAV_PL.map((p,i)=>`<button class="drawer-item" data-fp="${i}">${avatar(pf(p[1]).logo,p[0])}<span>${esc(p[0])}<small>Profil ve kariyer</small></span></button>`).join('');
  }
  translateTree(content);
}
function runMatchSimulation(){
  const home=$('#simHome')?.value,away=$('#simAway')?.value,out=$('#simResult');if(!out)return;
  if(!home||!away){out.innerHTML='— : —<small>Bir ev sahibi ve deplasman takımı seç.</small>';return}
  if(home===away){out.innerHTML='— : —<small>Aynı takımı iki kez seçemezsin.</small>';return}
  const poisson=lambda=>{const limit=Math.exp(-lambda);let k=0,p=1;do{k++;p*=Math.random()}while(p>limit&&k<8);return k-1};
  const gh=poisson(1.35),ga=poisson(1.12);
  out.innerHTML=`${esc(home)} ${gh} : ${ga} ${esc(away)}<small>Rastgele simülasyon sonucu</small>`;
}
let LEAGUE_LOGOS=(()=>{try{return {...JSON.parse(localStorage.leagueLogos||'{}'),...LEAGUE_PNGS}}catch(_){return {...LEAGUE_PNGS}}})(),leagueLogoLoading=false,leagueLogoLoaded=false;
function loadLeagueLogos(){if(leagueLogoLoaded||leagueLogoLoading)return;leagueLogoLoading=true;api('/league-logos?v=11').then(j=>{LEAGUE_LOGOS={...LEAGUE_LOGOS,...Object.fromEntries((j.logos||[]).filter(x=>x.logo).map(x=>[x.code,x.logo])),...LEAGUE_PNGS};try{localStorage.leagueLogos=JSON.stringify(LEAGUE_LOGOS)}catch(_){}if(drawerOpen)renderDrawer();if(cur&&cur.kind==='league')renderCur();else if(cur&&cur.kind==='search'&&cur.type==='league')renderSearch();else if(!sheetOpen)render()}).catch(()=>{leagueLogoLoaded=false}).finally(()=>{leagueLogoLoading=false})}
function leagueSearchLogoSources(item){const code=String(item.code||'').trim();const af=String(item.af||(code.match(/^af-(\d+)$/i)||[])[1]||'');const afId=af;const byId=LEAGUE_ID_PNGS[Number(afId)]||'';const espn=code&&!/^af-\d+$/i.test(code)?'https://a.espncdn.com/i/leaguelogos/soccer/500/'+encodeURIComponent(code.toLowerCase())+'.png':'';const candidates=[item.logo,item.apiLogo,item.badge,item.logoCandidates,byId,afId?'https://media.api-sports.io/football/leagues/'+encodeURIComponent(afId)+'.png':'',LEAGUE_PNGS[code],LEAGUE_LOGOS[code],LEAGUE_LOGOS[afId],espn].flat(Infinity);return photoList(candidates);}
function openMenu(){
  showSheet();cur={kind:'menu',prev:null};renderMenu();
  if(!FAVS&&!favsLoading){favsLoading=true;api('/favs').then(j=>{FAVS=j;if(cur&&cur.kind==='menu')renderMenu()}).catch(()=>{}).finally(()=>{favsLoading=false})}
  loadLeagueLogos();
}
function renderMenu(){
  $('#shTitle').textContent='Menü';
  const tf=raw=>FAVS&&FAVS.teams.find(x=>x.raw===raw)||{},pf=nm=>FAVS&&FAVS.players.find(x=>x.name===nm)||{};
  $('#shBody').innerHTML='<div class="pr" data-act="search"><span class="av">🔎</span><div>Ara<small>Futbolcu, teknik direktör veya takım</small></div><span class="go">›</span></div><div class="sec">Takımlar</div>'
   +FAV_TEAMS.map(t=>{const f=tf(t[2]);return `<div class="pr" data-team="${esc(JSON.stringify([f.id||'',t[1],t[2],t[0]]))}">${avatarT(f.logo,t[0])}<div>${esc(t[0])}<small>Fikstür, kadro ve puan durumu</small></div><span class="go">›</span></div>`}).join('')
   +'<div class="sec">Ligler</div>'+FAV_LEAGUES.map(l=>`<div class="pr" data-league="${esc(JSON.stringify(l))}">${avatarT(LEAGUE_PNGS[l[1]]||LEAGUE_LOGOS[l[1]]||`https://a.espncdn.com/i/leaguelogos/soccer/500/${l[1]}.png`,l[0])}<div>${esc(l[0])}<small>Maçlar ve puan durumu</small></div><span class="go">›</span></div>`).join('')
   +'<div class="sec">Oyuncular</div>'
   +FAV_PL.map((p,i)=>`<div class="pr" data-fp="${i}">${avatar(pf(p[1]).logo,p[0])}<div>${esc(p[0])}<small>Profil ve kariyer</small></div><span class="go">›</span></div>`).join('');
}

/* ---------- Takım sayfası ---------- */
function teamKey(m,side){
  const mm=String(m.fixture.id).match(/^e:([^:]+):/),t=m.teams[side];
  return JSON.stringify([mm&&/^\d+$/.test(String(t.id))?t.id:'',mm?mm[1]:'',t.raw||t.name,t.name]);
}
function currentTeamSeason(){const d=new Date();return d.getMonth()>=6?d.getFullYear():d.getFullYear()-1;}
function loadTeamData(my){
  const season=Number(my.season)||currentTeamSeason(),token=(my.teamReqToken||0)+1;
  my.teamReqToken=token;
  const qs='id='+encodeURIComponent(my.id||'')+'&code='+encodeURIComponent(my.code||'')+'&name='+encodeURIComponent(my.raw||my.name)+'&tsdb='+encodeURIComponent(my.tsdb||'')+'&af='+encodeURIComponent(my.af||'')+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)+'&logo='+encodeURIComponent(my.logo||'');
  api('/team?'+qs).then(j=>{
    if(cur!==my||my.teamReqToken!==token||Number(my.season)!==season)return;
    my.data=j;if(j.team&&!j.team.logo&&my.logo)j.team.logo=my.logo;my.seasons=j.seasons||my.seasons||[];if(j.afTeamId)my.af=j.afTeamId;my.st=Array.isArray(j.tables)&&j.tables.length?j.tables:null;my.sterr='';
    if(my.tab==='ozet'||my.tab==='kadro'||my.tab==='puan'||my.tab==='istat'||my.tab==='liderler'){teamTab(my.tab);return}renderTeam();
  }).catch(e=>{if(cur===my&&my.teamReqToken===token&&Number(my.season)===season){my.err=e.message||String(e);renderTeam()}});
}
const TEAM_FAV_KEY='skorhane.teamFavorites.v1';
function readTeamFavorites(){try{const v=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(TEAM_FAV_KEY)||'[]');return Array.isArray(v)?v:[]}catch(_){return []}}
function isTeamFavorite(name){const key=pitchClientTeamKey(name);return readTeamFavorites().includes(key)}
function toggleTeamFavorite(name){const key=pitchClientTeamKey(name),list=readTeamFavorites(),next=list.includes(key)?list.filter(x=>x!==key):[...list,key];try{localStorage.setItem(TEAM_FAV_KEY,JSON.stringify(next))}catch(_){}if(cur&&cur.kind==='team')renderTeam()}
function openTeam(key){
  let a;try{a=JSON.parse(key)}catch(_){return}
  const [id,code,raw,name,tsdb,af,season,logo]=a;
  const clean=careerTeamClean(raw||name||'');
  const fallbackLogo=typeof logo==='string'&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(logo)?logo:'';
  cur={kind:'team',prev:cur,id,code,name:clean,raw:clean,tsdb:tsdb||'',af:af||'',logo:fallbackLogo,season:Number(season)||currentTeamSeason(),seasons:[],tab:'ozet',comp:0,data:null,err:'',roster:null,rerr:'',st:null,sterr:'',stByCode:{},stComp:'',teamStatsMode:'team',teamStatsCode:'all',teamPlayerSort:'goals',pitchTeamStats:null,pitchTeamStatsLoading:false,pitchTeamStatsError:'',pitchTeamStatsReq:0};
  $('#sheet').scrollTop=0;
  renderTeam();
  loadTeamData(cur);
}
function changeTeamSeason(year){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='team')return;
  const my=cur;my.season=Number(year)||currentTeamSeason();my.comp=0;my.data=null;my.err='';
  my.roster=null;my.rerr='';my.rosterNote='';my.rosterLoading=false;my.rosterReqToken=(my.rosterReqToken||0)+1;
  my.st=null;my.sterr='';my.stLoading=false;my.stReqToken=(my.stReqToken||0)+1;my.stByCode={};my.stComp='';my.stCompName='';my.pitchTeamStats=null;my.pitchTeamStatsError='';my.pitchTeamStatsLoading=false;my.pitchTeamStatsReq=(my.pitchTeamStatsReq||0)+1;my.teamStatsCode='all';
  renderTeam();loadTeamData(my);
}
const TEAM_COMP_NAME_CODES=[
  ['tur.1',['süper lig','super lig','trendyol süper lig']],['tur.cup',['türkiye kupası','turkiye kupasi','turkish cup','zıraat türkiye kupası','ziraat turkiye kupasi']],
  ['uefa.champions',['şampiyonlar ligi','sampiyonlar ligi','uefa champions league','champions league']],['uefa.europa',['avrupa ligi','uefa europa league','europa league']],['uefa.europa.conf',['konferans ligi','uefa conference league','conference league']],
  ['eng.1',['premier league','premier lig']],['eng.fa',['fa cup']],['eng.league_cup',['efl cup','league cup','lig kupası']],
  ['esp.1',['la liga','laliga']],['esp.copa_del_rey',['kral kupası','copa del rey']],['ger.1',['bundesliga']],['ger.dfb_pokal',['dfb pokal']],
  ['ita.1',['serie a']],['ita.coppa_italia',['coppa italia','italya kupası']],['fra.1',['ligue 1']],['fra.coupe_de_france',['coupe de france','fransa kupası']],
  ['ned.1',['eredivisie']],['por.1',['liga portugal','primeira liga']],['sco.1',['scottish premiership','premiership']],
];
function teamCompetitionCodeFromName(value){
  const n=pitchClientTeamKey(value);if(!n)return '';
  for(const [code,names] of TEAM_COMP_NAME_CODES)if(names.some(name=>{const k=pitchClientTeamKey(name);return n===k||n.includes(k)||k.includes(n)}))return code;
  return '';
}
function teamCompetitionOptions(d,withAll=false){
  const out=[],seen=new Set();
  const push=(code,name)=>{code=String(code||'').trim();name=String(name||'').trim();if(!code||seen.has(code))return;seen.add(code);out.push({code,name:name||code})};
  for(const group of (d&&Array.isArray(d.groups)?d.groups:[])){
    if(!group)continue;
    const matches=Array.isArray(group.matches)?group.matches:[];
    for(const m of matches){
      const league=m&&m.league||{};const name=league.name||group.name||'';
      let code=league.code||league.pitchCode||'';
      if(!code&&league.afId!=null)code='af-'+league.afId;
      if(!code&&/^\d+$/.test(String(league.id||'')))code='af-'+String(league.id);
      if(!code&&m&&m.fixture&&m.fixture.leagueCode)code=m.fixture.leagueCode;
      if(!code)code=teamCompetitionCodeFromName(name);
      push(code,name);
    }
    if(!matches.length){const code=group.code||teamCompetitionCodeFromName(group.name);push(code,group.name)}
  }
  if(!out.length&&d&&d.code)push(d.code,(d.team&&d.team.league)||d.code);
  const priority=x=>/super lig|süper lig|premier league|la liga|bundesliga|serie a|ligue 1|eredivisie|liga portugal/i.test(x.name)?0:/champions league|şampiyonlar ligi/i.test(x.name)?1:2;
  out.sort((a,b)=>priority(a)-priority(b)||a.name.localeCompare(b.name,'tr'));
  return withAll?[{code:'all',name:'Tümü'},...out]:out;
}
function addTeamStatPage(target,page){
  const t=target.team,p=page.team||{};
  for(const key of ['matches','wins','draws','losses','goalsFor','goalsAgainst','points','cleanSheets'])t[key]=(Number(t[key])||0)+(Number(p[key])||0);
  const map=target.players;
  for(const row of page.players||[]){
    const id=playerNameKey(row.name||'')||String(row.id||row.name||'');if(!id)continue;
    if(!map[id])map[id]={...row,apps:0,starts:0,startsKnown:true,goals:0,assists:0,minutes:0,minutesKnown:true,ratingTotal:0,ratingCount:0,shots:0,shotsOnTarget:0,passes:0,accuratePasses:0,tackles:0,interceptions:0,saves:0,yellowCards:0,redCards:0};
    const x=map[id];x.apps+=Number(row.apps)||0;if(row.starts==null)x.startsKnown=false;else if(x.startsKnown)x.starts+=Number(row.starts)||0;for(const k of ['goals','assists','shots','shotsOnTarget','passes','accuratePasses','tackles','interceptions','saves','yellowCards','redCards'])x[k]+=Number(row[k])||0;
    // Worker sayfası ortalama yerine ratingTotal/ratingCount gönderir.
    // Eski arayüz satır.rating beklediği için bu değerler sıfırda kalıyordu.
    const incomingRatingCount=Number(row.ratingCount)||((row.rating!=null&&Number.isFinite(Number(row.rating)))?1:0);
    const incomingRatingTotal=Number(row.ratingTotal)||((row.rating!=null&&Number.isFinite(Number(row.rating)))?Number(row.rating):0);
    x.ratingTotal+=incomingRatingTotal;x.ratingCount+=incomingRatingCount;
    x.minutes+=Number(row.minutes)||0;if(row.minutesKnown===false||row.minutes==null)x.minutesKnown=false;
    x.name=x.name||row.name;x.photo=x.photo||row.photo;x.position=x.position||row.position;
  }
  for(const player of Object.values(map))player.starts=player.startsKnown?player.starts:null;
}
async function loadPitchTeamStats(my){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='team'||my.pitchTeamStatsLoading)return;
  my.teamStatsCode=my.teamStatsCode||'all';
  const allOptions=teamCompetitionOptions(my.data),opts=my.teamStatsCode==='all'?allOptions:allOptions.filter(x=>x.code===my.teamStatsCode),selected=opts[0];
  if(!selected){my.pitchTeamStatsError='Bu takım için PitchAPI lig eşleşmesi bulunamadı.';renderTeam();return}
  my.pitchTeamStatsCompetition=my.teamStatsCode==='all'?'Tüm Ligler':selected.name;my.pitchTeamStatsLoading=true;my.pitchTeamStatsError='';my.pitchTeamStatsFailedCompetitions=[];
  my.pitchTeamStats={team:{matches:0,wins:0,draws:0,losses:0,goalsFor:0,goalsAgainst:0,points:0,cleanSheets:0},players:{},done:false,totalMatches:0,failedMatches:0};
  const req=++my.pitchTeamStatsReq,season=Number(my.season)||currentTeamSeason(),team=my.raw||my.name;renderTeam();
  try{
    const competitions=my.teamStatsCode==='all'?allOptions:opts,failed=[];let succeeded=0;
    for(const comp of competitions){
      let offset=0,guard=0,done=false;
      try{
        while(!done&&guard++<30){
          const page=await api('/pitch-team-stats?code='+encodeURIComponent(comp.code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)+'&team='+encodeURIComponent(team)+'&offset='+offset+'&limit=20');
          if(cur!==my||my.pitchTeamStatsReq!==req)return;
          addTeamStatPage(my.pitchTeamStats,page);my.pitchTeamStats.totalMatches+=Number(page.totalMatches)||0;my.pitchTeamStats.failedMatches+=Number(page.failedMatches)||0;
          const next=Number(page.nextOffset);done=!!page.done||!Number.isInteger(next)||next<=offset;offset=next;
        }
        if(!done)throw new Error('Sayfalama sınırına ulaşıldı.');
        succeeded++;
      }catch(error){
        if(cur!==my||my.pitchTeamStatsReq!==req)return;
        failed.push(comp.name+': '+(error&&error.message||String(error)));
      }
    }
    my.pitchTeamStatsFailedCompetitions=failed;
    if(!succeeded)throw new Error(failed[0]||'Seçilen liglerde takım istatistik verisi bulunamadı.');
    // Oyuncu portreleri eksik kaldığında PitchAPI fotoğraf indeksiyle tamamla.
    const photoCodes=competitions.map(x=>x.code);
    for(const code of photoCodes){
      try{const photoResult=await api('/pitch-team-player-photos?code='+encodeURIComponent(code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)+'&team='+encodeURIComponent(team));if(cur!==my||my.pitchTeamStatsReq!==req)return;const photos=photoResult&&photoResult.photos&&typeof photoResult.photos==='object'?photoResult.photos:{};const entries=Object.entries(photos);for(const player of Object.values(my.pitchTeamStats.players||{})){if(player.photo)continue;const key=playerNameKey(player.name||'');const match=entries.find(([name,url])=>url&&playerNameKey(name)===key);if(match)player.photo=match[1];}}catch(_){ }
    }
    my.pitchTeamStats.done=true;
  }catch(e){if(cur===my&&my.pitchTeamStatsReq===req)my.pitchTeamStatsError=e.message||String(e)}
  finally{if(cur===my&&my.pitchTeamStatsReq===req){my.pitchTeamStatsLoading=false;renderTeam()}}
}

function teamTab(t){
  cur.tab=t;const my=cur,d=my.data,season=Number(my.season)||currentTeamSeason();
  if(d&&(t==='kadro'||t==='ozet')&&!my.roster&&!my.rerr&&!my.rosterLoading){
    const tid=d.team&&d.team.id,cd=d.code||my.code,af=d.afTeamId||my.af||'',token=(my.rosterReqToken||0)+1;my.rosterReqToken=token;my.rosterLoading=true;
    if((!tid||!cd)&&!af){my.rosterLoading=false;my.rerr='Kadro bilgisi şu anda görüntülenemiyor.'}
    else api('/roster?code='+encodeURIComponent(cd||'')+'&id='+encodeURIComponent(tid||'')+'&af='+encodeURIComponent(af)+'&tsdb='+encodeURIComponent(my.tsdb||'')+'&name='+encodeURIComponent(my.raw||my.name)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)).then(j=>{if(cur===my&&my.rosterReqToken===token&&Number(my.season)===season){my.roster=j.players||[];my.rosterLimited=!!j.limited;my.rosterNote=j.note||'';my.rosterLoading=false;renderTeam()}}).catch(e=>{if(cur===my&&my.rosterReqToken===token&&Number(my.season)===season){my.rerr=e.message||String(e);my.rosterLoading=false;renderTeam()}});
  }
  if(d&&t==='puan'){
    const options=teamCompetitionOptions(d),selected=options.find(x=>x.code===my.stComp)||options[0];
    if(selected){my.stComp=selected.code;my.stCompName=selected.name;const key=selected.code+':'+season;
      if(my.stByCode&&my.stByCode[key]){my.st=my.stByCode[key];my.sterr='';my.stLoading=false}
      else if(!my.stLoading||my.stLoadingCode!==selected.code){my.stLoading=true;my.stLoadingCode=selected.code;my.sterr='';my.st=null;const token=(my.stReqToken||0)+1;my.stReqToken=token;
        api('/pitch-team-standings?code='+encodeURIComponent(selected.code)+'&season='+encodeURIComponent(season)).then(j=>{const tb=j.tables||[];if(cur===my&&my.stReqToken===token){my.stByCode=my.stByCode||{};my.stByCode[key]=tb;my.st=tb;my.stLoading=false;if(!tb.length)my.sterr='Puan tablosu şu anda görüntülenemiyor.';renderTeam()}}).catch(e=>{if(cur===my&&my.stReqToken===token){my.stLoading=false;my.sterr=e.message||String(e);renderTeam()}});
      }
    }
  }
  if(d&&(t==='istat'||t==='liderler'||t==='ozet')&&!my.pitchTeamStats&&!my.pitchTeamStatsLoading)loadPitchTeamStats(my);
  if(t==='ozet'&&!my.teamStatsCode)my.teamStatsCode='all';if(t==='liderler')my.teamStatsMode='player';
  renderTeam();
}
function teamResultCls(m,team){
  if(!DONE.includes(stat(m))||m.goals.home==null||m.goals.away==null)return '';
  const mine=sameTeam(m.teams.home,team)?'home':sameTeam(m.teams.away,team)?'away':'';
  if(!mine)return '';
  const a=m.goals[mine],b=m.goals[mine==='home'?'away':'home'];
  return a>b?'rw':a<b?'rl':'rd';
}
function teamPlayerAverageRating(p){
  if(!p||!Number(p.ratingCount))return null;
  const value=Number(p.ratingTotal||0)/Number(p.ratingCount);
  return Number.isFinite(value)?value:null;
}
function teamPlayerLeaderboardRows(players,sort='all'){
  const rows=(Array.isArray(players)?players:[]).slice();
  if(sort==='goals')rows.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.goals)||0)-(Number(a.goals)||0)||(Number(b.assists)||0)-(Number(a.assists)||0)||(Number(b.apps)||0)-(Number(a.apps)||0));
  else if(sort==='assists')rows.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.assists)||0)-(Number(a.assists)||0)||(Number(b.goals)||0)-(Number(a.goals)||0)||(Number(b.apps)||0)-(Number(a.apps)||0));
  else if(sort==='yellow')rows.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.yellowCards)||0)-(Number(a.yellowCards)||0)||(Number(b.apps)||0)-(Number(a.apps)||0));
  else rows.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.goals)||0)-(Number(a.goals)||0)||(Number(b.assists)||0)-(Number(a.assists)||0)||(teamPlayerAverageRating(b)||0)-(teamPlayerAverageRating(a)||0));
  return rows.slice(0,10);
}
function renderTeam(){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='team')return;
  $('#shTitle').textContent=cur.name;
  const d=cur.data;
  const seasonList=(cur.seasons&&cur.seasons.length?cur.seasons:Array.from({length:20},(_,i)=>currentTeamSeason()-i));
  const seasonOptions=[...new Set([Number(cur.season),...seasonList.map(Number)].filter(Number.isFinite))].sort((a,b)=>b-a).map(y=>`<option value="${y}" ${Number(cur.season)===y?'selected':''}>${String(y).slice(-2)}-${String(y+1).slice(-2)}</option>`).join('');
  const seasonSelect=`<label class="team-season-select"><span>SEZON</span><select id="teamSeason" aria-label="Takım sezonunu değiştir">${seasonOptions}</select></label>`;
  const teamCountry=d&&d.team&&(d.team.country||d.team.country_name)||'';const teamFounded=d&&d.team&&(d.team.founded||d.team.foundation_year)||'';
  const fav=isTeamFavorite(cur.raw||cur.name);
  const head=`<div class="team-reference-head">${avatarT(d&&d.team&&d.team.logo||cur.logo,cur.name)}<div class="team-head-meta"><h2>${esc(cur.name)}</h2><small>${[teamCountry?'🌍 '+trC(teamCountry):'',teamFounded?'Kuruluş: '+teamFounded:''].filter(Boolean).map(esc).join(' · ')||'Takım profili'}</small></div><button type="button" class="team-fav-star ${fav?'is-fav':''}" data-team-fav="${esc(cur.raw||cur.name)}" aria-label="${fav?'Favorilerden çıkar':'Favorilere ekle'}">${fav?'★':'☆'}</button></div>`;
  const tabs=`<div class="stabs team-reference-tabs">${[['ozet','Özet'],['maclar','Maçlar'],['puan','Puan Durumu'],['istat','İstatistikler'],['liderler','En İyi Oyuncular'],['kadro','Kadro']].map(([k,l])=>`<div class="${cur.tab===k?'on':''}" data-tt="${k}">${l}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  if(cur.err){$('#shBody').innerHTML=head+'<div class="empty">'+nice(cur.err)+'</div>';return}
  if(!d){$('#shBody').innerHTML=head+tabs+'<div class="empty"><small>Yükleniyor…</small></div>';return}
  const team={id:d.team.id,name:cur.raw||cur.name,raw:cur.raw};
  let h=head+tabs;
  h+=`<div class="team-reference-filters"><label><span>Sezon</span><select id="teamSeason" aria-label="Takım sezonunu değiştir">${seasonOptions}</select></label>${cur.tab==='istat'||cur.tab==='liderler'?`<label class="team-reference-league"><span>Lig</span><select id="teamStatsCompetition" aria-label="İstatistik ligi">${teamCompetitionOptions(d,true).map(x=>`<option value="${esc(x.code)}" ${cur.teamStatsCode===x.code?'selected':''}>${esc(lgT({id:'x',name:x.name}).name)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label>`:''}</div>`;
  if(!d.groups.length&&d.warning)h+='<div class="pnote">Şu anda bu bölüm görüntülenemiyor. Lütfen daha sonra tekrar dene.</div>';
  if(cur.tab==='ozet'){
    const teamName=cur.raw||cur.name,now=Date.now();
    const allMatches=[...new Map((d.groups||[]).flatMap(group=>group.matches||[]).filter(m=>m&&m.teams&&(sameTeam(m.teams.home,team)||sameTeam(m.teams.away,team))).map(m=>[String(m.fixture&&m.fixture.id||[m.fixture&&m.fixture.date,m.teams.home.name,m.teams.away.name].join('|')),m])).values()];
    const next=allMatches.filter(m=>NS.includes(stat(m))&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(m.fixture&&m.fixture.date||''))&&Date.parse(m.fixture.date)>=now).sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(a.fixture.date)-Date.parse(b.fixture.date))[0];
    const previous=allMatches.filter(m=>DONE.includes(stat(m))&&m.goals&&m.goals.home!=null&&m.goals.away!=null&&Number.isFinite(Date.parse(m.fixture&&m.fixture.date||''))).sort((a,b)=>Date.parse(b.fixture.date)-Date.parse(a.fixture.date)).slice(0,5);
    const form=previous.map(m=>{const cls=teamResultCls(m,team);return cls==='rw'?'G':cls==='rd'?'B':cls==='rl'?'M':'—'});
    const players=Object.values(cur.pitchTeamStats&&cur.pitchTeamStats.players||{});
    const tops=(field)=>players.filter(p=>Number(p[field])>0).sort((a,b)=>(Number(b[field])||0)-(Number(a[field])||0)||(Number(b.apps)||0)-(Number(a.apps)||0)).slice(0,3);
    const scorerRows=tops('goals'),assistRows=tops('assists');
    const miniLeader=(rows,field)=>rows.length?rows.map((p,i)=>`<div class="team-summary-player" data-player-profile="${esc(p.name)}" data-player-team="${esc(teamName)}" data-pitch-id="${esc(/^p_[A-Za-z0-9]+$/.test(String(p.id||''))?p.id:'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(cur.teamStatsCode==='all'?cur.code:cur.teamStatsCode||cur.code)}" data-pitch-year="${esc(cur.season)}"><span>${i+1}</span>${avatar(p.photo,p.name)}<b>${esc(trPlayerName(p.name))}</b><strong>${Number(p[field])||0}</strong></div>`).join(''):'<div class="empty">İstatistik verisi bekleniyor…</div>';
    h+='<section class="team-summary-next"><div class="team-summary-section-head"><b>Sonraki Maç</b><small>'+(next?'':'Henüz açıklanmadı')+'</small></div>';
    if(next){const dt=new Date(next.fixture.date);h+=`<div class="team-summary-next-match"><div><small>${esc(next.league&&next.league.name||'Turnuva')} · ${esc(dt.toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'2-digit',month:'long'}))} · ${esc(dt.toLocaleTimeString('tr-TR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}))}</small></div><div class="team-summary-next-teams">${avatarT(next.teams.home.logo,next.teams.home.name)}<b>${esc(next.teams.home.name)}</b><span>VS</span><b>${esc(next.teams.away.name)}</b>${avatarT(next.teams.away.logo,next.teams.away.name)}</div></div>`}
    else h+='<div class="empty">Yaklaşan maç programı bulunamadı.</div>';
    h+='</section><section class="team-summary-form"><div class="team-summary-section-head"><b>Son Maç Formu</b><small>Son 5 tamamlanmış maç</small></div><div class="team-summary-form-pills">'+(form.length?form.map((x)=>`<span class="form-pill ${x==='G'?'win':x==='B'?'draw':x==='M'?'loss':''}">${x}</span>`).join(''):'<small>Form verisi yok</small>')+'</div></section>';
    h+='<section class="team-summary-leaders"><div class="team-summary-section-head"><b>En İyi Oyuncular</b><small>Sezon · Tüm Ligler</small></div><div class="team-summary-leader-cols"><div><h4>Golcüler</h4>'+miniLeader(scorerRows,'goals')+'</div><div><h4>Asistler</h4>'+miniLeader(assistRows,'assists')+'</div></div></section>';
    const roster=cur.roster||[],ages=roster.map(p=>Number(p.age)).filter(x=>Number.isFinite(x)&&x>14&&x<50),avgAge=ages.length?(ages.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/ages.length).toFixed(1):'—',u23=roster.filter(p=>Number(p.age)>0&&Number(p.age)<23).length;
    const countryKey=v=>{const k=playerNameKey(v);return ({turkey:'turkiye',turkiye:'turkiye',england:'ingiltere',unitedkingdom:'ingiltere',uk:'ingiltere',usa:'abd',unitedstates:'abd',germany:'almanya',spain:'ispanya',italy:'italya',france:'fransa'})[k]||k};const foreign=teamCountry?roster.filter(p=>p.nat||p.nationality).filter(p=>countryKey(p.nat||p.nationality)!==countryKey(teamCountry)).length:null;

    const founded=d.team&&(d.team.founded||d.team.foundation_year)||'';

    if(!cur.roster&&!cur.rosterLoading)teamTab('ozet');
    if(!cur.pitchTeamStats&&!cur.pitchTeamStatsLoading)teamTab('ozet');
  }else if(cur.tab==='maclar'){

    
    if(!d.groups.length)h+='<div class="empty">Bu takım için şu anda maç görünmüyor.</div>';
    else{
      const allMatches=[...new Map(d.groups.flatMap(group=>group.matches||[]).map(m=>[String(m.fixture&&m.fixture.id||[m.fixture&&m.fixture.date,m.teams.home.name,m.teams.away.name].join('|')),m])).values()],gi=cur.comp;

      }).join('');
    }
  }else if(cur.tab==='kadro'){

    else if(!cur.roster)h+='<div class="empty"><small>Kadro yükleniyor…</small></div>';
    else if(!cur.roster.length)h+='<div class="empty">Kadro bilgisi şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</div>';

  }else if(cur.tab==='puan'){

      h+='<section class="team-reference-stat-section"><h3>Takım İstatistikleri</h3><div class="team-stat-grid">'+stats.map(([l,v])=>`<div class="team-stat-card"><small>${l}</small><b>${v==null?'—':v}</b></div>`).join('')+'</div></section>';
    }else if(cur.tab==='istat'){
      const allPlayers=Object.values(data.players||{}).sort((a,b)=>(teamPlayerAverageRating(b)||0)-(teamPlayerAverageRating(a)||0)||(Number(b.apps)||0)-(Number(a.apps)||0));
      h+='<div class="team-stat-player-list"><div class="team-stat-player-head"><span>Oyuncular</span><span>O</span><span>G</span><span>A</span><span>İ11</span><span>S</span><span>K</span><span>Puan</span></div>';
      if(!allPlayers.length)h+='<div class="empty">Şu anda görüntülenecek oyuncu istatistiği yok.</div>';
      else h+=allPlayers.slice(0,35).map(p=>`<div class="team-stat-player-row" data-player-profile="${esc(p.name)}" data-player-team="${esc(cur.name)}" data-player-pos="${esc(p.position||'')}" data-pitch-id="${esc(/^p_[A-Za-z0-9]+$/.test(String(p.id||''))?p.id:'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(cur.teamStatsCode==='all'?cur.code:cur.teamStatsCode||cur.code||'')}" data-pitch-year="${esc(cur.season)}"><span class="team-stat-player-name">${avatar(p.photo,p.name)}<span><b>${esc(trPlayerName(p.name))}</b><small>${esc(posTR(p.position||''))}</small></span></span><span>${p.apps||0}</span><span>${p.goals||0}</span><span>${p.assists||0}</span><span>${p.starts==null?'—':p.starts}</span><span>${p.yellowCards||0}</span><span>${p.redCards||0}</span><b>${teamPlayerAverageRating(p)==null?'—':teamPlayerAverageRating(p).toFixed(1)}</b></div>`).join('');
      h+='</div>';
    }else{
      const sort=cur.teamPlayerSort||'goals';
      const sortTabs=[['goals','Golcüler'],['assists','Asistler'],['yellow','Sarı Kartlar']];
      h+='<div class="team-leader-filter reference-leader-filter">'+sortTabs.map(([key,label])=>`<button type="button" class="${sort===key?'on':''}" data-tplayersort="${key}">${label}</button>`).join('')+'</div>';
      const players=teamPlayerLeaderboardRows(Object.values(data.players||{}),sort).slice(0,15);
      const heading=({goals:'Golcüler',assists:'Asistler',yellow:'Sarı Kartlar'})[sort]||'Golcüler';

      if(!players.length)h+='<div class="empty">Şu anda görüntülenecek oyuncu istatistiği yok.</div>';
      else h+='<div class="team-leader-list">'+players.map((p,i)=>{const value=sort==='goals'?p.goals:sort==='assists'?p.assists:p.yellowCards;return `<div class="team-leader-row" data-player-profile="${esc(p.name)}" data-player-team="${esc(cur.name)}" data-player-pos="${esc(p.position||'')}" data-pitch-id="${esc(/^p_[A-Za-z0-9]+$/.test(String(p.id||''))?p.id:'')}" data-pitch-code="${esc(cur.teamStatsCode==='all'?cur.code:cur.teamStatsCode||cur.code||'')}" data-pitch-year="${esc(cur.season)}"><span class="team-leader-rank">${i+1}</span>${avatar(p.photo,p.name)}<span class="team-leader-player"><b>${esc(trPlayerName(p.name))}</b><small>${esc(posTR(p.position||''))} · ${p.apps||0} maç</small></span><b class="team-leader-value">${value==null?'—':value}</b></div>`}).join('')+'</div>';
    }
    h+='<div class="pnote">'+(data.done?'PitchAPI istatistikleri yüklendi.':'Maç verileri arka planda tamamlanıyor…')+(cur.pitchTeamStatsFailedCompetitions&&cur.pitchTeamStatsFailedCompetitions.length?' '+cur.pitchTeamStatsFailedCompetitions.length+' lig/kupa için veri alınamadı; diğer bulunan veriler gösteriliyor.':'')+'</div>';
  }
  $('#shBody').innerHTML=h;
}

const slugOf=u=>{const p=String(u||'').split('/').filter(Boolean);const i=p.indexOf('player');return i>=0&&p[i+1]?p[i+1]:''};
function findPlayers(j){return j&&Array.isArray(j.items)?j.items.filter(x=>x&&x.name):[]}
const photoList=src=>(Array.isArray(src)?src:[src]).filter((x,i,a)=>typeof x==='string'&&x&&a.indexOf(x)===i);
const EMPTY_PHOTO='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="8" r="4" fill="currentColor"/><path d="M4 21c.5-4.1 3.7-6.5 8-6.5s7.5 2.4 8 6.5" fill="currentColor"/></svg>';
const EMPTY_BADGE='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M12 2.5 20 5v6.2c0 5.1-3.2 8.4-8 10.3-4.8-1.9-8-5.2-8-10.3V5z" fill="currentColor"/><path d="M8 12h8M12 8v8" stroke="#1b2430" stroke-width="1.5" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>';
const avatar=(src,name)=>{const imgs=photoList(src);return imgs.length?`<span class="avw"><img class="avi" data-fallbacks="${esc(JSON.stringify(imgs.slice(1)))}" decoding="async" referrerpolicy="no-referrer" src="${esc(imgs[0])}" alt="" loading="eager"></span>`:`<span class="avw photo-placeholder" aria-label="Oyuncu fotoğrafı">${EMPTY_PHOTO}</span>`};
const avatarT=(src,name)=>{const all=photoList(src),imgs=[...all.filter(x=>/\.png(?:[?#]|$)/i.test(x)),...all.filter(x=>!/\.png(?:[?#]|$)/i.test(x))];return imgs.length?`<span class="avt"><img class="avi" data-fallbacks="${esc(JSON.stringify(imgs.slice(1)))}" src="${esc(imgs[0])}" alt="" loading="eager"></span>`:`<span class="avt photo-placeholder" aria-label="Takım logosu">${EMPTY_BADGE}</span>`};
document.addEventListener('load',e=>{const t=e.target;if(t&&t.classList&&t.classList.contains('avi'))t.classList.add('photo-loaded')},true);
document.addEventListener('error',e=>{const t=e.target;if(!t||!t.classList)return;if(t.classList.contains('avi')){let xs=[];try{xs=JSON.parse(t.dataset.fallbacks||'[]')}catch(_){}if(xs.length){t.dataset.fallbacks=JSON.stringify(xs.slice(1));t.src=xs[0];return}const holder=t.closest('.avw,.avt');if(holder){holder.classList.add('photo-placeholder');holder.innerHTML=holder.classList.contains('avw')?EMPTY_PHOTO:EMPTY_BADGE}return}if(t.classList.contains('career-logo')){let xs=[];try{xs=JSON.parse(t.dataset.fallbacks||'[]')}catch(_){}if(xs.length){t.dataset.fallbacks=JSON.stringify(xs.slice(1));t.src=xs[0]}else t.remove()}},true);
function openSearch(){
  showSheet();
  cur={kind:'search',type:'player',q:'',items:null,err:'',loading:false,raw:null,prev:cur&&cur.kind!=='menu'?cur:null};
  renderSearch();
  loadLeagueLogos();
}
let psTimer=null,psSeq=0;
const ST_TYPES=[['player','Futbolcu'],['coach','Teknik Direktör'],['team','Takım'],['league','Lig']];
function renderSearch(){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='search')return;
  $('#shTitle').textContent='Arama';
  if(!$('#psq')){
    $('#shBody').innerHTML='<div class="psb"><input id="psq" class="search" type="search" placeholder="Oyuncunun adını yazmaya başla…" autocomplete="off"></div><div id="pst"></div><div id="psr"></div>';
    $('#psq').value=cur.q;
    $('#psq').addEventListener('input',e=>{
      cur.q=e.target.value.trim();clearTimeout(psTimer);psSeq++;
      cur.items=null;cur.fastItems=[];cur.err='';cur.raw=null;cur.partial=false;
      if(cur.q.length<1){cur.loading=false;renderSearch();return}
      cur.loading=true;renderSearch();
      psTimer=setTimeout(doSearch,65);
    });
    if(!cur.q)setTimeout(()=>{const i=$('#psq');if(i)i.focus()},60);
  }
  $('#pst').innerHTML='<div class="stabs">'+ST_TYPES.map(([k,l])=>`<div class="${cur.type===k?'on':''}" data-st="${k}">${l}</div>`).join('')+'</div>';
  const T=cur.type,nm={player:'futbolcu',coach:'teknik direktör',team:'takım',league:'lig'}[T];
  let h;
  if(cur.loading&&cur.items==null)h='<div class="empty">Hızlı sonuçlar aranıyor…</div>';
  else if(cur.err&&!cur.items?.length)h='<div class="empty">'+nice(cur.err)+'</div>';
  else if(cur.items==null)h='<div class="empty">Bir '+nm+' adı yaz.<br><small>Yazdıkça sonuçlar görünür.</small></div>';
  else if(!cur.items.length)h=cur.partial?'<div class="empty">Arama devam ediyor…</div>':'<div class="empty">Şu anda eşleşen sonuç yok.</div>';
  else h=cur.items.map((p,i)=>{const portraits=[...(Array.isArray(p.photos)?p.photos:[]),p.logo,p.photo,p.image];const country=p.country||p.nationality||p.citizenship||'';return T==='player'?`<div class="pr player-search-row" data-pr="${i}">${avatar(portraits,p.name)}<div class="player-search-meta"><b>${esc(trPlayerName(p.name))}</b><small><span class="player-search-ball">⚽</span>${esc(country||p.team||'')}</small></div><button type="button" class="player-search-star" data-player-search-star="${i}" aria-label="Oyuncuyu favorilere ekle" title="Favorilere ekle">☆</button></div>`:`<div class="pr" data-pr="${i}">${T==='league'?avatarT(leagueSearchLogoSources(p),p.name):T==='team'?avatarT(p.logo,p.name):avatar(portraits,p.name)}<div>${esc(T==='team'?trTeam(p.name):p.name)}<small>${esc(T==='team'||T==='league'?(p.team||''):[p.team,posTR(p.pos)].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small></div>${T==='league'&&!p.code?'':'<span class="go">›</span>'}</div>`}).join('')+(cur.partial?'<div class="pnote">Arama devam ediyor…</div>':'')+rawBlock(cur.raw);
  $('#psr').innerHTML=h;
}
const srcText=j=>'';
const srcShort=j=>'';
const rawBlock=j=>'';
function mergeSearchPhotos(fullItems, fastItems){
  const norm=s=>String(s||'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim();
  const fast=Array.isArray(fastItems)?fastItems:[];
  return (Array.isArray(fullItems)?fullItems:[]).map(item=>{
    const sameName=fast.filter(x=>norm(x.name)===norm(item.name));
    const sameTeam=sameName.filter(x=>!item.team||!x.team||norm(x.team)===norm(item.team));
    // Kaynaklar aynı kulübü farklı adla yazabiliyor. Ad tekilse hızlı aramadaki
    // fotoğrafı koru; aynı adlı birden fazla oyuncu varsa takım eşleşmesini şart koş.
    const match=sameTeam.length===1?sameTeam[0]:(sameName.length===1?sameName[0]:null);
    if(!match)return item;
    const photos=[...(Array.isArray(item.photos)?item.photos:[]),...(Array.isArray(match.photos)?match.photos:[]),item.photo,match.photo,item.logo,match.logo,item.image,match.image].filter((v,i,a)=>typeof v==='string'&&/^https?:\/\//i.test(v)&&a.indexOf(v)===i);
    return {...item,photos,logo:item.logo||photos[0]||'',photo:item.photo||photos[0]||'',tm:item.tm||match.tm||'',tsdb:item.tsdb||match.tsdb||'',af:item.af||match.af||'',espn:item.espn||match.espn||'',slug:item.slug||match.slug||'',wiki:item.wiki||match.wiki||''};
  });
}
async function doSearch(){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='search'||!cur.q)return;
  const my=cur,q=my.q,type=my.type,seq=++psSeq;
  my.loading=true;my.partial=false;my.err='';renderSearch();
  const path='/search?q='+encodeURIComponent(q)+'&type='+encodeURIComponent(type);
  try{
    const fast=await api(path+'&fast=1');
    if(cur!==my||seq!==psSeq)return;
    my.raw=fast;my.fastItems=findPlayers(fast);my.items=my.fastItems;my.loading=false;my.partial=true;renderSearch();
  }catch(e){
    if(cur!==my||seq!==psSeq)return;
    my.loading=false;my.partial=true;my.err='';renderSearch();
  }
  try{
    const full=await api(path);
    if(cur!==my||seq!==psSeq)return;
    my.raw=full;my.items=mergeSearchPhotos(findPlayers(full),my.fastItems||[]);my.loading=false;my.partial=false;my.err='';renderSearch();
  }catch(e){
    if(cur!==my||seq!==psSeq)return;
    my.loading=false;my.partial=false;
    if(!my.items||!my.items.length){my.err=e.message||String(e);my.items=null}
    renderSearch();
  }
}
function pitchClientTeamKey(v){return careerTeamClean(v).normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLocaleLowerCase('tr-TR').replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,' ').trim()}
const PITCH_TEAM_LEAGUES={

function guessPitchCodeFromTeam(team){const key=pitchClientTeamKey(team);if(!key)return '';if(PITCH_TEAM_LEAGUES[key])return PITCH_TEAM_LEAGUES[key];const found=Object.keys(PITCH_TEAM_LEAGUES).find(k=>key===k||key.includes(k)||k.includes(key));return found?PITCH_TEAM_LEAGUES[found]:''}
function teamsFromPlayerAbout(about){const text=pitchClientTeamKey(about);const teams=Object.keys(PITCH_TEAM_LEAGUES).sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length);return teams.filter(name=>text.includes(name))}
function teamFromPlayerAbout(about){return teamsFromPlayerAbout(about)[0]||''}
function maybeStartPitchPlayerCareer(my){if(my){my.pitchCareerLoading=false;my.pitchCareerLoaded=true;my.pitchCareerRows=[];}}

function pitchCareerCategoryClient(code,name){
  const c=String(code||'').toLowerCase(),n=String(name||'').toLowerCase();
  if(/^(uefa|conmebol|afc|caf|concacaf|ofc|fifa)\./.test(c)||/champions league|europa league|conference league|uefa|libertadores|sudamericana|world cup|nations league/.test(n))return 'intl';
  return 'lig';
}
function mergePitchCareerIntoStats(stats, incoming){
  if(!stats||!Array.isArray(incoming)||!incoming.length)return;
  if(!Array.isArray(stats.seasons))stats.seasons=[];
  const fields=['Maç','Gol','Asist','İlk 11','Sarı Kart','Kırmızı Kart','Toplam Dakika','Puan Toplam','Puan Maç'];
  const grouped=new Map();
  for(const row of incoming){
    if(!row)continue;const season=careerSeasonYear(row['Sezon']),teamKey=pitchClientTeamKey(row['Takım']);if(!season||!teamKey)continue;
    const key=season+'|'+teamKey;
    if(!grouped.has(key))grouped.set(key,{season:row['Sezon'],team:row['Takım'],logo:row.logo||row.teamLogo||row['Takım Logosu']||'',logoCandidates:row.logoCandidates||[],teamId:row.teamId||'',teamCode:row.teamCode||'',teamAfId:row.teamAfId||'',afId:row.afId||'',tsdbId:row.tsdbId||'',codes:new Set(),totals:Object.fromEntries(fields.map(f=>[f,0])),known:Object.fromEntries(fields.map(f=>[f,true])),cats:{lig:{seen:false,a:0,s:0,y:0,r:0,known:{a:true,s:true,y:true,r:true}},intl:{seen:false,a:0,s:0,y:0,r:0,known:{a:true,s:true,y:true,r:true}}}});
    const g=grouped.get(key),code=String(row.pitchCode||'');if(code&&g.codes.has(code))continue;if(code)g.codes.add(code);
    for(const field of fields){const value=row[field];if(value==null||value==='')g.known[field]=false;else g.totals[field]+=Number(value)||0;}
    const category=String(row.pitchCategory||pitchCareerCategoryClient(code,row['Turnuva']||'')).toLowerCase(),bucket=g.cats[category];if(bucket){bucket.seen=true;for(const [field,k] of [['Asist','a'],['İlk 11','s'],['Sarı Kart','y'],['Kırmızı Kart','r']]){const value=row[field];if(value==null||value==='')bucket.known[k]=false;else bucket[k]+=Number(value)||0;}}
  }
  for(const g of grouped.values()){
    let target=stats.seasons.find(row=>careerSeasonYear(row&&row['Sezon'])===careerSeasonYear(g.season)&&pitchClientTeamKey(row&&row['Takım'])===pitchClientTeamKey(g.team));
    if(!target){target={'Sezon':g.season,'Takım':g.team};stats.seasons.push(target);}
    target.pitchApiFields=target.pitchApiFields||{};
    // PitchAPI'nin güncel maç bazlı toplamı, eski sezon tablosundaki eski Maç/Gol/Asist değerlerini ezer.
    for(const field of fields){if(g.known[field]){target[field]=g.totals[field];target.pitchApiFields[field]=true;}}
    if(g.known['Puan Toplam']&&g.known['Puan Maç']&&g.totals['Puan Maç']>0)target['Ort. Puan']=Number((g.totals['Puan Toplam']/g.totals['Puan Maç']).toFixed(2));
    target.pitchApiByCategory=target.pitchApiByCategory||{};
    for(const cat of ['lig','intl']){const source=g.cats[cat];if(!source.seen)continue;target.pitchApiByCategory[cat]={a:source.known.a?source.a:null,s:source.known.s?source.s:null,y:source.known.y?source.y:null,r:source.known.r?source.r:null,known:{...source.known}};}
    if(!target.logo&&g.logo)target.logo=g.logo;if(!target.logoCandidates&&g.logoCandidates)target.logoCandidates=g.logoCandidates;
    if(!target.teamId&&g.teamId)target.teamId=g.teamId;if(!target.teamAfId&&g.teamAfId)target.teamAfId=g.teamAfId;
  }
}
async function loadPitchPlayerCareer(my){
  // Kariyer sezonları /player cevabındaki Transfermarkt kayıtlarından gelir.
  // Bu yardımcı fonksiyon geriye dönük çağrı uyumluluğu için korunur;
  // PitchAPI üzerinden ikinci bir sezon tablosu yüklemez.
  if(!my||!my.it||my.pitchCareerLoading||my.pitchCareerLoaded)return;
  if(!my.stats||!Array.isArray(my.stats.seasons))return;
  my.pitchCareerLoading=false;
  my.pitchCareerLoaded=true;
  my.pitchCareerError='';
  my.pitchCareerProgress='';
  my.pitchCareerRows=[];
  my.stats.sources=my.stats.sources||{};
  my.stats.sources.pitch_api='devre dışı · oyuncu kariyer sezonları Transfermarkt kaynağından';
  my.stats.sources.transfermarkt=my.stats.seasons.length
    ? 'ok · '+my.stats.seasons.length+' sezon/takım satırı'
    : 'Transfermarkt sezon kaydı bulunamadı';
  renderPlayer();
}
const playerStatsCacheKey=name=>'skorhane.playerstats.legacy.v7.'+pitchClientTeamKey(name||'');
function readPlayerStatsCache(name){try{const raw=localStorage.getItem(playerStatsCacheKey(name));if(!raw)return null;const x=JSON.parse(raw);return x&&Date.now()-Number(x.savedAt||0)<7*86400e3&&x.data&&Array.isArray(x.data.seasons)?x.data:null}catch(_){return null}}
function savePlayerStatsCache(name,data){try{if(data&&Array.isArray(data.seasons))localStorage.setItem(playerStatsCacheKey(name),JSON.stringify({savedAt:Date.now(),data}))}catch(_){}}
function playerPitchLeagueCandidates(my){
  if(!my)return [];
  const info=my.info||{},bio=info.bio||my.stats&&my.stats.bio||{},about=info.about||'';
  const team=my.it.team||my.it.teamName||bio['Şu Anki Takım']||bio['Takım']||teamFromPlayerAbout(about)||'';
  const codes=[],add=value=>{const code=String(value||'').trim();if(code&&/^(?:[a-z]{2,6}\.[a-z0-9._-]+|af-\d+)$/i.test(code)&&!codes.includes(code))codes.push(code)};
  add(my.it.pitchCode);add(my.it.pitchLeagueCode);add(my.it.leagueCode);add(my.it.competitionCode);add(guessPitchCodeFromTeam(team));
  if(my.prev&&my.prev.code)add(my.prev.code);
  for(const row of (my.stats&&my.stats.seasons||[]).concat(my.stats&&my.stats.national||[])){
    if(!row)continue;
    for(const value of [row.pitchCode,row.pitchLeagueCode,row.leagueCode,row.league_code,row.code,row.competitionCode,row.league&&row.league.code])add(value);
    const club=row['Takım']||row.team||row.club||'';add(guessPitchCodeFromTeam(club));
    const competition=row['Lig']||row['Turnuva']||row.competitionName||row.leagueName||'';if(typeof competition==='string')add(teamCompetitionCodeFromName(competition));
  }
  for(const club of teamsFromPlayerAbout(about))add(guessPitchCodeFromTeam(club));
  const companion={'tur.1':['tur.cup'],'eng.1':['eng.fa','eng.league_cup'],'esp.1':['esp.copa_del_rey'],'ger.1':['ger.dfb_pokal'],'ita.1':['ita.coppa_italia'],'fra.1':['fra.coupe_de_france'],'ned.1':['ned.cup'],'por.1':['por.taca.portugal']};
  const european=new Set(['tur.1','eng.1','esp.1','ger.1','ita.1','fra.1','ned.1','por.1','bel.1','sco.1','aut.1','sui.1','den.1','swe.1','nor.1','pol.1','gre.1','cyp.1','ukr.1','rus.1','srb.1','cro.1','rou.1','hun.1']);
  for(const code of [...codes]){for(const cup of companion[code]||[])add(cup);if(european.has(code)){add('uefa.champions');add('uefa.europa');add('uefa.europa.conf')}}
  return {codes,team};
}
async function loadPitchPlayerRecent(my){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='player'||my.it.role==='coach'||my.pitchRecentLoading||my.pitchRecentLoaded)return;
  const resolved=playerPitchLeagueCandidates(my),knownCodes=resolved.codes,team=resolved.team;
  const currentTeamCode=guessPitchCodeFromTeam(team)||String(my.it.pitchCode||my.it.pitchLeagueCode||'');
  const codes=[...new Set([currentTeamCode,...knownCodes].filter(Boolean))].slice(0,6);
  const trustedContext=!!(team||(my.prev&&['team','league'].includes(my.prev.kind)&&my.prev.code));
  if(!trustedContext&&!my.infoLoaded)return;
  if(!trustedContext){my.pitchRecentError='Oyuncunun güncel takımı veya ligi doğrulanamadı; yanlış ligden maç göstermemek için arama durduruldu.';my.pitchRecentLoaded=true;renderPlayer();return;}
  if(!codes.length){my.pitchRecentError='Oyuncunun güncel ligi belirlenemedi.';my.pitchRecentLoaded=true;renderPlayer();return;}
  my.pitchRecentLoading=true;my.pitchRecentError='';renderPlayer();
  const requestCode=async(code,limit=13)=>{const params=new URLSearchParams({player:String(my.it.pitchId||''),name:String(my.it.name||''),team:String(team||my.it.team||my.it.teamName||''),code,season:String(Number(my.it.pitchYear)||currentTeamSeason()),limit:String(limit)});return api('/pitch-player-recent?'+params.toString())};
  const mergeResult=(code,data)=>{
    if(!data||!Array.isArray(data.matches))return false;
    const merged=new Map((my.pitchRecentMatches||[]).map(match=>[String(match.id||[match.date,match.home&&match.home.name,match.away&&match.away.name].join('|')),match]));
    for(const match of data.matches){const key=String(match.id||[match.date,match.home&&match.home.name,match.away&&match.away.name].join('|'));if(!merged.has(key))merged.set(key,match);}
    my.pitchRecentMatches=[...merged.values()].sort((a,b)=>String(b.date||'').localeCompare(String(a.date||''))).slice(0,40);
    if(data.matches.length&&(!my.pitchRecentCode||!my.pitchRecentMatches.length||code===currentTeamCode)){my.pitchRecentCode=code;my.pitchRecentTeam=data.team||team||my.pitchRecentTeam||'';my.pitchRecentTeamLogo=data.teamLogo||my.pitchRecentTeamLogo||'';my.pitchRecentLeague=data.league||my.pitchRecentLeague||'';my.pitchRecentSeason=data.season||my.pitchRecentSeason||'';}
    if(data.seasonStats&&(!my.pitchRecentSummary||code===currentTeamCode))my.pitchRecentSummary=data.seasonStats;
    my.pitchRecentPlayerId=data.playerId||my.it.pitchId||my.pitchRecentPlayerId||'';if(my.pitchRecentPlayerId)my.it.pitchId=my.pitchRecentPlayerId;
    const portrait=my.pitchRecentMatches.find(match=>match&&match.player&&/^https:\/\//i.test(String(match.player.photo||'')))?.player;
    if(portrait&&portrait.photo){my.pitchPhoto=portrait.photo;my.it.logo=my.it.logo||portrait.photo;if(my.info)my.info.pitchPhoto=portrait.photo;}
    return data.matches.length>0;
  };
  const primaryCode=currentTeamCode||codes[0];
  let primaryOk=false,lastError=null;
  try{
    const primary=await requestCode(primaryCode,13);
    if(cur!==my)return;
    primaryOk=mergeResult(primaryCode,primary);
    my.pitchRecentFailed=Number(primary.failedMatches)||0;
    my.pitchRecentLoaded=true;my.pitchRecentLoading=false;
    if(!primaryOk)my.pitchRecentError=primary&&primary.message||'';
    renderPlayer();
  }catch(error){lastError=error;my.pitchRecentLoaded=true;my.pitchRecentLoading=false;my.pitchRecentError='';renderPlayer();}
  // Profil ilk ligden veri geldiği anda açılır. Kupa/Avrupa müsabakaları daha sonra eklenir.
  const secondaryCodes=codes.filter(code=>code!==primaryCode);
  const secondaryJobs=secondaryCodes.map(async code=>{try{const data=await requestCode(code,13);if(cur!==my)return;const added=mergeResult(code,data);if(added){my.pitchRecentError='';renderPlayer();}}catch(error){lastError=lastError||error;}});
  // Daha geniş sezon taraması arka planda devam eder; ilk ekranı bekletmez.
  const deepJob=requestCode(primaryCode,60).then(full=>{if(cur!==my)return;mergeResult(primaryCode,full);my.pitchRecentFailed=Number(full.failedMatches)||my.pitchRecentFailed||0;renderPlayer();}).catch(()=>{});
  await Promise.allSettled(secondaryJobs);
  await deepJob;
  if(cur!==my)return;
  if(!my.pitchRecentMatches.length)my.pitchRecentError=lastError&&lastError.message||'PitchAPI bu oyuncu için güncel sezonda maç kaydı döndürmedi.';
  my.pitchRecentLoaded=true;my.pitchRecentLoading=false;renderPlayer();
}
async function loadPitchPlayerProfilePhoto(my){
  if(!my||my!==cur||my.kind!=='player'||my.it.role==='coach'||my.pitchProfilePhotoLoading||my.pitchProfilePhotoLoaded)return;
  const bio=my.info&&my.info.bio||my.stats&&my.stats.bio||{};
  const team=String(my.it.team||my.it.teamName||bio['Şu Anki Takım']||bio['Takım']||'').trim();
  const code=String(my.it.pitchCode||my.it.pitchLeagueCode||my.it.leagueCode||guessPitchCodeFromTeam(team)||my.prev&&my.prev.code||'').trim();
  if(!team||!code)return;
  my.pitchProfilePhotoLoading=true;
  const params=new URLSearchParams({name:String(my.it.name||''),team,code,season:String(Number(my.it.pitchYear)||currentTeamSeason()),player:String(my.it.pitchId||''),pos:String(my.it.pos||my.it.position||'')});
  try{const result=await api('/pitch-player-photo?'+params.toString());const photo=String(result&&result.photo||'');if(cur===my&&/^https:\/\//i.test(photo)){my.pitchPhoto=photo;my.pitchProfilePhotoLoaded=true;renderPlayer();}}
  catch(_){}
  finally{if(cur===my)my.pitchProfilePhotoLoading=false;}
}
function scheduleTmRefreshProbe(my,qs,attempt=0){
  const maxAttempts=36;
  if(!my||cur!==my||my.tmRefreshTimer||attempt>=maxAttempts)return;
  my.tmRefreshTimer=setTimeout(async()=>{
    my.tmRefreshTimer=null;
    if(cur!==my)return;
    my.tmRefreshStatus='checking';
    try{
      // Reload both halves of the player screen together: profile/summary and all Transfermarkt history tabs.
      const [freshInfo,freshStats]=await Promise.all([
        api('/player?part=info'+qs),
        api('/player?part=stats'+qs)
      ]);
      if(cur!==my)return;
      my.info=freshInfo;my.infoLoaded=true;my.err='';
      my.stats=freshStats;my.statsLoaded=true;my.serr='';
      if(Array.isArray(my.pitchCareerRows))mergePitchCareerIntoStats(my.stats,my.pitchCareerRows);
      savePlayerStatsCache(my.it.name,freshStats);
      const pending=!!(freshInfo.tmRefreshRequired||freshStats.tmRefreshRequired);
      const queued=!!(freshInfo.tmRefreshQueued||freshStats.tmRefreshQueued);
      const state=String(freshStats.tmRefreshState||freshInfo.tmRefreshState||'');
      if(!pending){
        my.tmRefreshStatus='completed';my.tmRefreshFailure='';my.tmRefreshRetryAt='';
        renderPlayer();
        loadPitchPlayerProfilePhoto(my);
        if(my.tab==='kariyer')maybeStartPitchPlayerCareer(my,my.info);
        return;
      }
      if(queued&&attempt<maxAttempts-1){
        my.tmRefreshStatus='queued';
        renderPlayer();
        scheduleTmRefreshProbe(my,qs,attempt+1);
        return;
      }
      my.tmRefreshStatus=state==='retry-wait'?'retry-wait':(queued?'waiting':'failed');
      my.tmRefreshRetryAt=freshStats.tmRefreshRetryAt||freshInfo.tmRefreshRetryAt||'';
      my.tmRefreshFailure=freshStats.tmRefreshReason||freshInfo.tmRefreshReason||'';
      renderPlayer();
    }catch(error){
      if(cur!==my)return;
      if(attempt<maxAttempts-1){
        my.tmRefreshStatus='queued';
        scheduleTmRefreshProbe(my,qs,attempt+1);
      }else{
        my.tmRefreshStatus='failed';
        my.tmRefreshFailure=error&&error.message||String(error);
      }
    }
  },attempt===0?6000:12000);
}
function openPlayer(it){
  if(!it||!it.name){return}
  const cachedLegacyStats=it.role==='coach'?null:readPlayerStatsCache(it.name);
  cur={kind:'player',prev:cur,it,info:null,stats:cachedLegacyStats,err:'',serr:'',tab:'ozet',f:'toplam',pitchCareerLoading:false,pitchRecentLoading:false,pitchRecentLoaded:false,pitchRecentMatches:[],pitchRecentCompetition:'all',pitchRecentError:'',pitchCareerLoaded:false,pitchCareerError:'',pitchCareerProgress:'',tmRefreshTimer:null,tmRefreshStatus:'',tmRefreshFailure:'',tmRefreshRetryAt:''};
  $('#shTitle').textContent=trPlayerName(it.name);$('#sheet').scrollTop=0;
  renderPlayer();
  const my=cur,qs='&slug='+encodeURIComponent(it.slug||slugOf(it.url||it.link)||'')+'&name='+encodeURIComponent(it.name)+'&tm='+encodeURIComponent(it.tm||it.transfermarktId||'')+'&tsdb='+encodeURIComponent(it.tsdb||it.tsdbPlayerId||'')+'&af='+encodeURIComponent(it.af||it.afPlayerId||'')+'&espn='+encodeURIComponent(it.espn||it.espnPlayerId||'')+'&team='+encodeURIComponent(it.team||it.teamName||'')+'&pos='+encodeURIComponent(it.pos||it.position||'')+'&wiki='+encodeURIComponent(it.wiki||'')+'&pitch='+encodeURIComponent(it.pitchId||'')+'&pitchCode='+encodeURIComponent(it.pitchCode||it.pitchLeagueCode||guessPitchCodeFromTeam(it.team||it.teamName||'')||'')+'&pitchYear='+encodeURIComponent(it.pitchYear||'')+'&photo='+encodeURIComponent((Array.isArray(it.photos)&&it.photos[0])||it.logo||it.photo||'')+(it.role==='coach'?'&role=coach':'');
  my.infoLoaded=false;my.statsLoaded=false;
  loadPitchPlayerProfilePhoto(my);loadPitchPlayerRecent(my);
  api('/player?part=info'+qs).then(j=>{if(cur===my){my.info=j;my.infoLoaded=true;renderPlayer();if(j.tmRefreshRequired&&j.tmRefreshQueued)scheduleTmRefreshProbe(my,qs,0);loadPitchPlayerProfilePhoto(my);loadPitchPlayerRecent(my);if(my.tab==='kariyer')maybeStartPitchPlayerCareer(my,j)}}).catch(e=>{if(cur===my){my.err=e.message||String(e);my.infoLoaded=true;renderPlayer();loadPitchPlayerProfilePhoto(my);loadPitchPlayerRecent(my);if(my.tab==='kariyer')maybeStartPitchPlayerCareer(my,my.info)}});
  api('/player?part=stats'+qs).then(j=>{if(cur===my){my.stats=j;my.statsLoaded=true;savePlayerStatsCache(it.name,j);if(Array.isArray(my.pitchCareerRows))mergePitchCareerIntoStats(my.stats,my.pitchCareerRows);renderPlayer();if(j.tmRefreshRequired&&j.tmRefreshQueued)scheduleTmRefreshProbe(my,qs,0);loadPitchPlayerRecent(my);if(my.tab==='kariyer')maybeStartPitchPlayerCareer(my,my.info)}}).catch(e=>{if(cur===my){my.serr=e.message||String(e);my.statsLoaded=true;if(Array.isArray(my.pitchCareerRows)&&my.pitchCareerRows.length){my.stats={player:{name:it.name},bio:{},seasons:[],national:[],sources:{}};mergePitchCareerIntoStats(my.stats,my.pitchCareerRows)}renderPlayer();loadPitchPlayerRecent(my);if(my.tab==='kariyer')maybeStartPitchPlayerCareer(my,my.info)}});
}
const PL_TR={season:'Sezon',year:'Sezon',team:'Takım',club:'Takım',competition:'Lig',league:'Lig',tournament:'Lig',appearances:'Maç',matches:'Maç',games:'Maç',played:'Maç',matches_played:'Maç',starts:'İlk 11',minutes:'Dakika',minutes_played:'Dakika',goals:'Gol',assists:'Asist',yellow_cards:'Sarı',yellow:'Sarı',red_cards:'Kırmızı',red:'Kırmızı',rating:'Puan',average_rating:'Puan',name:'Ad',position:'Mevki',nationality:'Uyruk',country:'Ülke',age:'Yaş',height:'Boy',weight:'Kilo',foot:'Ayak',preferred_foot:'Ayak',shirt_number:'Forma No',number:'Forma No',birth_date:'Doğum Tarihi',date_of_birth:'Doğum Tarihi',market_value:'Piyasa Değeri',contract_until:'Sözleşme',shots:'Şut',shots_on_target:'İsabetli Şut',passes:'Pas',tackles:'Top Kapma',clean_sheets:'Gol Yemeden',saves:'Kurtarış',penalties:'Penaltı',seasons:'Sezonlar',stats:'İstatistikler',statistics:'İstatistikler',career:'Kariyer',total:'Toplam',summary:'Özet',history:'Geçmiş',transfers:'Transferler',honours:'Başarılar',trophies:'Kupalar',height_cm:'Boy (cm)',weight_kg:'Kilo (kg)',jersey_number:'Forma No',fouls:'Faul',offsides:'Ofsayt',dribbles:'Çalım',key_passes:'Kilit Pas',duels_won:'Kazanılan İkili Mücadele',goals_conceded:'Yenen Gol',own_goals:'Kendi Kalesine',expected_goals:'Beklenen Gol (xG)',expected_assists:'Beklenen Asist (xA)',xg:'Beklenen Gol (xG)',xa:'Beklenen Asist (xA)',total_shots:'Toplam Şut',accurate_passes:'İsabetli Pas',total_passes:'Toplam Pas',keypasses:'Kilit Pas',key_passes:'Kilit Pas',crosses:'Orta',clearances:'Uzaklaştırma',blocks:'Blok',aerials_won:'Kazanılan Hava Topu',successful_dribbles:'Başarılı Çalım',touches:'Topla Buluşma',big_chances_created:'Yaratılan Net Fırsat',big_chances_missed:'Kaçırılan Net Fırsat',fouls:'Faul',yellow_cards:'Sarı Kart',red_cards:'Kırmızı Kart',minutes_played:'Oynadığı Dakika',penalties_saved:'Kurtarılan Penaltı'};
const plLabel=k=>PL_TR[String(k).toLowerCase()]||String(k).replace(/_/g,' ').replace(/^./,c=>c.toUpperCase());
const SKIP=/(^|_)(id|slug|url|link|logo|image|photo|avatar|icon|uuid)(_|$)|_id$/i;
const scalar=v=>v!==null&&v!==undefined&&typeof v!=='object'&&String(v)!=='';
function cell(v){if(v&&typeof v==='object'&&!Array.isArray(v))return v.name||v.title||'';return v==null?'':v}
const PROF_KEYS=['Doğum Tarihi','Yaş','Ayak','Piyasa Değeri','Şu Anki Takım','Boy','Uyruk','Mevki','Forma No','Sözleşme Bitişi'];
const PROF_MUST=['Doğum Tarihi','Yaş','Ayak','Piyasa Değeri','Şu Anki Takım'];
const FILT=[['toplam','Toplam'],['lig','Lig'],['intl','Uluslararası'],['milli','Milli Takım']];
const statNum=v=>v==null||v===''||!Number.isFinite(Number(v))?null:Number(v);
function careerClientCategory(row){
  const cats=row&&row.cats||{};
  const has=(b)=>!!b&&['m','g','a','s','y','r'].some(k=>b[k]!=null&&Number(b[k])>0);
  if(has(cats.intl))return 'intl';
  if(has(cats.lig)||has(cats.kupa))return 'lig';
  const code=String(row&&row.pitchCode||'').toLowerCase(),explicit=String(row&&(row.pitchCategory||row['Turnuva Türü'])||'').toLowerCase();
  const name=String(row&&(row['Turnuva']||row.competition||row.league||row['Lig'])||'').toLowerCase();
  if(explicit==='intl'||explicit==='international'||/^(uefa|conmebol|afc|caf|concacaf|ofc|fifa)\./.test(code)||/champions league|europa league|conference league|uefa|libertadores|sudamericana|club world|world cup|nations league|uluslararasi|uluslararası/.test(name))return 'intl';
  return 'lig';
}
function careerRows(se,nat,f){
  const out=[];
  if(f==='milli'){
    (nat||[]).forEach(r=>out.push({s:r['Sezon'],t:r['Takım'],logo:r.logo||r.teamLogo||r.team_logo||r['Takım Logosu']||'',logoCandidates:r.logoCandidates||[],teamId:r.teamId||'',teamCode:r.teamCode||'',afId:r.afId||'',teamAfId:r.teamAfId||'',tsdbId:r.tsdbId||'',m:statNum(r['Maç']),g:statNum(r['Gol']),a:null,s11:null,y:null,r:null,star:false}));
    return out;
  }
  const valid=(se||[]).filter(r=>{
    const season=String(r&&r['Sezon']||'').trim().replace(/[–—−]/g,'-'),team=String(r&&r['Takım']||'').trim();
    return /^(?:19|20)\d{2}(?:\s*[/-]\s*(?:(?:19|20)?\d{2}))?$/.test(season)&&team&&!/^[a-z]{2,5}\.[a-z0-9._-]+$/i.test(team)&&!(/^(?:19|20)\d{2}$/.test(team));
  });
  const n=(bucket,key)=>bucket&&statNum(bucket[key]);
  const addKnown=(a,b)=>a==null?b:b==null?a:a+b;
  const hasMatchesGoals=(b)=>!!b&&((Number(b.m)||0)>0||(Number(b.g)||0)>0);
  const pitchField=(row,cat,key)=>{
    const c=row&&row.pitchApiByCategory&&row.pitchApiByCategory[cat];
    return c&&c.known&&c.known[key]&&c[key]!=null?statNum(c[key]):null;
  };
  const categoryField=(row,cat,key)=>{
    const apiBucket=row&&row.apiFootballCats&&row.apiFootballCats[cat];
    if(apiBucket&&apiBucket[key]!=null&&apiBucket[key]!=='')return statNum(apiBucket[key]);
    return pitchField(row,cat,key);
  };
  const bucketHasCareerFields=(row,cat)=>{
    const apiBucket=row&&row.apiFootballCats&&row.apiFootballCats[cat];
    const pitchBucket=row&&row.pitchApiByCategory&&row.pitchApiByCategory[cat];
    return !!(apiBucket&&['a','s','y','r'].some(k=>apiBucket[k]!=null&&apiBucket[k]!==''))||!!(pitchBucket&&['a','s','y','r'].some(k=>pitchBucket.known&&pitchBucket.known[k]&&pitchBucket[k]!=null));
  };
  valid.forEach(r=>{
    const category=careerClientCategory(r),cats=r.cats||{},leagueBucket=cats.lig||null,cupBucket=cats.kupa||null,intlBucket=cats.intl||null,pitchCats=r.pitchApiByCategory||{};
    let m=null,g=null,a=null,s11=null,y=null,red=null;
    if(f==='toplam'){
      m=statNum(r['Maç']);g=statNum(r['Gol']);a=statNum(r['Asist']);s11=statNum(r['İlk 11']);y=statNum(r['Sarı Kart']);red=statNum(r['Kırmızı Kart']);
    }else if(f==='lig'){
      const hasLegacySplit=hasMatchesGoals(leagueBucket)||hasMatchesGoals(cupBucket);
      const pitch=pitchCats.lig||null;
      const pitchHas=bucketHasCareerFields(r,'lig')||bucketHasCareerFields(r,'kupa')||!!pitch&&['a','s','y','r'].some(k=>pitch.known&&pitch.known[k]&&pitch[k]!=null);
      if(!hasLegacySplit&&!pitchHas&&category!=='lig')return;
      // Eski kaynak lig ve yerel kupa maçlarını ayrı verdiyse birleştir; kategori kırılımı yoksa
      // eski satırın maç/gol toplamını koru. PitchAPI maç/gol rakamları ana kaynağın yerine geçmez.
      if(hasLegacySplit){m=addKnown(n(leagueBucket,'m'),n(cupBucket,'m'));g=addKnown(n(leagueBucket,'g'),n(cupBucket,'g'));}
      else if(category==='lig'){m=statNum(r['Maç']);g=statNum(r['Gol']);}
      a=addKnown(categoryField(r,'lig','a'),categoryField(r,'kupa','a'));
      s11=addKnown(categoryField(r,'lig','s'),categoryField(r,'kupa','s'));
      y=addKnown(categoryField(r,'lig','y'),categoryField(r,'kupa','y'));
      red=addKnown(categoryField(r,'lig','r'),categoryField(r,'kupa','r'));
    }else if(f==='intl'){
      const pitch=pitchCats.intl||null,pitchHas=bucketHasCareerFields(r,'intl')||!!pitch&&['a','s','y','r'].some(k=>pitch.known&&pitch.known[k]&&pitch[k]!=null);
      const legacyHas=hasMatchesGoals(intlBucket);
      if(!legacyHas&&!pitchHas&&category!=='intl')return;
      if(legacyHas){m=n(intlBucket,'m');g=n(intlBucket,'g');}
      else if(category==='intl'){m=statNum(r['Maç']);g=statNum(r['Gol']);}
      a=categoryField(r,'intl','a');s11=categoryField(r,'intl','s');y=categoryField(r,'intl','y');red=categoryField(r,'intl','r');
    }
    if([m,g,a,s11,y,red].every(v=>v==null))return;
    out.push({s:r['Sezon'],t:r['Takım'],order:Number(r.careerTimestamp||r.careerOrder)||0,transferLastTeam:!!r.transferLastTeam,seasonLastTeam:!!r.seasonLastTeam,current:!!r.currentTeam,logo:r.logo||r.teamLogo||r.team_logo||r['Takım Logosu']||'',logoCandidates:r.logoCandidates||[],teamId:r.teamId||'',teamCode:r.teamCode||'',teamAfId:r.teamAfId||'',afId:r.afId||'',tsdbId:r.tsdbId||'',m,g,a,s11,y,r:red,minutes:statNum(r['Toplam Dakika'])});
  });
  const map=new Map();
  for(const row of out){const key=careerSeasonYear(row.s)+'|'+pitchClientTeamKey(row.t);if(!map.has(key)){map.set(key,row);continue}const old=map.get(key);for(const k of ['m','g','a','s11','y','r']){if(old[k]==null&&row[k]!=null)old[k]=row[k];else if(old[k]!=null&&row[k]!=null&&k!=='m'&&k!=='g')old[k]=Math.max(Number(old[k])||0,Number(row[k])||0)}if(!old.logo&&row.logo)old.logo=row.logo;}
  return [...map.values()];
}
const showStat=v=>v==null?'—':v;
const sumRows=rows=>{const sum=k=>{const vals=rows.map(r=>statNum(r[k])).filter(v=>v!=null);return vals.length?vals.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0):null};return {m:sum('m'),g:sum('g'),a:sum('a'),s11:sum('s11'),y:sum('y'),r:sum('r')}};
function playerRecentMatchCard(match){
  const home=match.home||{},away=match.away||{},date=match.date?new Date(match.date+'T12:00:00Z').toLocaleDateString('tr-TR',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'}):'—';
  const sh=match.scoreHome,sa=match.scoreAway,known=sh!=null&&sa!=null,teamScore=match.isHome?sh:sa,oppScore=match.isHome?sa:sh;
  const outcome=!known?'':teamScore>oppScore?'W':teamScore<oppScore?'L':'D';
  const resultClass=outcome==='W'?'win':outcome==='L'?'loss':'draw';
  const rating=match.rating==null?'—':Number(match.rating).toFixed(1);
  return `<article class="player-recent-match"><div class="player-recent-date">${esc(date)}<small>${esc(match.league||'')}</small></div><div class="player-recent-teams"><span>${avatarT(home.logo,home.name)}<b>${esc(home.name||'')}</b></span><strong class="player-recent-score">${known?sh+' : '+sa:'— : —'}</strong><span>${avatarT(away.logo,away.name)}<b>${esc(away.name||'')}</b></span></div><div class="player-recent-stats"><span class="player-result ${resultClass}">${outcome||'—'}</span><span class="player-recent-stat-tile"><small>Puan</small><b>${rating}</b></span><span class="player-recent-stat-tile"><small>Gol</small><b>${match.goals==null?'—':match.goals}</b></span><span class="player-recent-stat-tile"><small>Asist</small><b>${match.assists==null?'—':match.assists}</b></span><span class="player-recent-stat-tile"><small>Dakika</small><b>${match.minutes==null?'—':match.minutes+"'"}</b></span></div></article>`;
}
function myTeamFromBio(bio,it){return (it&& (it.team||it.teamName))||(bio&& (bio['Şu Anki Takım']||bio['Takım']))||''}
function playerHTML(it,j,loadingStats,tab,f){
  const pl=(j&&j.player)||{},coach=it.role==='coach';
  const sub=[it.team,posTR(it.pos)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  const bio=j.bio||{},se=j.seasons||[],nat=j.national||[];
  const photoSource=(cur.pitchPhoto||(pl.photos&&pl.photos.length?pl.photos:pl.logo||it.logo));
  let h=`<div class="player-reference-head">${avatar(photoSource,pl.name||it.name)}<div class="player-reference-meta"><h2>${esc(trPlayerName(pl.name||it.name))}</h2><small>${esc(sub||bio['Mevki']||'Futbolcu profili')}</small><small>${esc([bio['Uyruk'],bio['Yaş']?bio['Yaş']+' yaş':'',bio['Boy']].filter(Boolean).join(' · '))}</small></div><div class="player-reference-rating">${(()=>{const rr=(cur.pitchRecentMatches||[]).map(x=>Number(x.rating)).filter(Number.isFinite);return rr.length?(rr.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/rr.length).toFixed(1):'—'})()}<small>Son maç ort.</small></div></div>`;
  const playerTabs=coach?[['profil','Profil'],['kariyer','Kariyer']]:[['ozet','Özet'],['maclar','Maçlar'],['kariyer','Kariyer'],['piyasa','Piyasa Değeri'],['sakatliklar','Sakatlıklar'],['transferler','Transferler']];
  h+=`<div class="stabs player-reference-tabs">${playerTabs.map(([key,label])=>`<div class="${tab===key?'on':''}" data-pt="${key}">${label}</div>`).join('')}</div>`;
  if(tab==='ozet'&&!coach){
    const recent=Array.isArray(cur.pitchRecentMatches)?cur.pitchRecentMatches:[],rated=recent.filter(x=>x.rating!=null&&Number.isFinite(Number(x.rating)));
    const seasonStats=cur.pitchRecentSummary||{};
    const legacyCurrent=careerRows(se,nat,'toplam').filter(r=>careerSeasonYear(r.s)===Number(cur.pitchRecentSeason&&String(cur.pitchRecentSeason).match(/\d{4}/)?.[0]||currentTeamSeason()));
    const fallbackTotals=sumRows(legacyCurrent);
    const careerRatingRows=(cur.pitchCareerRows||[]).filter(r=>careerSeasonYear(r['Sezon'])===Number(cur.pitchRecentSeason&&String(cur.pitchRecentSeason).match(/\d{4}/)?.[0]||currentTeamSeason()));
    const careerRatingTotal=careerRatingRows.reduce((sum,r)=>sum+(Number(r['Puan Toplam'])||0),0),careerRatingCount=careerRatingRows.reduce((sum,r)=>sum+(Number(r['Puan Maç'])||0),0);
    const fullSeasonRating=careerRatingCount?Number((careerRatingTotal/careerRatingCount).toFixed(1)):null;
    const teamName=cur.pitchRecentTeam||myTeamFromBio(bio,cur.it)||'';
    const currentCareerTeam=(cur.pitchCareerRows||[]).find(r=>pitchClientTeamKey(r['Takım'])===pitchClientTeamKey(teamName))||{};const teamLogo=careerLogoCandidates({...currentCareerTeam,logo:cur.pitchRecentTeamLogo||currentCareerTeam.logo||currentCareerTeam['Takım Logosu']||''});
    const seasonLabel=String(cur.pitchRecentSeason||currentTeamSeason()).replace('/','-');
    const counts=[['Maç',fallbackTotals.m??seasonStats.matches],['Gol',fallbackTotals.g??seasonStats.goals],['Asist',fallbackTotals.a??seasonStats.assists],['Puan',fullSeasonRating!=null?fullSeasonRating.toFixed(1):(seasonStats.averageRating!=null?Number(seasonStats.averageRating).toFixed(1):(rated.length?(rated.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.rating),0)/rated.length).toFixed(1):'—'))]];
    h+='<section class="player-current-season-card"><div class="player-current-season-team">'+avatarT(teamLogo,teamName||'Takım')+'<span><b>'+esc(trTeam(teamName||'Güncel Takım'))+'</b><small>'+esc(cur.pitchRecentLeague||'Güncel Lig')+' · '+esc(seasonLabel)+'</small></span><span class="player-current-season-chevron">›</span></div><div class="player-summary-numbers">'+counts.map(([label,value])=>`<div><small>${label}</small><b>${value==null?'—':value}</b></div>`).join('')+'</div></section>';
    const marketValueText=String(bio['Piyasa Değeri']||'').trim();
    h+='<section class="player-bio-card player-market-value-card"><div class="player-summary-title"><b>Piyasa Değeri</b><small>Transfermarkt</small></div><div class="player-bio-grid"><div><small>Güncel değer</small><b>'+esc(marketValueText||'Henüz veri aktarılmadı')+'</b></div></div></section>';
    h+='<section class="player-form-card"><div class="player-summary-title"><b>Son 13 Maç Performansı</b><small>'+((rated.length)?'Ort. '+(rated.reduce((a,x)=>a+Number(x.rating),0)/rated.length).toFixed(1):'PitchAPI')+'</small></div>';

    h+='</section>';
    const last=recent.slice(0,3);h+='<section class="player-recent-card"><div class="player-summary-title"><b>Son Maçlar</b><button type="button" data-pt="maclar">Tümünü gör ›</button></div>';

      const groups=new Map();for(const match of filteredRecent){const key=(match.leagueCode||'')+'|'+(match.league||'Diğer');if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,[]);groups.get(key).push(match)}
