    }else h+='<div class="empty">'+esc(recent.length?'Seçilen müsabakada maç kaydı bulunamadı.':cur.pitchRecentError||'Bu oyuncu için gösterilebilecek son maç kaydı yok.')+'</div>';
  }else if(tab==='sakatliklar'&&!coach){
    const injuries=Array.isArray(j.injuries)?j.injuries:[];
    h+='<section class="player-history-section"><div class="player-summary-title"><b>Sakatlık Geçmişi</b><small>Transfermarkt kayıtları</small></div>';
    if(injuries.length)h+=injuries.map(x=>`<article class="player-history-row"><span class="player-history-mark injury-mark">+</span><div><b>${esc(x.injury||x.reason||x.type||'Sakatlık kaydı')}</b><small>${esc([x.from_date||x.start||'',x.until_date?'– '+x.until_date:(x.end?'– '+x.end:''),x.season?'Sezon '+x.season:'',x.days!=null?x.days+' gün':'',x.games_missed!=null?x.games_missed+' maç kaçırdı':''].filter(Boolean).join(' '))}</small></div></article>`).join('');
    else h+='<div class="empty">'+(loadingStats?'Sakatlık kayıtları yükleniyor…':j.historyProviderAvailable?'Bu oyuncu için sakatlık kaydı bulunamadı.':'Sakatlık geçmişi sağlayıcısı yapılandırılmamış veya veri sunmamış.')+'</div>';
    h+='</section>';
  }else if(tab==='transferler'&&!coach){
    const transfers=Array.isArray(j.transfers)?j.transfers:[];
    h+='<section class="player-history-section"><div class="player-summary-title"><b>Transfer Geçmişi</b><small>Transfermarkt kayıtları</small></div>';
    if(transfers.length)h+=transfers.map(x=>`<article class="player-history-row transfer-history-row"><span class="player-history-mark transfer-mark">⇄</span><div class="player-transfer-route"><b>${esc(trTeam(x.from||'Bilinmeyen takım'))}</b><span>→</span><b>${esc(trTeam(x.to||'Bilinmeyen takım'))}</b><small>${esc(x.date||'Tarih yok')} · ${esc(x.kind||'Detay belirtilmemiş')}${x.fee?' · '+esc(x.fee):''}</small></div></article>`).join('');
    else h+='<div class="empty">'+(loadingStats?'Transfer geçmişi yükleniyor…':j.historyProviderAvailable?'Bu oyuncu için transfer kaydı bulunamadı.':'Transfer geçmişi sağlayıcısı yapılandırılmamış veya veri sunmamış.')+'</div>';
    h+='</section>';
  }else if(tab==='piyasa'&&!coach){
    const history=Array.isArray(j.marketValueHistory)?j.marketValueHistory:[];
    const formatMarketValue=value=>{
      if(value==null||value==='')return '—';
      if(typeof value==='number'||/^\d+(?:\.\d+)?$/.test(String(value))){
        return new Intl.NumberFormat('tr-TR',{style:'currency',currency:'EUR',maximumFractionDigits:0}).format(Number(value));
      }
      return String(value);
    };
    h+='<section class="player-history-section"><div class="player-summary-title"><b>Piyasa Değeri Geçmişi</b><small>Transfermarkt kayıtları</small></div>';
    if(history.length)h+=history.slice(0,50).map(x=>{
      const rawValue=x.display_value??x.displayValue??x.marketValueFormatted??x.market_value??x.marketValue??x.value_eur??x.value;
      const value=rawValue&&typeof rawValue==='object'?(rawValue.displayValue||rawValue.value||rawValue.amount||rawValue.text):rawValue;
      const clubRaw=x.clubName||x.club_name||x.club||'';
      const club=typeof clubRaw==='object'?(clubRaw.name||clubRaw.displayName||(clubRaw.id!=null?'Kulüp #'+clubRaw.id:'')):clubRaw;
      const clubId=x.clubId??x.clubID??x.club_id;
      const clubLabel=club||(clubId!=null?'Kulüp #'+clubId:'');
      const date=x.date||x.valuation_date||x.valuationDate||'Tarih yok';
      const age=x.age!=null?' · '+x.age+' yaş':'';
      return '<article class="player-history-row"><span class="player-history-mark transfer-mark">€</span><div><b>'+esc(formatMarketValue(value))+'</b><small>'+esc([date,clubLabel].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')+age)+'</small></div></article>';
    }).join('');
    else h+='<div class="empty">'+(loadingStats?'Piyasa değeri geçmişi yükleniyor…':j.historyProviderAvailable?'Bu oyuncu için piyasa değeri geçmişi bulunamadı.':'Piyasa değeri geçmişi sağlayıcısı veri sunmadı.')+'</div>';
    h+='</section>';
  }else if(tab==='profil'){
    const must=coach?['Doğum Tarihi','Yaş','Şu Anki Takım']:PROF_MUST;
    const keys=PROF_KEYS.filter(k=>must.includes(k)||(bio[k]!==''&&bio[k]!=null));
    const val=(k,v)=>v===''||v==null?'—':esc(k==='Uyruk'?String(v).split(/,\s*/).map(trC).join(', '):k==='Mevki'?trVal('position',v):k==='Şu Anki Takım'?trTeam(v):v);
    h+='<div class="kv" style="margin-top:12px">'+keys.map(k=>`<div><small>${esc(k)}</small>${val(k,bio[k])}</div>`).join('')+'</div>';
    
    if(j.about)h+='<div class="sec">Hakkında</div><div class="pnote" style="text-align:left">'+esc(j.about.slice(0,700))+(j.about.length>700?'…':'')+(j.aboutLang==='en'?' <small>(İngilizce kaynak)</small>':'')+'</div>';
    if(loadingStats)h+='<div class="empty"><small>Ek bilgiler yükleniyor…</small></div>';
  }else if(coach&&tab==='kariyer'){
    const cr=j.coach||[];
    if(cr.length){
      const hasCoachStats=cr.some(r=>['O','G','B','M'].some(k=>r[k]!=null&&r[k]!==''));
      h+='<div class="sec" style="padding-top:12px">Teknik Direktörlük Kariyeri</div><div class="tw"><table><tr><th>Dönem</th><th>Takım</th>'+(hasCoachStats?'<th>O</th><th>G</th><th>B</th><th>M</th><th>MBPO</th>':'')+'</tr>'+cr.map(r=>{const o=Number(r['O'])||0,g=Number(r['G'])||0,b=Number(r['B'])||0;const logos=careerLogoCandidates(r);return `<tr><td>${esc(r['Dönem'])}</td><td><div class="career-team" data-career-team="${esc(careerTeamPayload(r))}">${logos.length?`<img class="career-logo" src="${esc(logos[0])}" data-fallbacks="${esc(JSON.stringify(logos.slice(1)))}" alt="" loading="lazy">`:''}<span>${esc(trTeam(careerTeamClean(r['Takım'])))}</span></div></td>`+(hasCoachStats?`<td>${r['O']==null?'—':o}</td><td>${r['G']==null?'—':g}</td><td>${r['B']==null?'—':b}</td><td>${r['M']==null?'—':esc(r['M'])}</td><td>${o?((g*3+b)/o).toFixed(2):'—'}</td>`:'')+'</tr>'}).join('')+'</table></div>';
      const T=cr.reduce((a,r)=>({o:a.o+(+r['O']||0),g:a.g+(+r['G']||0),b:a.b+(+r['B']||0),m:a.m+(+r['M']||0)}),{o:0,g:0,b:0,m:0});
      if(hasCoachStats)h+=`<div class="sec">Toplam</div><div class="tot all"><b>Teknik Direktör Toplamı</b><span>${T.o} maç · ${T.g} G · ${T.b} B · ${T.m} M · MBPO ${T.o?((T.g*3+T.b)/T.o).toFixed(2):'—'}</span></div>`;
    }else if(loadingStats)h+='<div class="empty"><small>Yükleniyor…</small></div>';
    else h+='<div class="empty">Teknik direktörlük istatistikleri şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</div>'; 
  }else if(tab==='kariyer'){
    const pitchRows=Array.isArray(cur.pitchCareerRows)?cur.pitchCareerRows:[];
    const grouped=new Map();
    const add=(row)=>{const season=String(row['Sezon']||row.s||'').replace(/[–—]/g,'-'),team=String(row['Takım']||row.t||'');if(!season||!team)return;const key=careerSeasonYear(season)+'|'+pitchClientTeamKey(team);if(!grouped.has(key))grouped.set(key,{season,team,logo:row['Takım Logosu']||row.logo||row.teamLogo||'',matches:0,goals:0,assists:0,starts:0,minutes:0,minutesKnown:true,ratingTotal:0,ratingMatches:0,sourceRows:0});const g=grouped.get(key);g.sourceRows++;if(!g.logo)g.logo=row['Takım Logosu']||row.logo||row.teamLogo||'';for(const [field,key2] of [['Maç','matches'],['Gol','goals'],['Asist','assists'],['İlk 11','starts']]){const value=row[field]??row[key2[0].toUpperCase()+key2.slice(1)];if(value!=null)g[key2]+=Number(value)||0;}const mins=row['Toplam Dakika'];if(mins==null)g.minutesKnown=false;else g.minutes+=Number(mins)||0;const sum=Number(row['Puan Toplam']),count=Number(row['Puan Maç']);if(Number.isFinite(sum)&&Number.isFinite(count)&&count>0){g.ratingTotal+=sum;g.ratingMatches+=count}else if(row['Ort. Puan']!=null&&Number(row['Maç'])>0){g.ratingTotal+=Number(row['Ort. Puan'])*Number(row['Maç']);g.ratingMatches+=Number(row['Maç']);}};
    if(pitchRows.length)pitchRows.forEach(add);else if(Array.isArray(j.seasons)&&j.seasons.length)j.seasons.forEach(r=>add({'Sezon':r['Sezon']||r.season||r.s,'Takım':r['Takım']||r.team||r.t,'Takım Logosu':r['Takım Logosu']||r.logo||r.teamLogo,'Maç':r['Maç']??r.matches??r.m,'Gol':r['Gol']??r.goals??r.g,'Asist':r['Asist']??r.assists??r.a,'İlk 11':r['İlk 11']??r.starts??r.s11,'Toplam Dakika':r['Toplam Dakika']??r.minutes}));else careerRows(se,nat,'toplam').forEach(r=>add({'Sezon':r.s,'Takım':r.t,'Takım Logosu':r.logo,'Maç':r.m,'Gol':r.g,'Asist':r.a,'İlk 11':r.s11,'Toplam Dakika':r.minutes}));
    const rows=[...grouped.values()].sort((a,b)=>(careerSeasonYear(b.season)||0)-(careerSeasonYear(a.season)||0)||String(a.team).localeCompare(String(b.team),'tr'));
    h+='<div class="player-career-reference-list">';
    if(cur.pitchCareerLoading)h+='<div class="player-career-loading">Transfermarkt kariyer verileri yükleniyor…</div>';
    if(rows.length)h+=rows.map(row=>{const avg=row.ratingMatches?(row.ratingTotal/row.ratingMatches).toFixed(2):'—';return `<article class="player-career-season-card"><div class="player-career-season-head"><span class="player-career-season">${esc(row.season)}</span><span class="player-career-team">${avatarT(careerLogoCandidates(row),row.team)}<b>${esc(trTeam(row.team))}</b></span><span class="player-career-average"><b>${row.minutesKnown?row.minutes.toLocaleString('tr-TR'):'—'}</b><small>Toplam dakika</small></span></div><div class="player-career-season-stats"><div><small>Maç</small><b>${row.matches}</b></div><div><small>Gol</small><b>${row.goals}</b></div><div><small>Asist</small><b>${row.assists}</b></div><div><small>İlk 11</small><b>${row.starts}</b></div></div></article>`}).join('');
    else if(loadingStats||cur.pitchCareerLoading)h+='<div class="empty">Kariyer sezonları yükleniyor…</div>';
    else h+='<div class="empty">Bu oyuncu için Transfermarkt sezon kayıtları henüz bulunamadı.</div>';
    if(cur.pitchCareerError&&cur.pitchCareerLoaded)h+='<div class="pnote">'+esc(cur.pitchCareerError)+'</div>';
    h+='</div>';
  }
  return h+rawBlock(j);
}
function playerHTMLgeneric(it,j){
  const root=(j&&(j.player||j.data))||j||{};
  const photo=root.photo||root.image||root.avatar||root.logo||it.logo;
  const sub=[typeof root.team==='object'&&root.team?root.team.name:root.team,trVal('position',root.position)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||[it.team,posTR(it.pos)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
  let h=`<div class="pinfo">${photo?`<img src="${esc(photo)}" alt="">`:''}<div><h2>${esc(trPlayerName(root.name||it.name))}</h2><small>${esc(sub)}</small></div></div>`;
  const kv=Object.keys(root).filter(k=>!SKIP.test(k)&&scalar(root[k])&&String(root[k]).length<=60&&!/^(name|short_name|full_name|sport)$/i.test(k));
  if(kv.length)h+='<div class="kv">'+kv.map(k=>`<div><small>${esc(plLabel(k))}</small>${esc(trVal(k,root[k]))}</div>`).join('')+'</div>';
  const tables=[],objs=[];
  (function walk(o,d,key){
    if(!o||typeof o!=='object'||d>3)return;
    if(Array.isArray(o)){
      if(o.length&&o.every(x=>x&&typeof x==='object'&&!Array.isArray(x))){tables.push([key,o]);return}
      return;
    }
    if(d>0){const ks=Object.keys(o).filter(k=>!SKIP.test(k)&&scalar(o[k]));if(ks.length>=3&&d<=2&&key)objs.push([key,o,ks])}
    Object.keys(o).forEach(k=>walk(o[k],d+1,k));
  })(root,0,'');
  objs.forEach(([k,o,ks])=>{h+=`<div class="sec">${esc(plLabel(k))}</div><div class="kv">`+ks.map(x=>`<div><small>${esc(plLabel(x))}</small>${esc(trVal(x,o[x]))}</div>`).join('')+'</div>'});
  tables.forEach(([k,rows])=>{
    const cols=[];rows.slice(0,5).forEach(r=>Object.keys(r).forEach(c=>{if(!SKIP.test(c)&&!cols.includes(c)&&(scalar(r[c])||(r[c]&&typeof r[c]==='object'&&!Array.isArray(r[c])&&(r[c].name||r[c].title))))cols.push(c)}));
    if(cols.length<2)return;
    h+=`<div class="sec">${esc(plLabel(k)||'Tablo')}</div><div class="tw"><table><tr>${cols.map(c=>`<th>${esc(plLabel(c))}</th>`).join('')}</tr>`+rows.map(r=>`<tr>${cols.map(c=>`<td>${esc(trVal(c,cell(r[c])))}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')+'</table></div>';
  });
  if(!kv.length&&!tables.length&&!objs.length)h+='<div class="empty">Şu anda gösterilecek istatistik yok.</div>';
  return h+rawBlock(j);
}
function renderPlayer(){
  if(!cur||cur.kind!=='player')return;
  $('#shTitle').textContent=trPlayerName(cur.it.name);
  const i=cur.info,t=cur.stats;
  const photos=[cur.pitchPhoto,(i&&i.pitchPhoto),...((i&&i.profilePhotos)||[]),...((i&&i.player&&i.player.photos)||[]),...((t&&t.player&&t.player.photos)||[]),...(Array.isArray(cur.it.photos)?cur.it.photos:[]),cur.it.logo,cur.it.photo,cur.it.image,(i&&i.player&&i.player.logo),(t&&t.player&&t.player.logo)].filter((x,i,a)=>typeof x==='string'&&/^https:\/\//i.test(x)&&a.indexOf(x)===i);
  const j={player:{name:(i&&i.player&&i.player.name)||cur.it.name,logo:photos[0]||'',photos},about:(i&&i.about)||'',aboutLang:i&&i.aboutLang,bio:Object.assign({},i&&i.bio,t&&t.bio),seasons:(t&&t.seasons)||[],national:(t&&t.national)||[],coach:(t&&t.coach)||[],transfers:(t&&t.transfers)||(i&&i.transfers)||[],injuries:(t&&t.injuries)||(i&&i.injuries)||[],marketValueHistory:(t&&Array.isArray(t.marketValueHistory)&&t.marketValueHistory.length?t.marketValueHistory:(i&&i.marketValueHistory)||[]),historyProviderAvailable:!!(t&&t.sources&&t.sources.transfermarkt&&!/hata|bulunamadı/i.test(String(t.sources.transfermarkt))),tmRefreshRequired:!!(t&&t.tmRefreshRequired),tmRefreshQueued:!!(t&&t.tmRefreshQueued),tmRefreshConfigured:t&&t.tmRefreshConfigured,tmRefreshReason:(t&&t.tmRefreshReason)||'',tmRefreshState:(t&&t.tmRefreshState)||'',tmRefreshRetryAt:(t&&t.tmRefreshRetryAt)||'',totals:(t&&t.totals)||null,careerOrder:(t&&t.careerOrder)||{},sources:Object.assign({},i&&i.sources,t&&t.sources)};
  const refreshState=String((t&&t.tmRefreshState)||(i&&i.tmRefreshState)||'');
  const refreshPending=!!((t&&t.tmRefreshRequired)||(i&&i.tmRefreshRequired))&&(
    !!((t&&t.tmRefreshQueued)||(i&&i.tmRefreshQueued))||['queued','running','already-queued','concurrent-queue','retry-wait'].includes(refreshState)
  );
  const loading=(!cur.statsLoaded&&!cur.serr)||refreshPending||['checking','queued','waiting'].includes(cur.tmRefreshStatus);
  let h=playerHTML(cur.it,j,loading,cur.tab,cur.f);
  if(cur.serr&&!t)h+='<div class="empty"><small>İstatistikler şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</small></div>';
  if(cur.err&&!i)h+='<div class="empty"><small>Bu bölüm şu anda görüntülenemiyor.</small></div>';
  $('#shBody').innerHTML=h;
}

document.addEventListener('change',e=>{
  const playerCompetition=e.target.closest('[data-player-match-competition]');
  if(playerCompetition&&cur&&cur.kind==='player'){cur.pitchRecentCompetition=playerCompetition.value||'all';renderPlayer();return}
  const sel=e.target.closest('#teamSeason');
  if(sel&&cur&&cur.kind==='team'){changeTeamSeason(Number(sel.value));return}
  const teamComp=e.target.closest('#teamStatsCompetition');
  if(teamComp&&cur&&cur.kind==='team'){cur.teamStatsCode=teamComp.value;cur.pitchTeamStats=null;cur.pitchTeamStatsError='';cur.pitchTeamStatsLoading=false;cur.pitchTeamStatsReq=(cur.pitchTeamStatsReq||0)+1;const picked=teamCompetitionOptions(cur.data,true).find(x=>x.code===cur.teamStatsCode);cur.pitchTeamStatsCompetition=picked&&picked.name||'';loadPitchTeamStats(cur);return}
  const leagueSel=e.target.closest('#leagueSeason');
  if(leagueSel&&cur&&cur.kind==='league'){changeLeagueSeason(Number(leagueSel.value));}
});

  const theme=e.target.closest('#themeSelect');if(theme){applyTheme(theme.value);return}
  const lang=e.target.closest('#languageSelect');if(lang){applyLanguage(lang.value);return}
  const sound=e.target.closest('#soundSelect');if(sound){localStorage.setItem('canliskor.soundEffects',sound.value==='off'?'off':'on');return}
});
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{const target=e.target.closest('button,.d,.t,.chip,.stabs div,.pr,.lgh');if(target&&!target.disabled)playButtonClickSound()},true);
// Maç kartına uzun basıldığında maç önü/canlı İddaa oran seçicisini aç.
function matchForLongPress(id,row){
  const key=String(id||'');if(!key)return null;
  const seen=new Set();
  const scan=(node,depth)=>{
    if(!node||depth>8||typeof node!=='object')return null;
    if(seen.has(node))return null;seen.add(node);
    if(!Array.isArray(node)&&node.fixture&&String(node.fixture.id!=null?node.fixture.id:node.id||'')===key&&node.teams&&node.teams.home&&node.teams.away)return node;
    if(Array.isArray(node)){for(const child of node){const hit=scan(child,depth+1);if(hit)return hit;}return null;}
    // Match objects can be nested in competition groups, team pages, cached response wrappers or search results.
    for(const value of Object.values(node)){if(value&&typeof value==='object'){const hit=scan(value,depth+1);if(hit)return hit;}}
    return null;
  };
  const found=scan(state&&state.data,0)||scan(cur&&cur.data,0)||scan(cur&&cur.pitchFixtures,0)||scan(cur&&cur.base,0);
  if(found)return found;
  // Last-resort visible-row snapshot: a match row carries enough metadata to open the picker
  // even when its parent list was refreshed/replaced before the long-press timer fired.
  const d=row&&row.dataset||{};
  if(d.homeName&&d.awayName&&d.date){
    const numeric=v=>v==null||v===''?null:(Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):null);
    return {fixture:{id:key,date:d.date,status:{short:d.status||'NS',elapsed:numeric(d.elapsed)}},
      teams:{home:{id:d.homeId||'',name:d.homeName,raw:d.homeRaw||d.homeName},away:{id:d.awayId||'',name:d.awayName,raw:d.awayRaw||d.awayName}},
      goals:{home:numeric(d.homeScore),away:numeric(d.awayScore)},
      league:{name:d.leagueName||'',id:d.leagueId||'',code:d.leagueCode||'',country:d.leagueCountry||'',season:d.leagueSeason||''},events:[],lineups:[]};
  }
  return null;
}
function showLongPressFallback(message){
  const shell=ensureOddsPickerVisible();if(!shell)return;
  const {root,body}=shell;
  body.innerHTML='<div class="odds-picker-head"><b>İddaa marketleri</b><button class="ibtn" data-iddaa-close aria-label="Kapat">×</button></div><div class="iddaa-picker-scroll"><div class="iddaa-picker-empty">'+esc(message||'Maç bilgisi alınamadı.')+'</div></div>';
  ensureOddsPickerVisible();root.hidden=false;
}
// Uzun basma: masaüstünde Pointer Events, Android/iOS'ta gerçek Touch Events.
// Touch cihazlarında pointercancel uzun basmayı kesebildiği için touchstart ayrı izlenir.
function fireMatchLongPress(row,id){
  if(!id)return;
  clearTimeout(holdTimer);holdTimer=null;holdPointerId=null;holdTouchId=null;holdRowEl=null;holdStartAt=0;
  suppressMatchClickId=String(id);suppressMatchClickUntil=Date.now()+1800;
  // Overlay, parmak kalkınca oluşan sentetik click tarafından hemen kapatılmasın.
  suppressLongPressClickPending=true;suppressLongPressClickUntil=Date.now()+1100;
  const match=matchForLongPress(id,row);
  if(!match){showLongPressFallback('Maç bilgisi bulunamadı. Maç listesini yenileyip tekrar dene.');return;}
  try{
    const pending=openOddsPicker(match);
    if(pending&&typeof pending.catch==='function')pending.catch(error=>showLongPressFallback('İddaa ekranı açılamadı: '+String(error&&error.message||error).slice(0,140)));
  }catch(error){showLongPressFallback('İddaa ekranı açılamadı: '+String(error&&error.message||error).slice(0,140));}
}
const cancelMatchHold=()=>{
  clearTimeout(holdTimer);holdTimer=null;holdPointerId=null;holdTouchId=null;holdStartAt=0;holdRowEl=null;holdMatchId='';
};
// Mouse/kalem: Pointer Events.
document.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
  if(e.pointerType==='touch')return;
  const target=e.target,row=target&&target.closest?target.closest('.m'):null;
  if(!row||(target.closest&&target.closest('.fav,.iddaa-open-btn')))return;
  cancelMatchHold();holdMatchId=String(row.dataset.m||'');holdStartX=Number(e.clientX)||0;holdStartY=Number(e.clientY)||0;holdStartAt=Date.now();holdPointerId=e.pointerId;holdRowEl=row;
  const id=holdMatchId;holdTimer=setTimeout(()=>fireMatchLongPress(row,id),560);
},{passive:true,capture:true});
document.addEventListener('pointerup',e=>{if(e.pointerType!=='touch')cancelMatchHold();},{passive:true,capture:true});
document.addEventListener('pointercancel',e=>{if(e.pointerType!=='touch')cancelMatchHold();},{passive:true,capture:true});
document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
  if(e.pointerType==='touch'||!holdTimer||holdPointerId!==e.pointerId)return;
  if(Math.abs((Number(e.clientX)||0)-holdStartX)+Math.abs((Number(e.clientY)||0)-holdStartY)>16)cancelMatchHold();
},{passive:true,capture:true});
// Telefon/tablet: touchstart/touchend. Bu, önceki çalışan Android akışıdır.
document.addEventListener('touchstart',e=>{
  const touch=e.changedTouches&&e.changedTouches[0];if(!touch)return;
  const target=e.target,row=target&&target.closest?target.closest('.m'):null;
  if(!row||(target.closest&&target.closest('.fav,.iddaa-open-btn')))return;
  cancelMatchHold();holdTouchId=touch.identifier;holdMatchId=String(row.dataset.m||'');
  holdStartX=touch.clientX;holdStartY=touch.clientY;holdStartAt=Date.now();holdRowEl=row;
  const id=holdMatchId,identifier=touch.identifier;
  holdTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(holdTouchId===identifier)fireMatchLongPress(row,id);},560);
},{passive:true,capture:true});
document.addEventListener('touchmove',e=>{
  if(!holdTimer||holdTouchId==null)return;
  const touch=Array.from(e.touches||[]).find(t=>t.identifier===holdTouchId);
  if(!touch)return;
  if(Math.abs(touch.clientX-holdStartX)+Math.abs(touch.clientY-holdStartY)>16)cancelMatchHold();
},{passive:true,capture:true});
const endTouchHold=e=>{
  if(holdTouchId==null)return;
  const ended=Array.from(e.changedTouches||[]).some(t=>t.identifier===holdTouchId);
  if(ended)cancelMatchHold();
};
document.addEventListener('touchend',endTouchHold,{passive:true,capture:true});
document.addEventListener('touchcancel',endTouchHold,{passive:true,capture:true});
document.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{
  const target=e.target,row=target&&target.closest?target.closest('.m'):null;
  if(row){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();}
},true);
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{

  const picker=target&&target.closest('#iddaaPicker');
  const now=Date.now();
  // The overlay is created while the pointer is still down. On release, the browser may
  // retarget the synthetic click to the new backdrop; swallowing that one click prevents
  // it from immediately closing the just-opened panel.
  if(suppressLongPressClickPending&&now<=suppressLongPressClickUntil&&
      (picker||(row&&suppressMatchClickId===row.dataset.m&&now<=suppressMatchClickUntil))){
    suppressLongPressClickPending=false;e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
    if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();return;
  }
  if(now>suppressLongPressClickUntil)suppressLongPressClickPending=false;
  if(row&&suppressMatchClickId===row.dataset.m&&now<=suppressMatchClickUntil){
    e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();if(e.stopImmediatePropagation)e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    suppressMatchClickId='';suppressMatchClickUntil=0;return;
  }

window.addEventListener('focus',()=>{if(isToday()&&!sheetOpen){load(true);scheduleHomeRefresh(1200)}});
window.addEventListener('online',()=>{if(isToday()&&!sheetOpen){load(true);scheduleHomeRefresh(1200)}});
scheduleHomeRefresh(5000);

languageObserver.observe(document.body,{subtree:true,childList:true,characterData:true});
translateTree();
